Experimental results on standard object motion benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [149] [SAM2Matting: Generalized Image and Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27339)
*Ruiqi Shen,Guangquan Jie,Chang Liu,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM2Matting提出了一种追踪器到抠图的框架，将VOS追踪器（如SAM2、SAM3）增强为高质量视频抠图系统，通过区域提议桥接和专用抠图头解耦任务，仅用图像训练即达到视频抠图SOTA性能，支持多种提示类型，具备强时间一致性和鲁棒泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频抠图方法面临高层追踪（帧级理解）与低层抠图（细粒度细节）之间的固有鸿沟，依赖昂贵且范围狭窄的视频抠图数据集，导致域外泛化受限且追踪鲁棒性受损。

Method: 提出SAM2Matting框架，将任务解耦为：（1）利用基础追踪器（SAM2/SAM3）处理时间一致性；（2）通过区域提议桥接（region-proposal bridge）连接追踪器与抠图头；（3）专用抠图头（matting heads）解析细粒度细节。整个框架仅用图像训练。

Result: 仅用图像训练即在视频抠图任务上达到新的SOTA性能；支持多种提示类型；保持强时间一致性；在人类中心场景和野外场景均展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAM2Matting成功弥合了追踪与抠图之间的鸿沟，证明了将基础追踪器增强为视频抠图系统的有效性，为视频抠图提供了无需专门视频数据训练的新范式。

Abstract: Despite impressive advances in image matting, video matting remains challenging due to the inherent gap between high-level tracking, which requires frame-wise understanding, and low-level matting, which focuses on extremely fine-grained details. Existing methods attempt this with expensive and narrowly-scoped video matting datasets, which may limit out-of-domain generalization and compromise tracking robustness. We rethink the paradigm with SAM2Matting, a tracker-to-matting framework that advances VOS trackers to high-fidelity video matting. Specifically, it decouples the task by enhancing a foundational tracker (e.g., SAM2, SAM3) with a region-proposal bridge and dedicated matting heads, enabling the uncompromised tracker to handle temporal consistency while the matting components resolve fine-grained details. Notably, despite being trained only on images, SAM2Matting establishes new state-of-the-art performance on video matting, supports diverse prompt types, maintains strong temporal consistency, and demonstrates robust generalization across both human-centric and in-the-wild scenarios.

</details>


### [150] [RayPE: Ray-Space Positional Encoding for 3D-Aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27345)
*Minghao Yin,Jiahao Lu,Wenbo Hu,Wang Zhao,Shan Ying,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RayPE，通过将6D Plücker坐标作为位置编码注入注意力机制，使视频扩散Transformer能够感知3D几何结构，在不增加额外参数的情况下提升相机可控性和跨帧3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散Transformer的RoPE仅编码相机采样网格的(u,v,t)坐标，无法捕获场景的3D几何结构，导致相机可控性和跨帧3D一致性不足。

Method: 将每条相机射线的6D Plücker坐标以加法形式注入自注意力中的query和key，通过query/key翻转排列使对称恒等配置恰好对应Plücker互逆积；解耦射线方向与矩幅值，使用log-幅值的门控函数和RMSNorm对齐内容分支，零初始化以兼容预训练权重。

Result: 在四个数据集的混合训练中，RayPE提升了相机可控性、跨帧3D一致性和整体视频质量，参数量增加不到0.1%。

Conclusion: 通过Plücker坐标的几何先验注入，可高效增强视频DiT的3D感知能力，且分解出的内容项、几何项及两者的交叉项均被实验证明不可或缺。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion transformers position their tokens through RoPE on the (u,v,t) axes -- a description of the camera's sampling grid that says nothing about the 3D structure of the scene. We observe that the geometric relation between two camera rays is captured by the Plucker reciprocal product, which is bilinear in the two rays -- the same algebraic form as the dot product in Transformer attention. Building on this analogy, we propose RayPE, a positional-encoding extension that injects per-token 6D Plucker coordinates additively into the queries and keys of self-attention, with a query/key flip arrangement under which the symmetric identity configuration coincides exactly with the reciprocal product. The injection is additive, the resulting attention score decomposes into a content term, a geometry term, and two content and geometry cross-terms -- all of which our experiments find individually necessary. To make the encoding stable across video data with heterogeneous camera-translation scales (SfM, deep SLAM, metric), we further decouple ray direction from moment magnitude, gate the encoding by a learned function of the log-magnitude, and apply RMSNorm to align it with the QKNorm-normalized content branch. The full module adds less than 0.1% parameters to a pretrained video DiT, is zero-initialized to start from the pretrained weights, and improves camera controllability, cross-frame 3D consistency, and overall video quality on a four-dataset training mixture.

</details>


### [151] [FracEvent: Event-Camera Simulation via Fractional-Relaxation Pixel Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26636)
*Langyi Chen,Chuanzhi Xu,Haoxian Zhou,Pengfei Ye,Ziyu Luo,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Xiaoming Chen,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FracEvent是一种基于分数阶松弛电压动力学建模像素级生命周期的事件相机模拟器，通过保留记忆模态的连续电压轨迹生成更精确的事件时序，在事件流对比和下游任务（图像重建、光流估计）中优于现有模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实事件数据难以大规模采集（需专用传感器、精确同步和任务标注），现有模拟器简化了像素生命周期产生的时序结构，导致事件时序失真并削弱下游迁移性能。

Method: FracEvent使用分数阶松弛电压动力学驱动紧凑的松弛模态堆栈，将模态响应组合为电压状态；通过连续电压轨迹上的阈值交叉定位生成ON/OFF事件，更新参考值的同时保留底层记忆模态，将残余电压响应与后续事件时序关联。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FracEvent生成的事件具有更优的时序结构，并在图像重建和光流估计的下游迁移任务中取得优于竞争模拟器的结果。

Conclusion: FracEvent通过更精细地建模像素级电压动力学，有效提升了事件模拟的时序精度和下游任务迁移性能，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Event cameras asynchronously report brightness changes with microsecond-level temporal resolution, but real event data remain difficult to collect at scale because specialized sensors, careful synchronization, and task-specific annotations are required. Event-camera simulation is therefore important to event-based vision tasks. Most practical simulators build on contrast-threshold event generation, some with additional filtering, stochastic noise, or hand-tuned sensor parameters. While effective, such formulations often simplify the temporal structure produced by the lifecycle of each pixel, which can distort event timing and weaken downstream transfer. We introduce FracEvent, an event simulator that models this pixel-level lifecycle with fractional-relaxation voltage dynamics. Given a log-intensity trajectory, FracEvent drives a compact stack of relaxation modes, combines their responses into a voltage state, emits ON/OFF events by localizing threshold crossings on the continuous voltage trajectory, and updates the reference while retaining the underlying memory modes. This retained state links residual voltage response to later event timing. We evaluate FracEvent through event-stream comparison and downstream transfer on image reconstruction and optical flow estimation. Across multiple datasets, FracEvent improves the temporal structure of generated events and achieves stronger downstream-transfer results than competing simulator baselines, showing its practical value for event-camera simulation.

</details>


### [152] [PhysiFormer: Learning to Simulate Mechanics in World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27364)
*Yiming Chen,Yushi Lan,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysiFormer是一个扩散Transformer模型，直接在3D世界坐标中对物体顶点轨迹进行去噪扩散建模，无需刚性或因果性等归纳偏置，就能生成物理合理的刚体和弹性运动，并在轨迹精度、刚性保持和动量一致性上显著优于自回归基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在视图相关的像素空间操作，而神经物理方法常依赖特殊潜空间或显式施加刚性/因果约束。作者希望无需这些归纳偏置，直接在3D世界坐标中通过扩散过程建模物体运动，并捕捉物理动力学中的不确定性。

Method: 将物体表示为3D网格（世界坐标系中的顶点位置+速度+材料类型），直接对顶点轨迹进行去噪扩散过程；使用在时间、空间和物体维度上分解的注意力机制，实现高效的多物体推理；训练数据为超过10万条模拟轨迹。

Result: 在轨迹精度、刚性保持和动量一致性上大幅超越自回归基线；能够生成刚体和弹性力学行为；泛化到混合材料、未见真实世界几何形状和更多物体数量的场景。

Conclusion: 坐标空间扩散是朝向视角不变、几何感知的世界建模的有前景方向，可应用于机器人、图形学和物理设计。

Abstract: We present PhysiFormer, a diffusion transformer for physically-plausible 3D object motion. Unlike video world models that operate in view-dependent pixel space, PhysiFormer represents objects as 3D meshes expressed in world coordinates. Given the initial vertex positions and velocities, as well as object material type, rigid or elastic, the model samples future vertex trajectories. While related neural physics approaches build on ad-hoc latent spaces or explicitly enforce rigidity and causality, PhysiFormer shows that excellent results can be obtained without any such inductive biases, by casting vertex trajectory prediction as a single denoising diffusion process directly in world coordinates. The probabilistic formulation captures uncertainty in the learned dynamics, enabling diverse plausible futures from initial conditions, making this framework potentially useful for applications with unobserved uncertainty. The model features attention factorised over time, space, and objects for efficiency, enabling permutation-invariant multi-object reasoning without needing explicit object encoding. Trained on over 100k simulated trajectories, PhysiFormer generates rigid and elastic mechanics, and generalises to mixed-material settings, unseen real-world geometries, and larger object counts. It substantially outperforms autoregressive baselines in trajectory accuracy, rigidity preservation, and momentum-based physical consistency. Our results position coordinate-space diffusion as a promising step toward view-invariant, geometry-aware world modelling for robotics, graphics, and physical design. Visualisations, code, and models are available at https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer.

</details>


### [153] [Don't Settle at the Mode! Mitigating Diversity Collapse in Pretrained Flow Models via Feature Self-Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27371)
*Pradhaan S Bhat,Rishubh Parihar,Abhijnya Bhat,R. Venkatesh Babu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、无需额外奖励模型的自我引导机制，通过特征分散和流形正则化来解决流模型在条件生成中的多样性崩溃问题，仅增加微小推理成本即可显著提升多样性并保持保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流模型在相同条件下生成多个样本时存在多样性崩溃问题，已有的潜在引导方法效果有限，样本选择方法依赖外部奖励模型且推理开销大。

Method: 1) 特征自我引导：在批量生成过程中分散流模型的内部特征；2) 流形正则化：将分散的特征投影回数据流形，确保多样性与条件对齐的平衡；该方法作为即插即用模块集成到预训练流模型中。

Result: 在多种条件流模型（多步和少步文生图、深度图生图、参考图生成）上显著提升多样性，同时保持保真度，仅增加微小推理成本。

Conclusion: 提出的自我引导机制是高效、无需训练且不依赖外部奖励模型的通用解决方案，可有效缓解流模型的多样性崩溃问题。

Abstract: State-of-the-art flow models generate stunning images from text or image prompts. However, they suffer from diversity collapse when generating multiple samples under the same conditioning. Existing methods address this issue via either latent guidance, which has limited effectiveness, or sample selection, which relies on external reward models that incur significant inference-time overhead. In this work, we introduce an efficient, training-free self-guidance mechanism to mitigate diversity collapse without requiring additional reward models. Specifically, we disperse the internal features of the flow model during batch generation with feature self-guidance. Further, to keep the features close to the manifold, we introduce a manifold regularization step that projects these dispersed features back onto the data manifold, ensuring diverse generation without sacrificing alignment with the input conditions. Our method integrates seamlessly as a plug-and-play module into pretrained flow models, adding only a marginal inference cost. Experiments demonstrate significant improvements in diversity while preserving fidelity across several conditional flow models, including multi-step and few-step text-to-image, depth-to-image, and reference image generation.

</details>


### [154] [DnA: Denoising Attention for Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27372)
*Ron Campos,Subhajit Maity,Xin Li,Srijan Das,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出去噪注意力(DnA)机制，通过正负查询将图像特征投影到两个不同子空间，增大主角度以促进子空间分离和区分性，在ImageNet-1K上提升0.8%，在视频理解任务上提升1.8%和0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准softmax注意力机制在多頭注意力中会产生噪声注意力模式，稀释相关特征并降低性能，因此需要更干净的注意力机制来提升视觉感知任务的判别能力。

Method: DnA包含两个核心设计：(1) 正查询识别属于正确类别的图像特征，负查询识别紧密相关但不相关的图像特征；(2) 将这两种交互投影到两个不同子空间，通过增大子空间之间的主角度来促进子空间分离，从而提升判别能力。

Result: 基于ViT-B骨干网络，在ImageNet-1K上比基线绝对提升0.8%；视频理解任务中，视频Transformer提升1.8%，视频大语言模型提升0.5%。

Conclusion: DnA通过去噪注意力机制有效提升了注意力模式的干净程度，在多个视觉理解任务上均取得一致性改进，且设计选择通过大量实证分析得到验证。

Abstract: The softmax activation in multihead attention (MHA) is the de facto standard for attention-based models in visual perception tasks. However, standard softmax can produce noisy attention patterns that dilute relevant features and degrade its performance. In this paper, we propose Denoising Attention or DnA, in which, first, a positive query identifies which image features belong to the correct class, and a negative query identifies closely associated but irrelevant image features. DnA then projects these interactions into two distinct subspaces with larger principal angles, promoting subspace separation and improved discriminability. Using a ViT-B backbone, our proposed DnA achieves an absolute gain of 0.8% on ImageNet-1K compared to the baseline. We further show improvements across multiple visual understanding tasks, including video understanding with video transformers (1.8%) and video LLMs (0.5%). Our extensive empirical analyses justify the design choices involving two interacting subspaces and the denoising effect of DnA.

</details>


### [155] [Paying More Attention to Visual Tokens in Self-Evolving Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27373)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Ritesh Thawkar,Omkar Thawakar,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Hisham Cholakkal,Salman Khan,Fahad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有自进化多模态大模型存在"视觉欠条件化"问题——解码器依赖语言先验而非图像内容。本文提出VISE框架，通过几何不变性奖励和语义不变性奖励直接正则化视觉条件策略，无需外部监督即可提升视觉-语言理解能力，在18个基准测试上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化LMMs中的多角色自博弈和自一致性奖励方案仅优化答案一致性，未能确保解码器关注视觉内容，导致解码器依赖统计语言先验而非图像进行生成，在图像描述和视觉问答等任务上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VISE（Visual Invariance Self-Evolution），一种纯无监督自进化框架。通过两个互补的基于不变性的奖励来正则化模型的视觉条件策略：(1) 几何不变性奖励——在已知变换下强制空间一致性；(2) 语义不变性奖励——当预测区域被扰动时，要求模型识别证据缺失，惩罚无证据支持的生成。VISE在单一模型内运行，无需专家角色、外部奖励模型或标注，仅使用原始无标签图像训练。

Result: 基于Qwen3-VL-2B模型，VISE在COCO数据集上CIDEr提升+16.85，TextCaps上提升+19.66，物体幻觉减少5.0 Chair-I点数，且在4个模型家族和多种规模上具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: VISE通过视觉不变性约束有效解决了自进化LMMs的视觉欠条件化问题，在不依赖外部监督的情况下显著提升了视觉-语言理解能力，并且具有良好的跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Recently, self-evolving large multimodal models (LMMs) have received attention for improving visual reasoning in a purely unsupervised setting. However, multi-role self-play and self-consistency reward schemes in existing self-evolving LMMs optimize answer agreement without ensuring the decoder attends to visual content, relying instead on statistical language priors to produce self consistent outputs. This leads to a persistent failure mode we term visual under-conditioning, where the decoder relies on language priors rather than the image during generation, manifesting as insufficient attention to visual tokens. As a result, current self-evolving LMMs struggle on vision--language understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. To address this, we propose VISE (Visual Invariance Self-Evolution), a purely unsupervised self-evolving framework that directly regularizes the model's visual conditioning policy through two complementary invariance-based rewards: a geometric invariance reward that enforces spatial consistency under known transformations, and a semantic invariance reward that penalizes evidence-agnostic generation by requiring the model to recognize the absence of evidence when predicted regions are perturbed. VISE operates within a single model without specialist roles, external reward models, or annotations, and is trained on raw unlabeled images. Experiments on 18 benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of our approach. Using Qwen3-VL-2B as the base model, VISE achieves gains of $+16.85$ CIDEr on COCO and $+19.66$ CIDEr on TextCaps, reduces object hallucination by $5.0$ Chair-I points, and generalizes across four model families and scales. Our code and models are available at https://mbzuai-oryx.github.io/VISE

</details>


### [156] [LearniBridge: Learnable Calibration of Feature Caching for Diffusion Models Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26778)
*Xuyue Huang,Zhe Chen,Wang Shen,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LearniBridge是一种基于可学习低秩校正的特征缓存机制，通过LoRA更新桥接多个时间步，仅需3-5个训练样本即可显著加速扩散Transformer（DiTs）的图像和视频生成，在FLUX、HunyuanVideo和WAN2.1上分别实现最高5.87倍、5.75倍和4.10倍加速，并在WAN2.1上以4.10倍加速比提升VBench指标1.28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征缓存方法在高加速比下存在严重的误差累积问题，需要研究特征校正的本质，提出更有效的校正机制。

Method: 揭示最优校准更新具有跨不同提示共享的低秩子空间结构；基于此提出LearniBridge，通过轻量级LoRA更新实现多个时间步之间的可学习校准机制。

Result: 在FLUX、HunyuanVideo和WAN2.1上分别达到5.87倍、5.75倍和4.10倍加速；在WAN2.1上以4.10倍加速比超越先前最优方法1.28% VBench。

Conclusion: LearniBridge通过仅需少量训练样本的可学习低秩校正机制，有效解决了高加速比下的误差累积问题，在图像和视频生成任务上均取得了显著的加速效果和性能提升。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have driven substantial progress in image and video generation but suffer from prohibitive computational costs. Feature caching accelerates inference by reusing intermediate representations. Existing methods rely on historical features for implementation simplicity, yet suffer from severe error accumulation at high acceleration ratios. To address this limitation, we investigate the nature of the requisite feature correction. We demonstrate that the optimal calibration update is characterized by a shared low-rank subspace across diverse prompts. Guided by this structural insight, we propose LearniBridge, a learnable calibration mechanism for feature caching that bridges multiple timesteps through lightweight LoRA updates. This mechanism enables effective calibration requiring only 3-5 training samples. Extensive experiments on image and video generation show that LearniBridge achieves up to $5.87\times$, $5.75\times$, and $4.10\times$ acceleration on FLUX, HunyuanVideo, and WAN2.1, respectively. On WAN2.1, it improves VBench by 1.28% over the previous SOTA at $4.10\times$ acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/Iiiiiiirene/LearniBridge.

</details>


### [157] [Ask, Solve, Generate: Self-Evolving Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation via Self-Consistency Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27376)
*Ritesh Thawkar,Shravan Venkatraman,Omkar Thawakar,Abdelrahman Shaker,Fahad Khan,Hisham Cholakkal,Salman Khan,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型的自我进化训练框架，通过内部角色（提议者、求解者、生成者）协同和一致性信号，同时提升统一大多模态模型的视觉理解和图像生成能力。在多种架构上验证有效，MMMU提升+3.5%，GenEval从82%提升至85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一大多模态模型（LMMs）在同时支持视觉理解和图像生成时，仍依赖人工标注、偏好标签或外部奖励模型等后期监督信号。本文探索是否可以利用无标签图像，让模型自主提升这两方面能力。

Method: 提出自我进化训练框架，包含三个内部角色：Proposer（生成视觉问题）、Solver（回答问题并评估）、Generator（合成图像）。训练仅使用自我导出的一致性信号，无外部监督。引入Solver Token Entropy（STE）作为连续难度信号稳定学习，设计多尺度内部评估方案（QA保真度评分+循环一致性描述）用于图像生成评估。

Result: 在八项理解指标上持续优于基础模型；在BAGEL上MMMU绝对提升+3.5%，GenEval从82%提升至85%。方法在BLIP3o（扩散）、BAGEL（rectified-flow）、VARGPT-v1.1（自回归）三种架构上均有效。

Conclusion: 统一LMM可以通过自我演化的方式，仅利用无标签图像和内部一致性信号同时提升视觉理解与图像生成能力，无需外部监督。Solver Token Entropy是稳定自训练的关键技术。

Abstract: Most unified large multimodal models (LMMs) that support both visual understanding and image generation still rely on curated post-training supervision, such as human annotations, preference labels, or external reward models. We ask whether a unified LMM can improve both abilities autonomously using only unlabeled images. We propose a self-evolving training framework with three internal roles: a Proposer that generates visual questions, a Solver that answers and evaluates them, and a Generator that synthesizes images. Training uses only self-derived consistency signals, without human annotations, preference labels, or task-trained external reward/judge models. To stabilize learning, we introduce Solver Token Entropy (STE), a continuous difficulty signal based on token-level prediction uncertainty that remains useful even when sample-level consistency becomes unreliable. For image generation, we design a multi-scale internal evaluation scheme that combines question-answer fidelity scoring with cycle-consistent captioning. This creates a solver-mediated coupling, where better visual understanding enables more reliable generation assessment and stronger internal training signals. The framework preserves the same role decomposition, reward logic, and training schedule across diffusion-based BLIP3o, rectified-flow BAGEL, and autoregressive VARGPT-v1.1 architectures, requiring only each backbone's native prompting and generation interface. Across eight understanding metrics, our method consistently improves over the corresponding base models. On BAGEL, it achieves a $+3.5\%$ absolute gain on MMMU and improves GenEval image generation performance from $82\%$ to $85\%$. Code and models are publicly released.

</details>


### [158] [DanceOPD: On-Policy Generative Field Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27377)
*Wei Zhou,Xiongwei Zhu,Zelin Xu,Bo Dong,Lixue Gong,Yongyuan Liang,Meng Chu,Leigang Qu,Lingdong Kong,Wei Liu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DanceOPD提出了一种基于on-policy生成场蒸馏的框架，用于流匹配模型中统一文本到图像生成、局部编辑和全局编辑等多能力，通过将每个能力源定义为共享流状态空间上的速度场，让学生模型在其自生成的轨迹状态上学习，有效解决了多能力组合中的冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像生成需要一个统一多种能力（T2I、局部编辑、全局编辑）的单一模型，但这些能力天然不对齐且相互冲突（如编辑会损害T2I性能，全局与局部编辑相互干扰），因此如何有效组合这些能力成为核心挑战。

Method: 提出DanceOPD框架，采用on-policy生成场蒸馏：将每个能力源定义为共享流状态空间上的速度场，每个样本被路由到一个能力场，查询一个低噪声的学生诱导状态，使用简单的速度MSE损失进行训练。学生模型在其自身的rollout状态上查询专家场，从而学习组合多种能力。该框架还能吸收算子定义的场（如无分类器引导）。

Result: 在T2I、编辑、真实性场吸收和CFG吸收的综合实验表明，该方法改善了多能力组合，增强了目标能力同时保持了锚点生成质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为流匹配模型中的生成场蒸馏建立了一条实用路径，有效解决了多能力组合冲突问题。

Abstract: Modern image generation demands a single model that unifies diverse capabilities, including text-to-image (T2I), local editing, and global editing. However, these capabilities are rarely naturally aligned and often conflict. For instance, editing tends to degrade T2I performance, while global and local editing interfere with each other. Consequently, effectively composing these capabilities has become a central challenge for image generation model training. To tackle this, we introduce DanceOPD, an on-policy generative field distillation framework for flow-matching models that routes each sample to one capability field, queries one low-noise student-induced state, and trains with a simple velocity MSE objective. With each capability source defined as a velocity field over the shared flow state space, the student learns from fields queried on its own rollout states to compose expert capabilities. This formulation also absorbs operator-defined fields such as classifier-free guidance. Comprehensive experiments on T2I, editing, realism-field absorption, and CFG absorption show that our approach improves multi-capability composition, strengthening target capabilities while preserving anchor generation quality. We believe this work establishes a practical route for generative field distillation in flow-matching models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [MIRAGE: Protecting against Malicious Image Editing via False Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26199)
*Anshul Nasery,Ramnath Kumar,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Sewoong Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIRAGE 是一种针对AI图像编辑系统的免疫方法，通过向图像添加对抗扰动，触发商业图像编辑器的安全审核机制（而非干扰生成模型本身），从而阻止对个人图像的未授权编辑。该方法无需访问模型权重或编辑提示，在多个闭源API上实现超过88%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像免疫方法需要访问模型权重和编辑提示，无法应用于商业闭源图像编辑系统（如GPT-Image、Gemini Flash Image、Grok Imagine等）。因此需要一种无需这些信息且能防御强大商业编辑器的图像保护方法。

Method: MIRAGE 方法利用商业图像编辑系统的预生成安全审核机制这一攻击面。通过向图像添加对抗扰动，使图像在开源嵌入模型和审核模型的表示空间中与违反策略的概念对齐，从而触发审核分类器将其标记为违规，导致系统自动拒绝编辑请求。该方法与编辑提示无关。

Result: 在多个闭源图像编辑API上评估，MIRAGE 实现了超过88%的成功率，有效阻止了未授权的AI图像编辑。

Conclusion: MIRAGE 是一种简单、与提示无关且有效的图像免疫方法，通过利用系统级安全审核机制而非干扰生成模型，为保护个人图像免受AI编辑提供了实用路径。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-powered image editing systems raises serious concerns because it allows personal images to be arbitrarily manipulated at scale, with minimal effort, and a lower barrier to entry. Prior work on image immunization adds imperceptible perturbations to an image to protect against unauthorized manipulations. However, these methods usually require access to the model weights and the image manipulating prompt. This significantly limits their use, especially against powerful commercial image-editors such as GPT-Image, Gemini Flash Image (Nano Banana), and Grok Imagine. To address this, we take a system-level view of the problem and identify a previously unexplored attack surface common to all major commercial image editing systems: pre-generation safety moderation.Rather than disrupting the generative model itself, we propose to immunize images by causing these moderation classifiers to flag images as policy-violating, triggering an automatic refusal regardless of the editing prompt. We operationalize this by adding adversarial perturbations to align our image to policy-violating concepts in the representation space of an ensemble of open-source embedding and moderation models. We call our method MIRAGE, which stands for Moderation Induced Resistance Against Generative Editing. We evaluate MIRAGE against multiple closed-source image editing APIs and demonstrate success rates of more than 88%. Our approach is simple, prompt-agnostic, and effective, offering a practical path towards protecting personal images from unauthorized AI-powered editing.

</details>


### [160] [Data Facts: A Metadata Schema for Structured Data Exchange in the NANDini Multi-Agent Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26211)
*Jin Gao,Maria Gorskikh,Pradyumna Chari,Brittany Box,Mukul Kemla,Pratik Behera,Abhishek Mehta,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NANDini提出Data Facts元数据模式，通过轻量JSON格式在代理发现与数据访问间建立桥梁，结合安全管道实现自主数据交换。实验证明数据访问使决策准确率从35.2%提升至100%，安全机制有效防范数据伪造与泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理协议（NANDA、A2A、MCP）仅支持身份发现与通信，缺乏结构化数据交换机制；而企业数据共享框架（IDS-RAM、Gaia-X、Ocean Protocol）依赖人工监管，无法满足自主代理实时交互需求。

Method: 设计Data Facts轻量JSON元数据模式，通过data_facts_url指针接入现有Agent Facts注册表；包含数据集身份、访问层级（公开/半私有/私有）、端点、TTL新鲜度校验和SHA-256完整性校验和；对私有数据实施JWT认证、能力作用域网关授权、A2A凭据委托的三层安全管道。

Result: 840次决策评估中，数据驱动代理准确率100% vs 无数据访问35.2%（p<0.001）；TTL将过期数据错误从37.6%降至8.8%；校验和实现100%损坏检测；安全管道拦截全部46次伪造攻击，零数据泄露。

Conclusion: Data Facts作为NANDini生态核心组件，有效填补了自主代理间结构化数据交换的空白，为去中心化智能网络的数据可信流通提供了可行的基础设施方案。

Abstract: NANDini (Networked Agents Natural Distillation of Interconnected Nodal Intelligence) envisions an automated ecosystem where intelligent agents independently create, process, and exchange data to drive decisions at scale. Realizing this vision requires infrastructure beyond agent discovery and communication: agents must be able to advertise, evaluate, and verify the datasets they hold.
  Current protocols, including NANDA for federated registry and A2A and MCP for inter-agent messaging, address identity and communication but provide no mechanism for structured data exchange. Existing enterprise data-sharing frameworks, such as IDS-RAM, Gaia-X, and Ocean Protocol, assume human-in-the-loop governance that is incompatible with autonomous, real-time agent interactions.
  We introduce Data Facts, a core NANDini concept: a lightweight JSON metadata schema that bridges agent discovery and data access through a single pointer, `data_facts_url`, added to an existing Agent Facts registry record. The linked document encodes dataset identity, access tier, whether public, semi-private, or private, endpoint, a time-to-live for freshness validation, and a SHA-256 integrity checksum.
  For private and semi-private data, we implement a three-layer security pipeline: JWT authentication, capability-scoped gateway authorization, and an A2A credential delegation protocol. Across 840 decision-making evaluations, data-informed agents achieve 100% accuracy versus 35.2% without data access (p < 0.001); TTL enforcement reduces stale-data errors from 37.6% to 8.8%; checksum verification achieves 100% corruption detection at all injection rates; and the security pipeline blocks all 46 forgery attempts with zero data leakage.

</details>


### [161] [CyberChainBench: Can AI Agents Secure Smart Contracts Against Real-World On-Chain Vulnerabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26216)
*Jintao Huang,Fengqing Jiang,Radha Poovendran,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberChainBench是一个评估LLM智能体在智能合约安全方面能力的基准，涵盖漏洞检测、利用生成和补丁合成三个任务，基于541个真实DeFi攻击事件，在历史分叉链上进行端到端评估。最佳配置（Codex + GPT-5.5）在检测、利用和修补上分别得分37.5%、43.7%和23.4%，在200个案例中实现5740万美元总利用利润，单例成本2.39美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对智能体在真实区块链环境下进行端到端安全评估的能力，无法全面衡量LLM智能体在漏洞检测、利用和修复三个环节的综合表现。

Method: 从DeFiHackLabs收集541个真实攻击事件，覆盖9条EVM链，利用Harbor构建隔离评估环境，智能体通过工具读取代码、追踪交易并在主网分叉上验证利用。定义五类漏洞分类体系，利用历史交易作为测试预言机验证补丁有效性。

Result: 最佳配置（Codex + GPT-5.5）在检测、利用和修补任务上分别取得37.5%、43.7%和23.4%的得分，在200个利用案例中实现5740万美元总利润，单例成本2.39美元。

Conclusion: 任务难度呈现明显梯度：检测<利用<修补；当前LLM智能体在智能合约安全领域尤其是补丁生成方面仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: We present CyberChainBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents on smart contract security across three complementary tasks: vulnerability detection, exploit generation, and patch synthesis. Built from 541 real-world exploit incidents from DeFiHackLabs spanning 9 EVM chains, the benchmark provides end-to-end on-chain evaluation where agents interact with historical blockchain state through isolated evaluation environments orchestrated by Harbor, using tools to read code, trace transactions, and validate exploits on mainnet forks. Each case is anchored to a specific block and includes structured ground truth covering vulnerability type, localization, and attacker profit. Exploits are graded by economic impact on historical forks; patches are validated by replaying historical attacks and legitimate transactions as fail-to-pass test oracles on a proxy-upgradeable subset. We define a five-type vulnerability taxonomy and evaluate multiple agent--model configurations. Results reveal a clear difficulty gradient: the best configuration scores 37.5% on detection, 43.7% on exploitation, but only 23.4% on patching, with the top agent (Codex with GPT-5.5) realizing \$57.4M in total exploit profit across the 200-case exploit set at a cost of $2.39 per case.

</details>


### [162] [Expecting (Targeted Ads)? Network Analysis of User Health Data Leakage in Fertility Tracking Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26276)
*Yeeun Jo,Shahanaasree Sivakumar,Mahnoor Jameel,Camille Cobb,Adam Bates,Brad Reaves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对20款Android生育追踪App进行网络测量，发现其中5款存在用户健康数据显式泄露或通过上下文广告URL的隐式泄露，同时也有使用广告模型但无数据泄露的App，强调了消费者选择在隐私保护中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多关注生育追踪App中的人为隐私因素，但对App数据处理实践的技术层面关注不足，尤其是与第三方广告服务共享用户数据的机制尚不明确。

Method: 从Google Play商店选取20款Android生育追踪App，系统化其功能特征后，在记录TLS剥离网络流量的环境中执行标准化用户交互，进行基于网络的测量研究。

Result: 在5款App中检测到用户健康数据的显式泄露以及通过高度针对性的上下文广告URL的隐式泄露；同时发现部分App采用广告变现模型但无明显数据泄露，另有部分App与广告服务交互极少。

Conclusion: 研究为用户的广泛隐私担忧提供了技术层面的实证支持，同时强调了消费者在选择生育追踪App时对隐私影响进行权衡的重要性。

Abstract: While human factors in the privacy of fertility tracking apps -- health trackers that record user's menstrual or pregnancy data -- has been the subject of extensive study, little attention has been paid to the technical aspects of apps' data handling practices. We conduct a network-based measurement study of a corpus of 20 Android fertility tracking apps from the Google Play Store, focusing on how user data is shared with third party advertising services. After systematizing app features, we conduct a series of standardized user interactions across all apps in an environment that records TLS-stripped network traffic. In a subset of apps (n=5) we identify explicit leakage of user health data as well implicit leakage through highly targeted contextual advertising URL's. Equally importantly, we observe additional apps that use an ad-based monetization model without apparent leakage of user data, as well as several apps the interact only minimally with ad services. These findings provide technical grounding for widespread user concerns, but also underscore the importance of consumer choice in the privacy implications of app-based fertility tracking.

</details>


### [163] [TEMPO-Diffusion: Temporally Exposed Malicious Poisoning of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26285)
*William Aiken,Paula Branco,Guy-Vincent Jourdan,Iosif-Viorel Onut*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMPO-Diffusion，一种针对扩散模型的时间条件后门攻击框架，支持类间定向攻击、多子图后门修复和时间条件触发；同时引入CALISA数据集，实验证明该方法能有效毒化类特定合成数据并诱导下游分类器高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于噪声的后门攻击在扩散模型上依赖输入时触发注入、非定向激活和分布外目标生成，降低了隐蔽性和实际相关性，因此需要一种更隐蔽、更实用的定向后门攻击框架。

Method: 提出TEMPO-Diffusion框架：(i) 支持对特定类别的定向攻击；(ii) 多子图像后门，可在多个输出图像的多个位置重构特定特征；(iii) 时间条件触发的图像修复。同时引入CALISA数据集以评估实际场景下的安全性。

Result: 在CIFAR10、GTSRB和CALISA数据集上的实验表明，TEMPO-Diffusion能够可靠地毒化类特定合成数据生成，并在基于该数据训练的下游分类器中诱导高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: TEMPO-Diffusion通过时间条件触发机制实现了更隐蔽、更具实际相关性的定向后门攻击，对使用后门扩散模型生成合成训练数据的安全实践提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Noise-based backdoor attacks on diffusion models typically rely on input-time trigger injection, untargeted activation, and out-of-distribution target generation. Such assumptions reduce both the stealthiness and the practical relevance of these attacks. In this work, we present TEMPO-Diffusion, a targeted backdoor framework that localizes the malicious distribution shift to a temporal, in-distribution exposure. TEMPO-Diffusion supports: (i) targeted attacks on and to specific classes, (ii) multiple sub-image backdoors that reconstruct specific features within multiple, different output images and at multiple locations, and (iii) in-painting with time-conditioned triggers. To study relevant, practical security concerns in leveraging backdoored diffusion models for synthetic training data, we also introduce CALISA: a balanced, region-aware traffic-sign dataset emphasizing Canadian and U.S. road signs. Across CIFAR10, GTSRB, and CALISA, our experiments show that TEMPO-Diffusion can reliably poison class-specific synthetic data generation and induce high attack success rates in downstream classifiers trained on that data.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Takedown: Measuring Malicious Go Module Persistence in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26291)
*Minjae Bae,Carter Yagemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测量了Go生态系统中的自动化供应链攻击活动。攻击者将合法Go模块重新打包到其控制的账号下，并嵌入混淆代码以实现导入触发的下载器。通过手动搜索2113个仓库和对1230万索引条目进行大规模扫描，识别出2289个恶意版本的合法Go模块。研究发现仅依赖GitHub的搜索无法完全发现攻击，且至少99.4%被GitHub移除或暂停的恶意模块仍可通过Go代理检索。披露后，GitHub移除了684个恶意仓库，Google Go团队修复了1377个模块版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go生态系统缺乏对自动化供应链攻击的全面测量和理解，现有基于GitHub的检测方法存在盲区，无法捕捉完整的攻击范围。

Method: 采用两种互补分析方法：(a) 对2113个GitHub仓库进行手动搜索；(b) 使用自研的去混淆AST扫描器GOAST对1230万索引条目进行大规模扫描。此外，通过基于代理的测量来评估移除-修复差距。

Result: 识别出2289个恶意版本的合法Go模块；证明纯GitHub搜索无法发现全部攻击；至少99.4%被GitHub移除或标记的恶意模块仍可通过Go代理获取；披露后GitHub移除684个恶意仓库，Google Go团队修复1377个模块版本。

Conclusion: Go生态系统存在严重的自动化供应链攻击威胁，现有基于平台的检测和移除机制存在显著盲区，Go代理的缓存机制导致恶意模块在被移除后仍可长期被获取，需要更全面的安全防护措施。

Abstract: We measure an automation-based supply chain campaign in the Go ecosystem. The attackers repackage legitimate Go modules under attacker-controlled owners, and embed them with obfuscated code for an import-triggered downloader. Our results come from two complementary analyses: a) a manual search on GitHub across 2,113 repositories and b) a large-scale scan of 12.3M index entries using a deobfuscating AST scanner (GOAST) that we implemented. As a result, we identified 2,289 malicious versions of legitimate Go modules. We demonstrate that purely GitHub-centric searches fail to identify the full extent of the compromise and are only effective for as long as the affected code is present on the platform. Moreover, our proxy-based measurements of the takedown-remediation gap reveal that among artifacts later found to be GitHub-unobservable (i.e., removed or suspended), at least 99.4% remained retrievable via Go proxy. Following our disclosure, GitHub has removed 684 malicious repositories and the Google Go team has remediated 1,377 module versions.

</details>


### [165] [Hybrid privacy-aware semantic search: SVD-truncated document geometry and CKKS-encrypted query reranking under a restricted threat model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26373)
*Sergey Kurilenko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合保护方案：文档向量通过SVD截断+秘密旋转进行几何保护，查询向量通过CKKS同态加密进行密码学保护，在百万级规模和5种编码器上实现了亚秒级延迟和保序性，但文档保护仅属经验性混淆层而非密码学原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御手段存在两极困境：同态加密虽然安全但速度太慢无法扩展到百万文档规模，而添加隐私噪声在达到保护效果之前已严重破坏排序质量。本文希望探索一条中间路径，利用静态集合与动态查询之间的不对称性来兼顾安全与效率。

Method: 集合端：将每个文档向量截断到低维SVD子空间，再用仅所有者可知的秘密正交变换旋转。查询端：采用CKKS同态加密对查询进行重排序，诚实但好奇的服务器无法看到查询向量或得分。CKKS参数通过离线小规模基准测试确定。理论上：证明了攻击者在受限子空间内重建误差的紧下界。

Result: 在100万文档和5种编码器上：方案保持排序质量（对强编码器甚至略有提升，起到线性去噪作用），延迟低于1秒；现成的反演攻击在保护空间中完全失效。对更强攻击者：已知明文攻击可通过正交Procrustes以约等于保留维度的泄露对数恢复旋转；公开乘积量化码保留了大部分最近邻结构；与随机投影、校准噪声和BEIR基线对比显示截断是编码器相关的精度代价而非免费去噪。

Conclusion: 文档保护（SVD截断+秘密旋转）是经验性混淆层而非密码学原语，查询保密是密码学级别的。论文明确了每种声明的威胁模型边界。

Abstract: Dense embeddings power semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation, but embedding-inversion attacks can reconstruct source text from a vector: when a vector database leaks, the documents behind it leak too. The textbook defences are extremes - encrypting the whole search homomorphically is sound but too slow at million-document scale, while privacy noise degrades ranking long before it protects. We study a middle path exploiting the asymmetry between the static collection and the dynamic query. The collection is protected geometrically: each vector is truncated onto a lower-dimensional SVD subspace and rotated by a secret orthogonal transform known only to the owner. The query is protected cryptographically: it is reranked under CKKS homomorphic encryption, so an honest-but-curious server never sees the query or the scores. CKKS parameters come from a small offline benchmark.
  We prove a tight lower bound on the reconstruction error of any attacker confined to the protected subspace. On one million documents and five encoders the scheme preserves ranking quality (slightly improving it on strong encoders, as a linear denoiser) at sub-second latency, and an off-the-shelf inversion attack on the protected space collapses to the noise floor. We then test stronger adversaries: a known-plaintext attacker recovers the rotation by orthogonal Procrustes from about as many leaked pairs as the retained dimension; the public product-quantization codes preserve most nearest-neighbour structure; and random-projection, calibrated-noise and BEIR baselines show the truncation is an encoder-dependent accuracy cost, not a free denoiser. We state the limits: query confidentiality is cryptographic, but document protection is an empirical obfuscation layer (SVD truncation plus a secret rotation), not a cryptographic primitive, and we delimit the threat model for each claim.

</details>


### [166] [Verifying Intent and Harm: A Unified Defense Against LLM-Generated Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26377)
*Poojitha Thota,Yun Lei,Santhosh Thangaraj,Siddhartha Reddy Jonnalagadda,Shirin Nilizadeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合验证用户提示意图与LLM响应危害性的防御框架，通过专门的分析器（意图评估+危害评估）和裁决器（Judge）协同工作，在五类威胁场景下将平均F1提升至0.95，攻击成功率降至4.1%，显著优于单侧防御和单智能体推理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法仅单独分析用户提示或模型输出，但许多真实攻击利用提示中的对抗意图与响应中的可操作危害之间的分离来绕过检测，导致单侧防御频繁遗漏恶意交互。

Method: 提出验证中心防御框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 意图分析器（评估提示意图）；2) 危害分析器（评估响应危害）；3) 裁决器（解决矛盾并做出最终判定）。框架在五类威胁（越狱、提示注入、钓鱼、网络滥用、有害内容）上进行了评估。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，联合验证将平均F1从最强基线的0.90提升至0.95，攻击成功率降至4.1%。相比单智能体+CoT基线，F1从0.87提升至0.95，假阳性率从0.12降至0.06。还验证了对架构感知自适应攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提示-响应联合验证为保障LLM应用安全提供了实用基础，能够有效抵御不断演进的对抗性威胁。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in interactive applications, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial interactions that induce harmful, deceptive, or policy-violating outputs. Existing defenses typically analyze either user prompts or generated outputs, but not both. However, many real-world attacks exploit a separation between adversarial intent expressed in the prompt and actionable harm manifested only in the response. As a result, prompt-only and response-only defenses frequently miss unsafe interactions that appear benign when viewed from either side in isolation. We present a verification-centric defense framework that jointly evaluates prompt intent and response harm before an LLM response is delivered to a user. The framework employs specialized analysts for intent and harm assessment together with a Judge for conflict resolution. We formalize a threat model for prompt-response attacks and evaluate the framework across five threat categories: jailbreaks, prompt injection, phishing, cyber abuse, and harmful content. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets show that jointly verifying prompt intent and response harm consistently outperforms single-sided defenses and single-agent reasoning baselines. Across threat categories, the framework improves average F1 from 0.90 for the strongest applicable baselines to 0.95 while reducing the average attack success rate to 4.1 percent. Compared with a Single-Agent+CoT baseline, it improves average F1 from 0.87 to 0.95 and reduces the false positive rate on benign-sensitive requests from 0.12 to 0.06. We further evaluate architecture-aware adaptive attacks in which the attacker knows the verifier structure and attempts to bypass individual verification components. Our results suggest that prompt-response verification provides a practical foundation for securing LLM applications against evolving adversarial threats.

</details>


### [167] [Lessons from the Adoption and Deprecation of the Privacy Sandbox Web APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26390)
*Yohan Beugin,Paul Barford,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Google Privacy Sandbox API进行了为期七年的纵向测量与分析，发现其采纳率有限且不均衡，最终于2025年底被Google取消。研究基于HTTP Archive和Chrome遥测数据，分析了各API在整个生命周期内的普及情况，并从激励与风险角度解释了低采纳率的原因，最后为下一代Web隐私方案提出了建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多年来多个Web行为者一直试图减少网络追踪，但如何在隐私与效用之间取得理想平衡仍不明确。Google于2019年启动Privacy Sandbox旨在解决这一权衡，但其2025年的取消为研究生态系统的反应及失败原因提供了独特机会。

Method: 利用历史HTTP Archive爬虫数据和公开的Chrome遥测数据，对Privacy Sandbox各API在整个生命周期（部分超过5年）内，在CrUX前10万流行网站上的普及程度进行纵向测量与分析，同时结合Chrome用户浏览旅程的实际体验数据。

Result: 采纳率始终有限且不均衡：仅少数Web行为者实施了非常特定的API，且实施方式各异。从激励（兴趣、资源、时间线等）和风险（潜在权衡、隐私侵犯、法律风险等）角度解释了这一结果。

Conclusion: Privacy Sandbox的失败揭示了“在浏览器中修复”方案的局限性和跨浏览器差异——Chrome中的追踪和第三方Cookie限制仍主要是选择加入，而Brave、Firefox、Safari等浏览器默认启用限制。研究为下一代Web隐私提案提供了可行建议。

Abstract: While several web actors have been trying to reduce web tracking for years, it remains unclear how to achieve both desirable levels of utility and privacy. In 2019, Google launched the Privacy Sandbox initiative to balance that trade-off and find privacy alternatives to common use cases such as advertising. Yet, in late 2025, Google canceled the project and deprecated most of the newly introduced APIs. Despite its end, the Privacy Sandbox represents a unique opportunity to learn about how the ecosystem reacted to the proposed changes and make observations about why and how it failed. In this paper, we present a longitudinal measurement and analysis study of the Privacy Sandbox APIs to characterize their adoption and deprecation over the past seven years by different web actors. Leveraging historical HTTP Archive crawls and public Chrome telemetry data, we offer the largest study of its kind into the prevalence of each Privacy Sandbox feature, during their entire respective lifetime (5+ years for some), on popular websites (CrUX top 100k), and as experienced by Chrome users during their browsing journey. Our results showcase an adoption that remained limited and uneven across the years; only few web actors implemented very specific APIs, and in disparate manners. We motivate our interpretation of these results by considering the incentives (interest, resources, timeline, etc.) and risks (potential trade-offs, privacy violations, and legal exposure, etc.) for these actors. Finally, our analysis also yields actionable recommendations for the next generation of web privacy proposals. More broadly, the Privacy Sandbox illustrates the limitations and disparities across browsers of ``fix it in the browser'' remedies: today, tracking and third-party cookies limitations in Chrome still remain largely opt-in, while they have been enabled by default on other browsers like Brave, Firefox, or Safari.

</details>


### [168] [What Browsers Do in the Shaders: A Measurement Study of WebGPU Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26412)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出WGPULens框架，系统测量WebGPU在浏览器中泄露隐私信号的途径。研究发现管线编译状态是最清晰的隐私泄露面，冷/热管线探测可揭示跨来源、配置文件和浏览器位置的编译状态。参与者研究表明WebGPU行为具有高度区分性，网页爬虫发现WebGPU主要用于适配器探测而非实际计算。缓解策略试验表明源码级密钥分离可作为评估管线缓存分区的代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU通过验证模型保障内存安全，但共享的浏览器、驱动、操作系统和GPU状态仍可能暴露隐私相关信号，现有研究缺乏对这些信号的系统性测量框架。

Method: WGPULens框架：1) 控制场景实验测量泄漏、边界和缓解策略；2) 参与者实地研究评估部署、兼容性和可指纹性；3) Tranco爬虫测量真实页面中的WebGPU暴露情况。

Result: 1) 管线编译状态是最清晰的隐私泄露面，可跨源探测GPU活动；2) 参与者研究中WebGPU行为高度区分，确定性组件在单次运行内稳定但跨访问稳定性较低；3) 网页爬虫显示WebGPU主要用于适配器探测，未观察到实际计算活动。

Conclusion: WebGPU隐私分析必须针对特定表面进行：浏览器需要测量哪些GPU状态跨越哪些边界、哪些浏览器可见信号会泄露这些状态，以及相应缓解措施的成本。

Abstract: WebGPU lets ordinary web pages run GPU workloads through a validated programming model. Validation protects memory safety, but shared browser, driver, OS, and GPU state can still expose privacy-relevant signals. We present WGPULens, a framework for measuring those signals across controlled scenarios, browser-native co-residency, a participant field study, public page loads, and mitigation policies. Our framework separates measurements: controlled scenarios support leakage, boundary, and mitigation claims; participant runs support deployment, compatibility, and fingerprintability; and a Tranco crawl measures WebGPU exposure in real-world pages.
  Our controlled results identify persistent pipeline compilation state as the clearest surface. Cold/warm pipeline probes reveal prior compilation state across selected origin, profile, and browser placements. Controlled browser/native experiments also show native GPU activity can be inferred from browser-visible observables under labeled workloads. Other resource probes provide weaker positive results and negative controls.
  The participant field study shows active WebGPU behavior is highly distinctive within the sample, with deterministic components stable within runs and lower exact stability across repeated visits. A page-load crawl finds WebGPU use mainly as adapter probing and static support code, with no observed page-load shader, pipeline, queue, query, or map activity. Mitigation pilots identify source-level key separation as a proxy for evaluating pipeline-cache partitioning. Overall, WGPULens shows that WebGPU privacy analysis must be surface-specific: browsers need to measure which GPU state crosses which boundary, which browser-visible signals reveal it, and what the corresponding mitigations cost.

</details>


### [169] [Adaptive Evaluation of Out-of-Band Defenses Against Prompt Injection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26479)
*Praneeth Narisetty,Shiva Nagendra Babu Kore,Uday Kumar Reddy Kattamanchi,Jayaram Kumarapu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对工具使用型LLM代理的间接提示注入攻击，现有防御策略已从模型内训练转向模型外确定性策略强制（如CaMeL、FIDES、Progent等）。本文指出这些防御仅在静态基准上验证，存在方法论缺陷，随后在AgentDojo上对Progent进行自适应攻击复现，结果表明该防御有效（攻击成功率从25.8%降至4.2%），但仅为一个弱模型上的小规模数据点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型外强制策略的防御方法（如CaMeL、FIDES、Progent等）仅在静态基准上评估，而此前基于模型内训练的防御正是在自适应攻击下被大量攻破。作者担忧这些新防御可能重蹈覆辙，因此需要采用自适应评估协议重新检验。

Method: 首先将现有多种模型外防御统一归约为经典完整性保护（Biba）、引用监视器和最小权限原则进行结构化比较。然后指定自适应评估所需的威胁模型和协议，并在AgentDojo上对Progent进行独立复现和扩展实验，使用自托管开源模型Qwen2.5-7B在单张H200上运行。

Result: 三次运行平均结果显示，Progent将平均攻击成功率从25.8%降低至4.2%（约六倍降低），手工设计的自适应攻击也未提升成功率（2.6%），但此结果仅基于一个弱模型和单一黑盒攻击模板。

Conclusion: 确定性模型外强制策略可能比模型内检测更难被自适应攻击者攻破，但目前仅有一个小规模数据点支持这一假设，尚需更强攻击（如白盒GCG优化攻击）的进一步验证。

Abstract: Recent work (2024 to 2026) has converged on a strategy for defending tool-using LLM agents against indirect prompt injection: rather than training the model to refuse malicious instructions, enforce security outside the model with a deterministic policy that mediates the agent's actions. Systems such as CaMeL, FIDES, Progent, RTBAS, and FORGE realize this with capabilities, information-flow labels, and reference monitors, and several report near-elimination of attacks on the AgentDojo benchmark. We make two contributions. First, we organize these out-of-band defenses as instances of classical integrity protection (Biba), reference monitoring, and least privilege, yielding a structured comparison of what they do and do not cover. Second, we warn that every one of them is validated only on static benchmarks (a fixed set of injection attempts), the same methodology that made in-band defenses look strong until adaptive, defense-aware attacks broke twelve of them at over 90% success; we specify the threat model and protocol an adaptive evaluation requires. We then run that protocol as an independent reproduction and extension of Progent's own adaptive-attack analysis, on AgentDojo, with an open-weight agent (Qwen2.5-7B) self-hosted on a single H200, a setting its authors did not test. Averaged over three runs, the defense held: Progent cut mean attack success roughly sixfold (25.8% to 4.2%), and a hand-crafted adaptive attack did not raise it (2.6%). This is one small-scale data point on a weak model with a single black-box attack template; a stronger optimized (white-box GCG) attack remains open. The result is consistent with, but does not establish, the hypothesis that deterministic out-of-band enforcement is a harder target for an adaptive attacker than in-band detection.

</details>


### [170] [DKVE: Decentralized Key Validation for End-to-End Encrypted Messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26486)
*Subin Song,Taekyoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DKVE是一种利用社交图谱进行隐私保护式交叉验证的公钥验证协议，通过OPRF和OKVS技术保护查询隐私，结合SPRT检测MitM攻击，在强到中社交关系网络中检测率超过97%，可将KT查询频率降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端加密系统在公钥分发上存在两难：带外验证安全性高但不可扩展，密钥透明度系统可自动化验证但存储成本和操作复杂度高，缺乏一种兼具安全性与可扩展性的公钥验证机制。

Method: DKVE协议：客户端从密钥服务器获取联系人公钥后，向共同联系人查询验证；使用Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions (OPRF)和Oblivious Key-Value Stores (OKVS)保护查询隐私和联系人列表隐私；采用Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT)聚合响应并检测服务器异常行为，支持用户可配置的错误边界。

Result: 在真实社交网络数据集上的仿真显示：DKVE在强到中社交关系网络中MitM攻击检测率超过97%；将KT查询频率降低两个数量级；概念验证实现证明可在商用硬件上作为后台任务运行。

Conclusion: DKVE无法完全替代现有方法（存在引导问题、弱连接网络性能下降），但作为一种实用的补充性密钥验证机制，能够减少安全消息系统在大规模部署中的障碍，使KT目录可从快速但空间效率低的Merkle树迁移到空间高效的数据结构如RSA累加器。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted messaging systems depend on authentic public key distribution to prevent man-in-the-middle (MitM) attacks. Current solutions present a stark trade-off: out-of-band (OOB) verification provides strong security but lacks scalability for large contact lists, while key transparency (KT) systems enable automated verification at high storage costs and operational complexity.
  We propose DKVE, a protocol that validates public keys through privacy-preserving cross-validation within users' social graphs. When obtaining a contact's public key from a key server, clients query mutual contacts to verify they hold the same key, combining Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions (OPRF) and Oblivious Key-Value Stores (OKVS) to preserve privacy of both queries and contact lists. DKVE employs a Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT) to aggregate responses and detect server misbehavior with user-configurable error bounds.
  We evaluate DKVE through simulations on real social network datasets, demonstrating DKVE can detect MitM attacks with exceeding 97% for strong-to-moderate-tie networks. The remaining 3% of cases require validation through alternative methods such as KT and OOB verification. Our proof-of-concept implementation confirms feasibility for background operation on commodity hardware, in terms of the latency and bandwidth.
  As DKVE can reduce the frequency of KT queries by two orders of magnitude, it enables fundamental architectural shifts: KT directories can migrate from fast but space-inefficient Merkle trees to space-efficient data structures like RSA accumulators. While DKVE cannot replace existing methods entirely -- suffering from bootstrapping problems and degraded performance on weak-tie networks -- it provides a practical complementary key validation mechanism, making secure messaging more deployable for billion-user systems.

</details>


### [171] [VIGIL: Runtime Enforcement of Behavioral Specifications in AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26524)
*Ying Li,Yanju Chen,Hongbo Wen,Bosi Zhang,Hanzhi Liu,Peiran Wang,Yu Feng,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VIGIL是一个面向智能体系统的端到端运行时强制框架，通过将行为策略转化为SMT约束来检测执行轨迹中的违规行为，在多种任务场景下实现了超过95%的召回率和低于10%的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统越来越多地通过第三方技能行动，但这些技能的自然语言规范缺乏可执行的运行时强制能力。现有强制机制假设固定的事件模型或强制点，无法处理上下文粒度挑战——即监控器需要决定观察哪些事件、保留什么状态、推理多远以及何处干预，选择错误的粒度要么阻碍良性执行，要么遗漏跨多个动作才显现的违规。

Method: VIGIL提出了一种策略语言，能够捕捉智能体-工具事件上的上下文特定强制需求，包括时间依赖、参数约束和值流条件。该语言配有一套符号化评估规则，将策略转化为有限迹上的SMT约束，从而检测依赖于事件顺序、参数关系或跨调用值流的违规行为。

Result: 在涵盖办公文档、运维和工程任务的真实LLM智能体运行中，VIGIL检测策略违规的召回率超过95%，误报率低于10%。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过细粒度的上下文感知强制机制，有效解决了智能体系统中策略执行的粒度挑战，实现了高召回率和低误报率，为智能体系统的安全运行时监控提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly act through third-party skills, allowing model-generated decisions to affect files, communication channels, and cyber-physical devices. These skills often include natural-language specifications that define access permissions, disclosure limits, execution privileges, and required preconditions. Although such specifications describe the intended boundaries of skill behavior, they do not by themselves provide executable runtime enforcement. Enforcing them raises a contextual granularity challenge: even when a policy is written for a particular task context, a monitor must still decide which events to observe, what state to retain, how far across the execution to reason, and where to intervene. Choosing the wrong granularity can either block benign executions or miss violations that emerge only across multiple actions. Most existing enforcement mechanisms, however, assume a fixed event model or enforcement point.
  In this work, we present VIGIL, an end-to-end runtime enforcement framework for agentic systems. VIGIL checks an agent's actual execution trace against behavioral policies from skill specifications, operator-defined constraints, and global rules spanning multiple skills. To make such policies executable, VIGIL introduces a policy language that captures context-specific enforcement requirements over agent-tool events, including temporal dependencies, argument constraints, and value-flow conditions. The language is paired with symbolic evaluation rules that translate policies into SMT constraints over finite traces, allowing VIGIL to detect violations that depend on event order, argument relationships, or cross-call value flow rather than relying on fixed single-call filters. On real LLM-agent runs spanning office-document, operational, and engineering tasks, VIGIL detects policy violations with over 95% recall and a false-positive rate below 10%.

</details>


### [172] [TESLA-for-5G: Broadcast Authentication for 5G Networks Using TESLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26528)
*Subin Song,Michael K. Reiter,Taekyoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TESLA-for-5G (TF5)，一种结合TESLA和GG09 IBS的5G SIB1广播认证协议，用对称MAC替代数字签名以降低UE计算开销，并通过Tamarin形式化验证和OAI实现验证其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案要求UE对每个SIB1进行数字签名验证，计算开销大，对资源受限的UE造成沉重负担。

Method: 将TESLA广播认证协议与GG09 Schnorr类身份基签名(IBS)结合：稳态下使用对称MAC和延迟密钥披露认证SIB1，避免逐消息数字签名；初始信任通过轻量级GG09 IBS引导TESLA参数，免除证书分发开销。

Result: 在Tamarin中通过Dolev-Yao敌手模型的形式化验证，并在OpenAirInterface 5G协议栈上实现及基于trace的驱动分析，证明了TF5在计算、通信和存储成本上的优势。

Conclusion: TF5能有效降低UE认证SIB1的计算开销，同时保证安全性，适用于资源受限的5G用户设备。

Abstract: 5G base stations broadcast unauthenticated system information (SI) that every user equipment (UE) reads during cell selection. This enables attackers to broadcast forged SI from a fake base station (FBS), deceiving UEs into camping on it. Prior approaches require UEs to authenticate System Information Block 1 (SIB1) using digital signatures. This necessitates computation-heavy verification for every SIB1 reception, imposing a significant burden on resource-constrained UEs. We propose TESLA-for-5G (TF5), a broadcast authentication protocol for 5G SIB1 that combines TESLA with GG09 Schnorr-like identity-based signatures (IBS). In the steady state, TF5 enables UEs to authenticate each SIB1 message using a symmetric MAC and delayed key disclosure, eliminating the need for per-message digital signatures. Initial trust is bootstrapped during cell entry using a lightweight GG09 IBS over the TESLA parameters, avoiding certificate distribution overhead. We formally verify TF5 in Tamarin under a Dolev-Yao adversary and demonstrate its favorable computation, communication, and storage costs through both an implementation on the OpenAirInterface 5G stack and trace-driven analysis.

</details>


### [173] [Adversarial Diffusion Across Modalities: A Fusion Survey of Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation for Text, Vision, and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26566)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对AI系统中的对抗性评估进行了综述，将四种原本分离的研究方向（基于扩散的文本/LLM攻击、图像分类器攻击、视觉语言模型越狱、扩散防御）整合为统一框架，提出了六类扩散角色分类法、五维评估体系，并识别了当前LLM侧文献的五项共性缺陷及未来研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对抗性评估领域存在四个分离的研究轨道，各自有独立的词汇体系、威胁模型和基准测试，缺乏统一的分类和评估标准，阻碍了跨社区的知识迁移和方法论共识。

Method: 采用元研究层面的信息融合方法，系统梳理50篇已发表论文（覆盖文本/LLM、图像分类器、视觉语言模型、防御四个领域），提出六类扩散角色分类法、威胁模型轴（攻击者知识、查询预算、目标可访问性）和五维评估框架（攻击成功率、迁移性、查询预算、困惑度、防御规避）。

Result: 建立了统一的扩散攻击分类体系；识别出LLM侧文献的五个反复出现的弱点；提供了对比基准（含10种非扩散基线）；发布了配套目录和电子表格。

Conclusion: 该综述为扩散模型在AI对抗性评估中的角色提供了统一的视角，揭示了现有研究的不足，并指明了未来关键研究方向（开放问题与具体实验设计），强调这是一篇含质量评估的叙述性综述而非PRISMA系统性综述。

Abstract: Adversarial evaluation of AI systems has matured along four largely disconnected tracks: diffusion-based attacks on text and large language models (LLMs), diffusion-based attacks on image classifiers, jailbreak pipelines against vision-language models, and diffusion-based input purification defenses. Each has developed its own vocabulary, threat models, and benchmarks, with denoising diffusion models emerging as a shared generative mechanism whose recipes are now actively ported between communities. This survey performs an information-fusion exercise at the meta-research level: we integrate these four tracks into a single conceptual framework with a unified taxonomy, evaluation criteria, and research agenda, focusing on the LLM-side slice. We catalog fifty published papers across four scope areas (text/LLM, image classifier, vision-language model, defense), plus four diffusion-LLM-as-victim entries and ten non-diffusion baselines against which any new attack must be compared. We propose a six-class taxonomy of diffusion roles in adversarial pipelines, augmented by a threat-model axis recording attacker knowledge, query budget, and target accessibility, and apply a five-dimension framework (attack success rate, transferability, query budget, perplexity, defense-evasion) uniformly across modalities. The review adopts a dual attacker-defender perspective: alongside the attack catalog we cover four diffusion-based defenses that form the natural evaluation backdrop for new attacks. Our critical analysis identifies five recurring weaknesses of the current LLM-side literature, and we close with a research agenda of open questions and concrete experimental designs. The companion catalog and spreadsheet are released with the paper. We are explicit that this is a narrative review with quality assessment, not a PRISMA-compliant systematic review, and discuss the implications for replication.

</details>


### [174] [Agents That Know Too Much: A Data-Centric Survey of Privacy in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26627)
*Nada Lahjouji,Ashwin Gerard Colaco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文从数据中心的视角系统调查了大语言模型智能体的隐私风险，梳理了智能体接触的数据源、各数据源产生的隐私风险及其治理机制，并指出信息流控制是唯一能同时覆盖组合推理和跨会话推理泄露两种风险最小的治理机制，同时发现缺乏统一的隐私策略基准是当前领域最突出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体从回答问题转向处理敏感数据（查询数据库、调用外部API、记忆跨会话交互等），隐私保护变得更加困难。敏感信息不仅可能通过最终答案泄露，还可能通过发出的查询、处理的中间结果、写入的记忆以及与其他智能体交换的消息泄露。然而相关研究分散在检索增强生成、文本到SQL接口、智能体记忆、提示注入、访问控制和上下文隐私等多个领域，缺乏系统性的整合。

Method: 采用数据中心视角，对LLM智能体的隐私风险进行分类梳理：对智能体接触的数据源进行系统化分类，识别每个数据源产生的隐私风险，梳理应对这些风险的治理机制，并映射用于衡量这些风险的基准测试，识别其缺失部分。

Result: 两个核心发现：(1) 在各类治理机制中，只有信息流控制能够同时覆盖组合推理泄露和跨会话推理泄露这两种最缺乏保护的风险；(2) 目前没有任何基准测试能在统一的隐私策略下驱动智能体遍历其所有数据面，这是该领域最缺乏的研究工具。

Conclusion: 该综述为分散的文献提供了统一的参考框架，为未来研究提供了共同的问题表述方式，并指出了最关键的开放问题（统一的隐私策略基准的缺失）。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly query databases, search document collections, call external APIs, remember past interactions, and act on a user's behalf. As they move from answering questions to operating over sensitive data, privacy becomes harder to enforce. An agent touches many data sources, runs multi-step workflows, keeps state across sessions, and acts with delegated permissions. Sensitive information can therefore leak not only through its final answer but through the queries it issues, the intermediate results it handles, the memory it writes, and the messages it exchanges with other agents. We survey the privacy of LLM agents from a data-centric view, organizing the field around the data an agent touches rather than by attack type, and we use data agent as shorthand for an LLM agent that works with data. Research on these risks is active but scattered across retrieval-augmented generation, text-to-SQL interfaces, agent memory, prompt injection, access control, and contextual privacy. This survey brings that work together: we taxonomize the data sources an agent touches, the privacy risks each source creates, and the governance mechanisms that address them; we map the benchmarks used to measure these risks and identify what is missing; and we set out the open problems. Two findings recur: among governance mechanisms only information-flow control covers both compositional and cross-session inference leakage, the two least-protected risks; and no benchmark drives an agent across its data surfaces under one privacy policy, the instrument the field most lacks. Our goal is a reference that situates the scattered literature and gives future work a common framing.

</details>


### [175] [TGHE: Template-based Graph Homomorphic Encryption for Privacy-Preserving GNN Inference in Edge-Cloud Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26664)
*Ngoc Bao Anh Le,Thai T. Vu,John Le,Heath Cooper,Jun Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TGHE框架，利用交易图中局部计算树的模板现象，通过模板规范化和SIMD并行加密推理，在370万节点的大规模金融图上实现比顺序加密基线66.9倍加速且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于同态加密的GNN系统采用图中心范式，将每次查询成本与全局图规模耦合，最多只能处理约2万节点，无法适用于动态大规模金融图。

Method: 利用交易图中局部计算树收敛为少量结构模板的现象，在边缘对自我图进行规范化，将结构相同的树打包到共享CKKS密文中进行SIMD并行加密推理；并设计近似模板拟合和拓扑折叠两个长尾优化器实现完整SIMD覆盖。

Result: 在DGraphFin（370万节点、430万边）上，TGHE-Collapse相比顺序加密基线达到66.9倍加速，AUC损失小于0.002。

Conclusion: TGHE通过自我中心范式打破了图中心范式对图规模的限制，实现了大规模金融图上高效的隐私保护GNN推理。

Abstract: Existing homomorphic encryption (HE)-based GNN systems adopt a graph-centric paradigm that couples per-query cost to global graph size, limiting evaluations to at most ~20k nodes and making them incompatible with dynamic, large-scale financial graphs. We propose TGHE (Template-based Graph Homomorphic Encryption), an ego-centric framework that resolves this by exploiting a template phenomenon: local computation trees in transaction graphs converge into a small set of structural shapes. TGHE canonicalizes ego-graphs at the edge and packs structurally identical trees into shared CKKS ciphertexts for SIMD-parallel encrypted inference, with two long-tail optimizers (Approximate Template Fitting and Topology Collapse) ensuring full SIMD coverage. On DGraphFin (3.7M nodes, 4.3M edges), TGHE-Collapse achieves a 66.9x speedup over the sequential encrypted baseline with less than 0.002 AUC loss.

</details>


### [176] [The Fungible Reserve Standard: A Deterministic Framework for Encoding Carrying Costs in Asset-Backed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26704)
*JJ Jia Jing Tan,Eva Meng,Josh Ng,Zack Zhang,September Liu,Teelet Wang,Ludwig Zhang,Seth Yan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fungible Reserve Standard (FRS)标准，通过在链上透明编码托管、保险和审计等结构性负持仓成本，解决RWA代币化中资产单位价值随时间递减的问题，同时保持ERC-20兼容性和DeFi可组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界资产代币化面临结构性负持仓成本（托管、保险、审计费用）的挑战，现有方案在发行层面管理运营成本，缺乏链上透明的成本编码机制，且代币重基数方案破坏了同质性和DeFi可组合性。

Method: 提出FRS确定性代币设计框架，引入资产-代币比率变量q(t)，该变量按预定义年化持仓成本率递减；同时设计供应对账机制，在保持持有者余额不变和ERC-20兼容性的前提下实现成本链上编码。

Result: FRS框架是资产无关的，适用于任何具有可预测正持有成本的现实世界资产，能够在不牺牲DeFi兼容性的前提下提供纯机构级会计透明度。

Conclusion: FRS为RWA代币化中持仓成本管理提供了新型标准化解决方案，兼顾透明度、同质性、可组合性与机构级会计要求，潜在应用规模达数万亿美元。

Abstract: The tokenization of real-world assets (RWAs) has emerged as a transformative application of blockchain technology, with market projections estimating trillions of dollars in tokenized assets within the coming decade. However, a fundamental challenge remains unaddressed: physical assets such as precious metals, stored commodities, and warehoused goods incur structural negative carry -- custody, insurance, and audit costs that accumulate over time. While existing tokenization models have successfully established the market for digital gold and treasuries, they typically manage operational costs at the issuer level. The FRS introduces a framework to bring these economics directly on-chain, avoiding mechanisms such as token rebasing that compromise fungibility and composability with decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols. This paper proposes the Fungible Reserve Standard (FRS), a deterministic token design framework that encodes carrying costs transparently into on-chain logic. The FRS introduces an asset-per-token variable q(t) that decreases according to a predefined annualized carrying cost rate, coupled with a supply reconciliation mechanism that preserves holder balances and ERC-20 composability. While mathematically inspired by the daily expense ratio accrual in traditional asset management -- which often embed centralized profit margins -- the FRS design specifically encodes actual operational carrying costs to provide pure institutional-grade accounting clarity without compromising DeFi compatibility. The framework is asset-agnostic and applicable to any real-world asset with positive, predictable holding costs.

</details>


### [177] [DroidBreaker: Practical and Functional Problem-Space Attacks on Machine-Learning Android Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26707)
*Christian Scano,Diego Soi,Angelo Sotgiu,Luca Demetrio,Davide Maiorca,Giorgio Giacinto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DROIDBREAKER，一种实用且保持语义的对抗性APK生成框架，通过细粒度、构建安全的操作（API调用注入与混淆、权限和URL修改）及运行时等价性测试，在白盒和黑盒设置下以少量查询实现高逃避率，并显著降低VirusTotal上商业检测器的检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对Android恶意软件检测器的对抗样本生成方法大多不实用：粗粒度注入引入大量副作用特征且易构建失败，细粒度方法效果有限，依赖字节码重写的混淆技术生成语法有效但语义不可用的APK，且先前工作仅通过安装和基本执行的冒烟测试高估攻击成功率，未评估修改后APK是否保持预期行为。

Method: DROIDBREAKER包含三个核心组件：(i) 查询高效的白盒与黑盒攻击，仅操纵对目标模型最关键的APK组件；(ii) 一组细粒度、构建安全的操作（API调用注入与混淆、应用模块注入、权限和URL修改），最小化副作用；(iii) 语义保持的功能测试，通过比较初始APK与修改后APK的执行日志和API级轨迹来强制执行运行时等价性。

Result: 在最新Android应用语料上，DROIDBREAKER在白盒和黑盒设置下均以少量查询和最小副作用实现高逃避率，并显著降低VirusTotal上商业恶意软件扫描器的检测率。

Conclusion: DROIDBREAKER克服了现有问题空间对抗攻击的实用性不足和语义不可用问题，证明了构建真正可部署的对抗性APK是可行的，同时揭示了当前基于机器学习的Android恶意软件检测器的脆弱性。

Abstract: Adversarial APKs are Android applications modified in the problem space to evade machine-learning malware detectors. In this work, we first show that, despite claims, existing problem-space attacks remain largely impractical. Most techniques leverage software transplantation to inject entire benign modules, introducing many side-effect features and often causing build-time failures. Fine-grained methods that inject only a narrow subset of components exhibit limited effectiveness, while those that also use obfuscation rely on brittle bytecode rewriting, producing APKs that are syntactically valid but semantically unusable. Prior work further overestimates attack success rates by running smoke tests that only validate installation and basic execution, without assessing whether the modified APK still preserves its intended behavior. To overcome these limitations, we present DROIDBREAKER, a practical (build-safe) and functional (semantics-preserving) problem-space attack framework that provides: (i) query-efficient white- and black-box attacks by manipulating only the APK components most influential to the target model; (ii) a set of fine-grained, build-safe manipulations (including injection and obfuscation of API calls, app modules, permissions, and URLs) with minimal side effects; and (iii) a semantics-preserving functionality test that enforces runtime equivalence by comparing execution logs and API-level traces between the initial and the modified APK. Evaluated on a recent corpus of Android applications, DROIDBREAKER achieves high evasion rates with few queries and minimal side effects in both white-box and black-box settings, and drastically reduces detections by commercial malware scanners hosted on VirusTotal.

</details>


### [178] [MergeLLL: A Hierarchical Divide-and-Conquer Framework for LLL-Based Lattice Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26784)
*Niharika Gauraha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MergeLLL提出了一种基于分治策略的格基约简算法，通过将格基拆分为子基、独立局部约简和层次合并，实现了比经典LLL算法更好的正交性、更少的交换操作和更优的Hermite因子，且天然可并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典格基约简算法（如LLL）在高维情况下计算复杂度急剧增加，亟需一种能够降低计算成本、支持并行化且保持约简质量的方法。

Method: 借鉴归并排序的分治策略，将格基拆分为子基进行独立局部约简，然后在层次合并过程中引入PotLLL风格的深度插入操作，通过幺模变换保持格结构，实现对数级并行深度。

Result: 在子集和问题与NTRU派生格上的实验表明，MergeLLL在正交性、昂贵交换操作次数和Hermite因子方面均优于经典格基约简算法。

Conclusion: MergeLLL通过先改善局部格结构再优化全局性质的方式，有效降低了计算成本，提高了数值稳定性，且天然支持多核与分布式执行，是一种高质量的格基约简方法。

Abstract: Lattice basis reduction algorithms have various applications in computational number theory and lattice-based cryptography, but their complexity increases rapidly with the dimension. Motivated by the divide-and-conquer strategy of merge sort and incorporating PotLLL-style deep insertions during recombination, MergeLLL is proposed. In this framework, a lattice basis is split into sub-bases, local reductions are performed independently, and the full basis is reconstructed through hierarchical merging.
  The approach is focused on improving local lattice structure first before global basis properties are refined, resulting in enhanced Gram-Schmidt orthogonality and numerical stability, while overall computational cost is reduced. The method is naturally parallelizable, allowing efficient multicore and distributed execution. It is shown that the reduction and merging steps preserve the lattice structure through unimodular transformations and achieve logarithmic parallel depth. In experiments on subset-sum and NTRU-derived lattices, improvements over classical lattice reduction algorithms are demonstrated, including better orthogonality, a reduced number of expensive swap operations, and an improved Hermite factor, indicating higher-quality reduced bases.

</details>


### [179] [MIRROR: Novelty-Constrained Memory-Guided MCTS Red-Teaming for Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26793)
*Inderjeet Singh,Andrés Murillo,Motoyoshi Sekiya,Yuki Unno,Junichi Suga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIRROR是一个统一的多表面红队框架，采用记忆引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索与新颖性约束机制，在多模态RAG系统的四个攻击面上均取得优于基线的攻击成功率，并发布了包含4万+记录的ART-SafeBench基准测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态RAG系统的攻击面已从提示注入扩展到文本投毒、图像注入、直接查询攻击和编排器工具操纵，但现有红队方法通常是表面特化的且常复用已知攻击模板（文本投毒基准中73-84%为完全重复），缺乏统一的多表面攻击框架。

Method: 提出MIRROR框架，核心包括：1）记忆引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）；2）基于检索上下文的条件候选生成；3）显式新颖性约束（Novelty Gate）——通过归一化比较拒绝与检索集匹配的候选，允许检索信息指导搜索先验但不允许提示复制。

Result: 在四个攻击面上：图像投毒ASR 76%（基线52%），编排器攻击ASR 97%且查询成本减半，跨表面变异系数最低（0.47）；基线方法跨表面失效（如后缀优化文本投毒ASR 79%但直接查询仅1%）。发布ART-SafeBench包含41,815条包内记录及运行时适配器，总计41,991+条记录。

Conclusion: MIRROR作为统一的多表面红队框架，通过新颖性约束和记忆引导搜索有效克服了现有方法的表面特化与攻击模板重复问题，在多模态RAG系统的安全评估中展现出更优的泛化能力和攻击效率。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems expand the attack surface beyond prompt injection to include text poisoning, image injection, direct-query attacks, and orchestrator-level tool manipulation. Existing red-teaming approaches are typically surface-specific and often recycle known attack templates; on text-poisoning benchmarks we measure 73-84% exact duplication. We present MIRROR, a unified cross-surface framework that performs memory-guided Monte Carlo tree search while conditioning candidate generation on retrieved context under an explicit novelty constraint. A deterministic Novelty Gate rejects any candidate matching the retrieval set under normalized comparison, allowing retrieval to inform search priors without enabling prompt copying. Across four attack surfaces on a multimodal agentic RAG target, MIRROR attains 76% ASR on image poisoning compared with 52% for baselines, 97% ASR on orchestrator attacks at half the query cost, and the lowest cross-surface variance (coefficient of variation 0.47). In contrast, specialized baselines collapse across surfaces: suffix optimization reaches 79% ASR on text poisoning but 1% on direct queries. We release ART-SafeBench with 41,815 in-package records and runtime adapters yielding 41,991+ total records across four surfaces.

</details>


### [180] [SpikeTimer: Exploring Active Copyright Protection in Spiking Neural Networks via Temporal Backdoor Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26841)
*Xiao Yang,Gaolei Li,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Zhiquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikeTimer，一种基于时间反向门控学习的SNN主动版权保护框架，通过将神经形态数据划分为时间片并仅在授权时间片内嵌入授权令牌实现版权保护，支持多用户授权机制，在多个数据集上实现了约10%的未授权数据准确率和仅1.5%的授权输入性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在节能计算和事件驱动实时应用中展现出卓越能力，但其版权保护由于时间编码复杂性和脉冲驱动计算的特性而严重缺乏研究，不同于DNN有大量版权保护解决方案。

Method: 提出SpikeTimer框架，通过时间反向门控学习实现版权保护：将神经形态数据划分为指定时间片，仅在授权时间片内嵌入授权令牌；利用时间分段特性支持多用户授权和任意形态令牌嵌入；对包含正确时间片内令牌的授权数据产生正确响应，对未授权数据产生错误响应。

Result: 在多个神经形态数据集上，SpikeTimer在未授权数据上实现约10%的准确率，授权输入性能仅下降约1.5%，并展现出对模型微调和剪枝攻击的鲁棒抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 建立了时间依赖的授权机制，通过时间令牌有效性保护SNN版权，即使在对抗性尝试下仍能保持防御效果，为SNN版权保护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNN) have emerged as a revolutionary paradigm compared to traditional Deep Neural Networks (DNN) in energy-efficient computing, showcasing exceptional capabilities in processing event-driven sensory data for real-time applications like robotics and edge AI systems. However, unlike extensive studies on DNN copyright solutions, SNN copyright protection remains largely underexplored due to their inherent temporal coding complexities and spike-driven computation. In this study, we propose a novel active copyright protection framework named SpikeTimer for SNNs via temporal backdoor learning. SpikeTimer partitions neuromorphic data into designated timeslices and exclusively embeds authorized tokens within authorized slices. Furthermore, the inherent temporal segmentation characteristic intrinsically enables SpikeTimer to support multi-user authorization mechanisms and accommodates token embedding of arbitrary morphology. Based on this, SpikeTimer precisely responds to authorized data containing a token within the correct timeslice, while producing erroneous responses to unauthorized data. Our key innovation lies in establishing a time-dependent authorization mechanism that protects the SNN copyright by temporal token validity. Additionally, SpikeTimer retains its defensive efficacy even under adversarial attempts. Evaluations on multiple neuromorphic datasets manifest that SpikeTimer achieves around 10% accuracy on unauthorized data with merely around 1.5% degradation on authorized inputs. Moreover, SpikeTimer demonstrates robust resistance against model finetuning and pruning threats.

</details>


### [181] [Fortress and Gatekeeper: Theorizing Transitive Trust in Third-Party Cybersecurity Risk Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26866)
*Yijun Chen,Misita Anwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2025年OpenAI-Mixpanel安全事件，探讨第三方供应商安全事件如何成为面向客户组织的治理与问责问题，提出“传递性信任”概念以及“堡垒与守门人”框架，解释网络安全治理边界应基于信任与数据流而非正式组织所有权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方供应商（如分析平台、云服务、身份提供商等）日益嵌入数字服务交付，但客户数据和安全实践进入客户难以查看、选择或评估的环境。供应商安全事件会转化为与客户保持关系的焦点组织的治理与问责问题，现有研究对此缺乏系统解释。

Method: 采用文档分析法，对2025年11月发生的OpenAI-Mixpanel安全事件进行案例研究，并结合组织信任研究与代理理论进行理论构建。

Result: 提出了“传递性信任”概念（客户对数字服务的信任依赖于该服务提供商授权的供应商的安全实践），以及“堡垒与守门人”框架（基于信任与数据流而非正式组织所有权解释网络安全治理边界），并推导出关于供应商集成、元数据暴露、供应商保证和数据扩散的四项命题。

Conclusion: 本文为网络安全治理学术研究做出贡献，解释了委托数据处理如何产生面向客户的问责问题，并识别了对供应商分层、数据分类、合同设计、持续保证和数据最小化的实践启示。

Abstract: Third-party vendors, such as analytics platforms, cloud services, identity providers, and software suppliers, are increasingly embedded in digital service delivery. While these arrangements enable scale and specialization, they also move customer data and security-relevant practices into environments that customers rarely see, select, or evaluate. This paper examines this problem through a document analysis of the November 2025 OpenAI-Mixpanel security incident. The incident serves as an illustrative case for showing how a security event in a vendor environment can become a governance and accountability problem for the focal organization that maintains the customer relationship. Drawing on organizational trust research and agency theory, the paper argues that third-party cybersecurity risk is both a trust relationship and a delegation problem. Customers trust the visible service provider, while the provider relies on vendors whose security practices are only partially visible and controllable. The paper develops the concept of transitive trust, where customer trust in a digital service depends on the security practices of vendors authorized by that service provider. It then presents the Fortress and Gatekeeper framework, which explains cybersecurity governance boundaries through trust and data flows rather than formal organizational ownership alone. The analysis develops four propositions concerning vendor integration, metadata exposure, vendor assurance, and data proliferation. The paper contributes to cybersecurity governance scholarship by explaining how delegated data processing creates customer-facing accountability and by identifying implications for vendor tiering, data classification, contractual design, continuous assurance, and data minimization.

</details>


### [182] [Chai: Agentic Discovery of Cryptographic Misuse Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26933)
*Corban Villa,Sohee Kim,Austin Chu,Alon Shakevsky,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Chai是一个基于AI的系统，通过重新构想差分测试范式，利用自然信号发现和验证密码学误用漏洞，在X.509、JWT和SAML库中发现了超过100个漏洞，包括影响数十亿设备的SSL库中的关键漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多危险漏洞类别（如密码学误用）缺乏类似内存安全检测中的插桩机制，导致无法有效过滤误报和发现真实漏洞。

Method: Chai重新构想差分测试范式，利用AI提高检测库中真实安全问题的精度，并将常被忽视的差异重新利用为下游应用中具体漏洞的线索。该方法颠覆了传统AI漏洞发现范式：从审计单个代码库的多个缺陷，转为在库级别编录缺陷并通过密码学依赖图传播，实现复合效率增益。

Result: 在X.509、JWT和SAML库的评估中，Chai发现了一个影响数十亿设备的SSL库中先前未知的关键漏洞，以及一个主要Web浏览器底层库和主流Linux发行版中库的安全漏洞，总计发现超过100个漏洞。

Conclusion: Chai通过AI增强的差分测试方法，成功将密码学误用漏洞的发现从库级别推广到下游应用，证明了该范式在缺乏插桩支持的漏洞类别中的有效性。

Abstract: AI-assisted vulnerability discovery has proven effective for bug classes like memory safety, where instrumentation confirms memory violations and efficiently filters false positives. Many dangerous vulnerability classes, such as cryptographic misuse, however, lack any comparable instrumentation. In this work, we present Chai, an AI-based system that discovers and validates cryptographic misuse vulnerabilities through naturally occurring signals. To achieve this, Chai rethinks the classical technique of differential testing by leveraging AI to 1) improve precision for detecting real security issues in libraries, and 2) repurpose commonly overlooked discrepancies as leads for tangible vulnerabilities in downstream applications. In doing so, Chai inverts the prevailing paradigm of AI vulnerability discovery: instead of auditing one codebase for many flaws, it catalogs flaws at the library level and propagates them across a cryptographic dependency graph, delivering compounding efficiency gains. We evaluate Chai across X.509, JWT, and SAML libraries. Chai discovered a previously unknown critical vulnerability in an SSL library that powers billions of devices, along with security bugs in one library behind a major web browser and another in major Linux distributions.
