In total, these techniques surfaced over 100 vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [183] [Jailbreaking for the Average Jane: Choosing Optimal Jailbreaks via Bandit Algorithms for Automatically Enhanced Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26936)
*Prarabdh Shukla,Ritik,Suhas Rao,Arpit Agarwal,Arjun Bhagoji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究非专业恶意用户是否能够利用现成的越狱方法对LLM发起有效攻击。作者提出基于多臂老虎机框架的在线学习攻击策略，并构建了包含11,279个恶意查询的FrankensteinBench基准测试集。实验表明，该攻击方法在15个开源LLM上平均成功率达97%，且增加查询复杂度可使成功率平均提升26%。结论证实了安全担忧的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证非专业恶意行为者（"普通用户"）是否能够利用现有的越狱方法对LLM发起成功的攻击，从而引发实际安全风险。

Method: 1) 提出基于多臂老虎机（Multi-Armed Bandit）框架的在线攻击策略，通过少量查询的噪声探索高效学习最优越狱方法，并在后续利用集上应用学习到的策略。2) 构建FrankensteinBench基准测试集，包含11,279个恶意查询，涵盖7个现有基准的自动扩充与生成，并按技术复杂度分为简单和复杂两类。

Result: 1) 基于多臂老虎机的攻击在15个最先进开源LLM上平均成功率达97%。2) 增加查询复杂度可使攻击成功率平均提升26%，表明这是一种有效且可自动化的提示策略。

Conclusion: 非专业恶意用户确实能够对LLM构成严重安全威胁，现有安全防护措施不足以应对此类攻击。

Abstract: With a profusion of jailbreaks for LLMs now widely known, a growing concern is that non-expert malicious actors ("the average Jane") could elicit actionable responses to malicious requests. In this work, we examine whether this concern is justified. A non-expert malicious actor requires two ingredients for a successful attack: a powerful jailbreak for their target model, acting on an effective malicious query. For the former, we propose a novel attack strategy based on the multi-armed bandit framework. This allows efficient online learning of the optimal jailbreak from a large choice set via noisy exploration on a small number of queries, with subsequent application of the learnt policy on an exploitation set. For the latter, we curate $\mathrm{FrankensteinBench}$, a safety benchmark of $11,279$ malicious queries drawn from manual curation over $7$ existing benchmarks, along with automated enhancement and generation. Each query is categorized as simple or complex by the technical expertise required to craft it. Our findings confirm the concern. Our bandit-based attack achieves success rates as high as $97\%$ on average over $15$ SoTA open-weight LLMs. Moreover, adding complexity to queries raises the attack success rate by up to $26\%$ on average across models -- making it an effective, automatable prompting strategy.

</details>


### [184] [Protocol Prying: Systematic Vulnerability Research in the Apple AirDrop and Android Quick Share Proximity Transfer Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26967)
*Arash Ale Ebrahim,Nils Ole Tippenhauer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对Apple AirDrop和Google/Samsung Quick Share两大近场文件传输协议进行跨平台逆向工程与协议感知模糊测试，发现了6个零点击漏洞（V1-V6），包括macOS/iOS AirDrop的3个预认证问题、Samsung Quick Share的2个协议层缺陷以及Google Quick Share for Windows的1个堆释放后重用漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两大近场文件传输协议（AirDrop和Quick Share）覆盖超50亿设备，但因其私有且无文档的应用层安全特性尚未被系统性研究，且协议无需预配对即可从无线近场访问、在特权守护进程中处理复杂序列化内容，构成多操作系统下的零点击攻击面。

Method: 采用跨平台逆向工程与协议感知模糊测试方法：重建AirDrop七层状态机和DVZip自适应压缩；构建协议感知模糊器AIRFUZZ（对压缩前表示进行变异）；辅以针对Samsung Quick Share服务和Google Quick Share for Windows的手工分析。

Result: 发现6个漏洞：V1-Swift fatalError DoS（AirDrop HTTP路径路由）、V2-无界XML plist递归（Foundation）、V3-NULL解引用（Network.framework HTTP/1.1解析器）、V4-预认证OfflineFrame分发、V5-D2D加密绕过（三种帧类型）、V6-堆释放后重用（Google Quick Share for Windows，已获谷歌奖励）。

Conclusion: 所有漏洞均已负责任的披露，Apple、Samsung和Google已确认报告，表明即使广泛部署的私有协议仍需系统性安全审计。

Abstract: Apple AirDrop and Google/Samsung Quick Share are proximity file-transfer protocols used by over five billion devices, yet their application-layer security properties remain largely unstudied because both stacks are proprietary and undocumented. Both protocols are reachable from wireless proximity without any prior pairing and process complex serialized content (binary plists, CPIO archives, Protocol Buffers, UKEY2 handshakes) inside privileged daemons, making them attractive zero-click targets across multiple operating systems. We perform the first cross-platform reverse engineering and protocol-aware fuzzing study of both stacks. We reconstruct AirDrop's seven-layer state machine and DVZip adaptive compression from binary analysis, build AIRFUZZ, a protocol-aware fuzzer that mutates pre-compression representations, and complement it with targeted hand-written analyses of Samsung's Quick Share service and Google's Quick Share for Windows. We discover six vulnerabilities (V1-V6): three pre-authentication issues in macOS/iOS AirDrop (V1: Swift fatalError DoS in the HTTP path router; V2: unbounded XML plist recursion in Foundation; V3: NULL dereference in Network.framework's HTTP/1.1 parser), two protocol-layer flaws in Samsung Quick Share (V4: pre-authentication OfflineFrame dispatch; V5: D2D encryption bypass for three frame types), and a heap use-after-free in Google Quick Share for Windows (V6) for which Google awarded a bounty. We responsibly disclosed all findings, and Apple, Samsung, and Google have acknowledged the reports.

</details>


### [185] [ShareLock: A Stealthy Multi-Tool Threshold Poisoning Attack Against MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27027)
*Liwei Liu,Tianzhu Han,Zijian Liu,Zishu Dong,Na Ruan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ShareLock，一种基于Shamir阈值方案的多工具投毒框架，将恶意指令分割为无害的秘密共享片段分布在多个工具描述中，实现高隐蔽性和容错性，在主流LLM上攻击成功率超90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP协议下的工具投毒攻击（TPA）采用单一明文嵌入范式，无法抵御人工审查或自动检测；且缺乏对多工具协同投毒的系统性分析。需要一种更隐蔽、更具容错性的多工具协同投毒方案。

Method: 基于Shamir阈值方案，将恶意指令分割为多个秘密共享片段，分布嵌入到多个工具描述中；在服务器更新时植入隐蔽重建触发器，聚合片段后还原隐藏指令。

Result: 在四种多工具场景的基准测试中，ShareLock在基于工具描述的检测上显著优于现有单工具投毒策略，平均攻击成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: ShareLock实现了信息论级别的保密性和对适度审查的鲁棒性，有效揭示了MCP生态中多工具协同投毒的新安全威胁。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of LLM-driven agents, Model Context Protocol (MCP), an open protocol bridging LLMs with external tools, has quickly become foundational to modern agent ecosystems. However, the expanding adoption of MCP has also introduced novel security concerns such as Tool Poisoning Attack (TPA), which exploit LLM-server interactions to inject malicious prompts. Existing poisoning schemes typically adopt a monolithic plaintext embedding paradigm, which fails to withstand manual inspection or automated detectors. Current research still lacks a systematic analysis on multi-tool poisoning, where multiple tools can be exploited cooperatively to disperse detection risk. In this paper, we introduce ShareLock, a multi-tool threshold poisoning framework that utilizes Shamir's threshold scheme to ensure exceptional stealth and fault tolerance. ShareLock distributes the malicious instruction as benign-looking secret shares across multiple tool descriptions, achieving both information-theoretic secrecy and attack robustness against moderate auditing. After a covert reconstruction trigger is planted during server update, the aggregated shares reconstruct the hidden instruction, resulting in critical breaches of system assets or private data. To evaluate the realistic threat of ShareLock, we constructed a comprehensive benchmark encompassing four multi-tool scenarios and conducted extensive experiments across mainstream LLMs on two distinct MCP clients. Our results demonstrate that ShareLock significantly outperforms existing single-tool poisoning strategies in tool description-based detection while maintaining an average attack success rate exceeding 90%.

</details>


### [186] [Design and Performance Evaluation of Secure RF and WiFi-Based Communication in Drone Swarms via Testbed Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27028)
*Bhavya Dixit,Aayushi Rajgor,Subham Kumar,Rushikesh Patil,Ananthapadmanabhan A.,Gaurav S. Kasbekar,Arnab Maity*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MAVShield轻量级加密框架，用于无人机群MAVLink通信加密，并在四架自制无人机上通过RF和WiFi信道实验验证其性能，结果表明MAVShield在效率上优于AES-CTR、Speck-CTR、ChaCha20和Rabbit，且能抵抗密钥恢复攻击，保障遥测数据机密性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机群依赖RF和WiFi等无线通信技术，但MAVLink协议缺乏内置加密，遥测流量易受窃听攻击，现有加密方案在资源受限的无人机上性能开销过大，亟需轻量级且安全的加密方案。

Method: 将MAVShield、AES-CTR、Speck-CTR、ChaCha20、Rabbit四种加密方案集成到四架自制无人机中，通过RF和WiFi信道建立安全通信链路；开发基于大地坐标系的改进人工势场(APF)算法用于碰撞避免；在无人机群试验平台上进行飞行实验，测量CPU利用率、内存消耗和PDR等性能指标，并进行代数密码分析和Wireshark流量分析。

Result: MAVShield性能与未加密通信相当，整体效率优于AES-CTR、Speck-CTR、ChaCha20和Rabbit；改进的APF算法消除了笛卡尔坐标变换，减少了轨迹振荡并避免了局部极小值陷阱；密码分析表明MAVShield能抵抗密钥恢复攻击，Wireshark分析证实了遥测数据机密性保护。

Conclusion: MAVShield是一种高效且安全的无人机群通信加密方案，适用于资源受限的无人机平台，可保障自主编队控制与碰撞避免过程中的通信安全。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms rely on distributed coordination and cooperative communication to support scalable operations, extended coverage, and applications such as surveillance and real-time data exchange. Wireless technologies such as radio frequency (RF) and WiFi are widely used for UAV-to-UAV and UAV-to-ground control station (GCS) communication but introduce significant security challenges. MAVLink, the predominant communication protocol in UAV systems, provides message integrity and authentication but lacks built-in encryption, leaving telemetry traffic vulnerable to eavesdropping. In our previous work, we proposed MAVShield, a lightweight encryption framework for MAVLink communications. In this paper, MAVShield, AES-CTR, Speck-CTR, ChaCha20, and Rabbit are integrated into four custom-built UAVs to establish secure communication links over RF and WiFi channels. Their performance is evaluated through flight experiments using a UAV swarm testbed. Encrypted telemetry data enable autonomous formation control and collision avoidance during flight. For collision avoidance, we develop a modified artificial potential field (APF) algorithm that computes attractive and repulsive forces directly in geodetic coordinates, eliminating Cartesian transformations and reducing trajectory oscillations while avoiding local-minimum trapping. CPU utilization, memory consumption, and packet delivery ratio (PDR) are measured for each encryption scheme. Results show that MAVShield achieves performance comparable to unencrypted communication while outperforming AES-CTR, Speck-CTR, ChaCha20, and Rabbit in overall efficiency. Algebraic cryptanalysis and Wireshark-based traffic analysis demonstrate resistance to key-recovery attacks and protection of telemetry confidentiality. The results indicate that MAVShield is an efficient and secure solution for UAV swarm communication.

</details>


### [187] [Physical Layer Authentication With Channel Knowledge Maps in Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27044)
*Luca Bonaventura,Francesco Ardizzon,Stefano Tomasin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于室内移动设备的物理层认证机制，利用多个接入点估计的主信道路径损耗和到达角，与预先构建的信道知识地图进行比对，在设备移动和室内多径环境下实现有效认证，并通过随机和最优攻击下的安全分析验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理层认证方法依赖信道测量的时间一致性或其演化模型，但在室内移动设备场景下，多径传播和遮挡导致这些假设不再成立。

Method: 利用多个接入点估计主信道路径损耗和到达角，将其与预先建立的信道知识地图中邻近位置的测量值进行比对，从而实现认证。

Result: 在代表性室内场景中，通过射线追踪获得信道知识地图进行数值实验，验证了所提方法的有效性；在随机攻击和最优攻击下均进行了全面的安全分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于信道知识地图的物理层认证机制能够有效应对室内移动设备的认证挑战，具有良好的安全性能。

Abstract: Physical layer authentication (PLA) allows to authenticate the user by comparing measurements over time, assuming their time consistency or by modeling their evolution. However, these assumptions become problematic when devices are in motion and in indoor environments due to multipath propagation and obstructions. In this paper, we propose a PLA mechanism for moving devices in indoor environments, where multiple access points (APs) estimate the dominant channel tap path loss (PL) and angle of arrival (AoA) from the received signals and compare them with previously collected channel knowledge maps (CKMs). Specifically, the measurements are compared to those in the neighborhood of the previously known position obtained from CKMs. A comprehensive security analysis is conducted under both random and optimal attacks. Numerical results in a representative indoor scenario, with CKM obtained via ray tracing, validate the effectiveness of the proposed PLA approach.

</details>


### [188] [Type-based information flow analysis for $π$-calculus with a dynamically extensible security lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27059)
*Yukihiro Oda,Eijiro Sumii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持动态创建和插入安全级别的类型系统，通过扩展Kobayashi基于π演算的类型安全信息流分析，将安全格从简单的二元格推广到动态可扩展格，并形式化地保证了保密性作为非干扰性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于类型系统的安全信息流分析通常假设安全格是静态固定的，无法适应需要在程序执行过程中动态创建新安全级别的场景，因此需要一种支持动态安全格扩展的类型系统。

Method: 以Milner的π演算为基础，扩展Kobayashi的类型化安全信息流分析方法，将安全格从2元素格（High和Low）推广为可动态扩展的一般格结构，并小心处理格扩展本身及其对非干扰性证明的影响。

Result: 成功构建了一个支持动态安全级别创建与插入的类型系统，并在π演算这一表达力极强的模型上形式化了保密性作为非干扰性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在高度表达性的并发计算模型（π演算）中，通过类型系统可以处理动态安全格的扩展，同时保持安全信息流分析的正确性。

Abstract: We develop a type system for secure information flow where new security levels can be created and inserted into the security lattice dynamically, i.e., even in the middle of an execution of a system. Our system is formalized by extending Kobayashi's type-based secure information flow analysis for Milner's pi-calculus, which is one of the most expressive models (or "languages") supporting both sequential and concurrent computations, with concise syntax, reduction-based semantics, and bisimulation equivalence as a robust formalization of secrecy as non-interference. The development required careful treatment of extensions of lattices themselves as well as deliberate generalization from the simple 2-element lattice (consisting of only High and Low) in the original system.

</details>


### [189] [Inherited Circuits, Learned Semantics: How Fine-Tuning Creates Evasion Vulnerabilities Invisible to Standard Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27091)
*Ryan Fetterman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 安全微调可能会引入新的漏洞：模型学习基于令牌的指示器语义，在标准评估中表现良好，但在行为保持变换（如别名替换、命令重构等）下容易受到攻击。研究表明Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct存在这类脆弱性，而基座模型Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct则没有。本文还提出了部署前的监测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示安全微调本身可能引入的漏洞——模型学习脆弱的指示器规则，在标准分布内测试表现正常，但在变换攻击下失效，从而质疑当前仅基于分布内指标评估安全微调模型的做法。

Method: 1) 使用匹配的PowerShell分类队列对比Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct与Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct；2) 因果干预定位分类电路；3) 构建三层逃避基准测试（别名替换、命令重构、字符串构造、执行间接、大小写变异）；4) 提出线性探针和指示器令牌符号检验作为部署前监测方法。

Result: Foundation-Sec在iwr替换、Invoke-Expression重构和大小写变异的IEX/IEX变体上存在漏检，而Llama无此问题。微调集中并语义特化了继承自Llama的晚注意力路径结构，在改善基线行为的同时产生了变换敏感的受攻击面。

Conclusion: 安全微调可能通过特化继承的模型结构形成脆弱的指示器规则，在保持留出集准确率的同时扩大逃避攻击面。不应将小规模任务特定微调视为更安全的安全分类器；鲁棒的AI安全需要定义完整变换空间并监测微调过程中的语义漂移。

Abstract: LLMs fine-tuned for security classification are usually evaluated on held-out examples from the same distribution as their training data. We show that this can miss vulnerabilities introduced by fine-tuning itself: models can learn token-level indicator semantics that preserve canonical accuracy while failing under behavior-preserving transformations such as PowerShell alias substitution, command reconstruction, string construction, execution indirection, and case mutation. We study Foundation-Sec-8B-Instruct and its base model, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, on matched PowerShell classification cohorts. Causal interventions localize the classification circuit to a late-attention route inherited from Llama rather than created by fine-tuning. Fine-tuning concentrates and semantically specializes this inherited structure, improving baseline behavior while creating transformation-sensitive attack surfaces. A three-tier evasion benchmark finds Foundation-Sec misses on iwr substitution, Invoke-Expression reconstruction, and case-mutated Invoke-Expression/IEX variants that Llama does not share. We also derive a pre-deployment monitoring method: a linear probe at the classification boundary and an indicator-token sign test identify command families where canonical indicators change role after fine-tuning. These signals prioritize red-team variant generation using only canonical inputs, showing that security fine-tuning can improve task accuracy while expanding the evasion surface. These results caution against treating small task-specific fine-tunes as straightforwardly safer security classifiers: specialization can convert inherited model structure into brittle indicator rules that preserve held-out accuracy while expanding the evasion surface. Robust AI-enabled security will require specifying the full transformation space of the task and monitoring semantic drift through fine-tuning.

</details>


### [190] [zQR: A Verifiable QR-Driven zkSNARK Proof Verification Framework for Mobile Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27092)
*Goshgar Can Ismayilov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于QR码的zkSNARK证明验证框架zQR，将零知识证明编码为QR码以实现离线验证，结合区块链实现审计与不可否认性，利用大语言模型自动生成电路。实验表明该概念验证框架在移动平台上的可行性，使用版本19、低纠错级别的QR码紧凑表示证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护技术在实际日常交互中的采用仍然有限，零知识证明虽提供强隐私保障但技术复杂性高。作者希望降低使用门槛，使隐私保护验证在移动平台上更易落地。

Method: 1) 提出可验证QR码的核心思想，将zkSNARK证明编码为QR码供离线验证；2) 构建zQR框架，集成区块链实现审计、不可否认性和日志记录；3) 集成大语言模型实现自动电路生成；4) 进行安全性讨论，考虑多攻击面；5) 实验评估时间成本（证明生成/验证延迟、QR编解码延迟）和财务成本（区块链Gas消耗）。

Result: zQR作为概念验证框架在移动平台上可行，证明可紧凑地以版本19、低纠错级别的QR码表示。实验测量了证明生成/验证延迟、QR编解码延迟及区块链Gas消耗等指标。

Conclusion: zQR证明了在移动平台上通过QR码实现隐私保护验证的可行性，但仍有局限性，未来需进一步推动隐私保护技术在日常交互中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Privacy is one of the fundamental rights of individuals in modern societies. Yet, the practical adoption of privacy-preserving technologies in daily interactions remains limited. Zero-knowledge proofs offer strong privacy guarantees but are often hindered by their technical complexity. In this paper, we advance the idea of verifiable QR codes that enable off-line verifiers to verify proofs encoded in QR codes. Based on this core idea, we build a novel QR-driven zkSNARK proof verification framework (i.e., zQR) for mobile platforms. The framework integrates blockchain for auditability, non-repudiation and logging; and large-language models for automatic circuit generation. We perform a security discussion of the framework by considering multiple attack surfaces. Furthermore, we present an experimental evaluation measuring temporal costs (proof generation and verification latency, QR code encoding and decoding latency) and financial costs (blockchain gas consumption). Our results demonstrate the feasibility of zQR as a proof-of-concept framework for privacy-preserving verification on mobile platform where proofs are compactly represented with QR code symbol version of 19 with low error correction level. Finally, we discuss potential applications, current limitations and future directions for the broader adoption of privacy-preserving technologies in daily interactions.

</details>


### [191] [Application of LLMs to Threat Assessment of Foreign Peacekeeping Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27106)
*Gerhard Backfried,Christian Schmidt,Diego Pilutti,Michael Suker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）、OSINT媒体采集和跨学科风险模型的方法，用于支持外国维和任务中的威胁评估。该方法以PINPOINT项目和欧盟驻格鲁吉亚监测团为案例，通过LLM从媒体文档中提取结构化威胁信息，并验证了自动结果与人工判断在威胁和任务相关性方面的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有威胁评估方法在处理大量非结构化OSINT媒体数据时效率低下，难以快速、准确地为维和任务提供可操作的威胁情报，因此需要引入LLM来辅助分析人员。

Method: 基于PINPOINT项目和EUMM Georgia案例，结合跨学科风险模型与OSINT媒体采集，设计了一套LLM驱动的威胁提取工作流：将媒体内容映射到任务相关威胁类别，提取结构化信息，并通过多个LLM处理步骤提升相关性和grounding。

Result: 从媒体文档中提取的威胁评估结果与人工判断在威胁相关性和任务相关性等核心维度上表现出高度一致性。

Conclusion: LLM为维和任务中的威胁评估提供了一种有前景的分析辅助方法，能够显著提升分析效率和准确性。

Abstract: We present a novel approach for applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to threat assessment in the context of foreign peacekeeping missions. Building on the PINPOINT project and its use case, the EU Monitoring Mission in Georgia, we combine an interdisciplinary risk-model with OSINT-based media collection and LLM-supported threat extraction. The proposed workflow maps media contents to mission-relevant threats, extracts structured information and applies several additional LLM-based processing steps to improve relevance and grounding. An evaluation of threats extracted from media documents shows high agreement between automatically generated results and human judgment for core aspects such as threat and mission relevance. These results indicate that LLMs provide a promising approach to support analysts in the context of peacekeeping missions.

</details>


### [192] [PRISM: PE Relational Inter-Section Matrix. A 2D Section-Aware Dataset for Static PE Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27109)
*José M. Sacristán,Ana I. González-Tablas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRISM是一个将Windows PE文件表示为二维矩阵（按文件顺序排列的节向量加全局摘要行）的开放数据集和特征表示方法，在1/6维度下几乎达到EMBER全量特征的二分类性能，并保留了更适合家族分类等任务的节间结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PE恶意软件检测基准（EMBER、BODMAS、SOREL-20M）将PE文件表示为扁平的一维特征向量，丢弃了节的排序信息和节之间的关系上下文，无法捕捉结构化的判别信息。

Method: 将每个PE二进制文件编码为二维矩阵，行对应按文件顺序排列的各个PE节，附加一个全局摘要行以保持与EMBER风格模型的兼容性；从四个恶意软件源和SOREL-20M良性软件构建数据集，经去重得到83,633个矩阵，家族过滤后得到49,204个样本（684个家族）；使用Fisher判别比、互信息和节间信息增益进行形式化可分离性分析。

Result: 在严格控制的样本匹配对比下，基于PRISM压缩表示的梯度提升分类器以约1/6的维度恢复了一维EMBER向量上相同分类器的几乎所有二分类检测性能；EMBER仅在极低假阳性率区间保持微小且一致的边际优势，在决策阈值下两者操作上不可区分。二分类任务已趋饱和，PRISM的结构化内容更适合家族分类等具有更大度量空间的任务。

Conclusion: PRISM作为一种保留PE节位置结构的开放数据集和特征表示，在几乎不损失二分类性能的前提下大幅降低维度，且其结构化信息为家族分类等更具挑战性的任务提供了支撑，相关资源已开源发布。

Abstract: We introduce PRISM (PE Relational Inter-Section Matrix), an open dataset and feature representation for static Windows PE malware detection. Existing benchmarks such as EMBER, BODMAS, and SOREL-20M represent each PE file as a flat one-dimensional feature vector, discarding the ordering of sections and the relational context between them. PRISM instead encodes every binary as a two-dimensional matrix whose rows are individual PE sections in file order, with a global summary row that preserves compatibility with EMBER-style models. We build the corpus from four malware sources (BODMAS, MalwareBazaar, VirusShare, and CAPE) together with SOREL-20M benign software, yielding 83,633 deduplicated matrices and a family-filtered analysis corpus of 49,204 samples across 684 malware families.
  A formal separability analysis (Fisher Discriminant Ratio, mutual information, and inter-section information gain) shows that the per-section positional structure carries discriminative information that flat representations cannot capture. Under a strictly controlled, sample-matched comparison, a gradient-boosted classifier on the compact PRISM representation recovers nearly all of the binary-detection performance of the same classifier on the much larger EMBER vector, at roughly one-sixth the dimensionality; EMBER retains only a small, consistent advantage confined to the extreme low-false-positive regime, the two being operationally indistinguishable at the decision threshold. We are explicit that this binary task is saturated, so the structural content PRISM preserves is reserved for tasks with greater metric headroom, such as family classification and architectures that exploit the 2D structure directly. The dataset, extraction library, trained models, and full analysis pipeline are released under CC BY-NC-SA and MIT licences.

</details>


### [193] [The Observer World: A Cryptographic Extension of Impagliazzo's Five Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27139)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出观察者层级（Observer Hierarchy）作为计算复杂性理论的新正交轴，扩展了Impagliazzo的五世界框架。研究发现存在无条件的复杂性坍塌（P^{O_prof}=NP^{O_prof}⊂P），表明观察盲区与计算困难性是独立的。论文定义了观察者世界W_O，对所有世界-观察者对进行分类，并引入参数化族W_O^ε来建模观察不变量的部分违反，同时该框架与热力学、量子及宇宙学物理信息限制相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Impagliazzo五世界框架隐含地假设所有参与方（包括对手）都能完整观察全部输入，即观察者始终处于O_top层级。这一假设过于理想化，且从未被明确陈述。作者旨在明确这一隐含假设并将其放宽，通过引入观察层级作为新的正交轴，揭示在有限观察条件下新的结构性现象。

Method: 引入观察者层级（Observer Hierarchy）作为与计算复杂性正交的第二轴，扩展五世界分类框架。通过理论分析证明P^{O_prof}=NP^{O_prof}⊂P的无条件坍塌结果，对所有世界-观察者对进行分类标识，并构建参数化族W_O^ε以建模部分违反情形。

Result: 发现观察盲区与计算困难性相互独立，在五世界中均无条件成立坍塌结果P^{O_prof}=NP^{O_prof}⊂P。完整分类了所有世界-观察者对，识别出标记单元(a)-(d)，并建立了与物理信息界限（热力学、量子、宇宙学）的接口。

Conclusion: 观察层级的引入揭示了传统五世界框架无法表达的结构性现象，表明观察能力与计算能力是独立的两条轴线。该框架不仅扩展了计算复杂性理论，还能与物理学中的信息限制理论建立联系，为理解计算与观察之间的深层关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: Impagliazzo's five worlds classify computational assumptions along a single axis, the existence of cryptographic primitives. All five worlds implicitly assume that every party, including the adversary, observes the full input, that the observer is always $O_{top}$. This assumption is so natural that it is never stated. This work makes it explicit and relaxes it by introducing a second, orthogonal axis, the observational axis, defined by the observer hierarchy introduced in previous work. Relaxing the assumption reveals structural phenomena, such as the collapse $P^{O_{prof}} = NP^{O_{prof}} \subset P$, that the five-world framework cannot express. We prove that this collapse holds unconditionally in all five worlds, showing that observational blindness and computational hardness are independent. We define the Observer World $W_O$, classify all world-observer pairs, identify the labeled cells (a)--(d), and introduce a parametric family $W_O^{\varepsilon}$ modelling partial violations of observational invariants. The framework also interfaces with physical information limits, including thermodynamic, quantum, and cosmological bounds.

</details>


### [194] [Tilikum: Transaction Fair Ordering on a DAG without Weak Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27250)
*Giulio Segalini,Yigit Çolakoğlu,Marko Putnik,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Tilikum，一种基于DAG的账本协议，通过中位数时间戳聚合实现公平排序，在完全阻止DAG特定重排序攻击的同时，吞吐量比现有方案最高提升39倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi应用易受重排序攻击导致BEV提取，虽然以太坊等线性区块链已有公平排序研究，但DAG共识协议在公平排序保护方面仍存在空白。

Method: Tilikum协议基于DAG结构，采用中位数时间戳聚合实现批次顺序公平性（batch order fairness），不依赖弱边（weak edges），同时保持低数据冗余和鲁棒垃圾回收机制。

Result: 在Rust实现中，与Narwhal/Tusk、Pompē、Themis和FairDAG等基线相比，Tilikum吞吐量最高提升39倍，并能完全阻止最先进的DAG特定重排序攻击。

Conclusion: Tilikum为DAG共识协议提供了有效的公平排序保障，兼顾安全性与高性能，填补了DAG系统中BEV防护的研究空白。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) applications rely heavily on the order in which transactions are executed, making them susceptible to reordering attacks that enable adversaries to extract Blockchain Extractable Value (BEV). While linear blockchain systems such as Ethereum have inspired extensive research into fair ordering mechanisms, DAG-based consensus protocols have remained largely unprotected despite their growing adoption for scalability and performance. In this paper, we introduce Tilikum, a DAG-based ledger protocol that ensures fair transaction ordering without relying on weak edges. Tilikum achieves ordering linearizability by leveraging median-based timestamp aggregation, or batch order fairness, while maintaining low data redundancy and robust garbage collection. We implemented Tilikum in Rust and evaluated it against representative baselines, namely Narwhal/Tusk, Pompē, Themis and FairDAG. Our results show that Tilikum achieves up to $39\times$ higher throughput than other fair-ordering baselines, while fully blocking state-of-the-art DAG-specific reordering attacks.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [The Role of Input Dimensionality in the Emergence and Targeted Control of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26207)
*Nasrin Malekzadeh Goradel,Niccolo Pancino,Yaser Gholizade Atani,Benedetta Tondi,Giovanni Bellettini,Mauro Barni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了输入维度在对抗样本产生与目标控制中的作用，通过理论分析和大量实验证明：高输入维度使得对抗样本更容易构造，且目标攻击与无目标攻击所需扰动之间的差距随维度增加而缩小。尽管高维几何是关键因素，但其根源究竟是高维几何与数据分布的相互作用还是深度网络架构特性，仍是一个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论工作尝试用高维几何性质解释深度神经网络的对抗脆弱性，但这些理论的假设缺乏系统性的实证检验。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究输入维度在对抗样本产生与目标控制中的实际作用。

Method: 1) 分析现有基于测度集中理论的理论框架的适用范围与局限性，揭示真实图像类表现出强经验局部化；2) 在跨越广泛输入维度层次化图像数据集上，对多种神经网络架构进行大规模实证评估；3) 提供理论论证表明高维几何意味着目标攻击相比无目标攻击仅需有限的额外扰动；4) 通过大量实验验证目标攻击与无目标攻击扰动差距随维度增加而缩小的趋势。

Result: 1) 对抗样本随输入维度增加而更易构造；2) 目标攻击与无目标攻击之间的扰动差距始终很小，且随维度增加进一步缩小；3) 真实图像类表现出超出理论假设的强经验局部化特性。

Conclusion: 高输入维度是影响对抗样本产生与目标控制的基础性因素，但其根本原因（高维几何与数据分布相互作用还是网络架构特性）尚待明确。

Abstract: Several theoretical works have tried to explain the adversarial vulnerability of deep neural networks through properties of high-dimensional geometry. However, the assumptions underlying these works are rarely examined empirically, and systematic evidence remains limited. In this work, we present a systematic study of the role of input dimensionality in both the emergence and the targeted control of adversarial examples. We first analyse the scope and limitations of existing theoretical frameworks based on concentration of measure, showing that real image classes exhibit strong empirical localization, beyond what such theories typically assume. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation across hierarchical image datasets spanning a wide range of input dimensionalities and diverse neural architectures. Our results consistently show that adversarial examples become easier to construct as dimensionality increases. We also investigate how input dimensionality affects the additional difficulty of crafting targeted adversarial examples. In particular, we provide theoretical arguments showing that high-dimensional geometry implies that enforcing a specific target label entails only a limited additional distortion compared to untargeted attacks. We corroborate this insight through extensive experiments, demonstrating that the gap between targeted and untargeted perturbations remains small and further narrows as input dimensionality increases. While, taken together, our findings establish high input dimensionality as a fundamental factor underlying the emergence and targeted control of adversarial examples, whether this phenomenon primarily arises from the interplay between high-dimensional geometry and data distributions or from the architectural properties of deep neural networks remains an open question.

</details>


### [196] [A probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26457)
*Daniel Corrales,David Ríos Insua*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于在线测试时自适应问题的概率框架，基于状态空间建模架构统一描述参数学习、时间演化、先验调优和预测过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法缺乏统一的概率视角，模型在训练与测试分布可能不同的情况下需要在线适应未标记数据，当前方法难以系统性地刻画参数学习、动态演化与预测之间的关系。

Method: 采用状态空间建模架构构建概率框架，将参数学习、参数的时间演化、先验调优以及预测纳入统一的概率模型中进行表征。

Result: 该框架为在线测试时自适应问题提供了统一的理论基础，能够有效应对训练与测试分布可能存在差异（分布偏移）的场景。

Conclusion: 基于状态空间的概率建模为在线测试时自适应提供了一种系统性的解决方案，能够统一处理参数学习、动态演化与不确定性量化等关键挑战。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation problems. In them, a model is trained on labeled data but must adapt to unlabeled data at test time under the assumption that training and test distributions potentially differ, that is, there might have been a distributional shift. The framework is based on a state-space modelling architecture from which parameter learning, parameter time evolution, prior tuning, and prediction can be characterized.

</details>


### [197] [XMSE-Aware Adaptive Empirical Bayes Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26975)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种XMSE感知的混合估计器，在经验贝叶斯（EB）收缩和最大似然估计（ML）之间进行自适应插值，当核函数与真实参数对齐良好时保留正则化优势，在核函数误设时退回到ML，并证明了其二阶遗憾界和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，基于核的经验贝叶斯估计在核函数与真实参数对齐不良时，其二阶风险（XMSE）可能比最大似然估计更差。本文旨在将这一诊断转化为设计原则，提出一种能够在EB和ML之间自适应权衡的混合估计方法。

Method: 提出XMSE感知的混合估计器，通过标量二次型构建固定权重XMSE，导出闭式oracle混合权重；基于有限样本XMSE近似实现plug-in估计并证明一致性；推导oracle权重的二阶遗憾界；扩展到紧致核族、有限及增长核字典的高概率oracle界；引入阈值边界规则。

Result: 在SURE调优、硬选择和迹校正基线以及Silverbox和Cascaded Tanks基准测试上，所提估计器在正则化有益时保留大部分收益，在核函数误设时退回到ML，并在基准测试上完成了有限样本分析。

Conclusion: XMSE感知的混合估计器通过自适应插值策略有效结合了EB和ML的优点，在核函数对齐良好时享受正则化收益，在误设时避免性能退化，具有理论保证和实证验证。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) estimators can match the first-order asymptotic risk of maximum likelihood (ML) while behaving very differently at second order: recent excess mean squared error (XMSE) analysis shows that kernel-based EB estimation may be worse than ML when the kernel is poorly aligned with the true parameter. This paper turns that diagnostic into a design principle. We propose an XMSE-aware mixed estimator that interpolates between ML and EB shrinkage. Its fixed-weight XMSE is a scalar quadratic, yielding a closed-form oracle mixing weight that is no worse than both ML and the base EB estimator at the XMSE scale. A plug-in implementation based on finite-sample XMSE approximations is proved consistent, with a second-order oracle regret rate for an interior oracle weight. We further establish a transfer of the regret bound to the fixed-weight risk curve evaluated at the selected weight, a thresholded boundary rule, and extensions to compact kernel families and to finite and growing kernel dictionaries with high-probability oracle bounds. Finite impulse response simulations with SURE-tuned, hard-selection, and trace-corrected baselines, together with the public Silverbox and Cascaded Tanks benchmarks, show that the proposed estimator retains most of the benefit of regularization when it is helpful and retreats toward ML under kernel misspecification, with an identified finite-de analyzed on the benchmarks.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Global Divergences: A Local-Mass Perspective on Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27090)
*Hanli Xu,Fengxiang He,Sarat Moka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯推理中全局目标（如KL散度和ELBO）无法捕捉的局部质量行为，提出了质量指数（Mass Index）和正则化扩展KL（RE-KL）两种数学工具，并证明了局部小球质量的绝对、相对和方向性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局目标（如KL散度、ELBO）在贝叶斯推理中被广泛用于度量分布差异，但它们无法捕捉局部质量行为。本文旨在从局部理论角度解释贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量。

Method: (1) 提出质量指数（Mass Index），用于记录局部质量的幂律和对数衰减尺度；(2) 提出正则化扩展KL（RE-KL），一种可在奇异分量存在时定义的局部化散度。利用这两个工具分析贝叶斯更新对局部质量的影响。

Result: 证明了：(1) 幂对数似然因子显式地改变局部质量；(2) 依赖于参数的支持域或其平滑软化可通过参数值附近剩余质量改变局部尺度。利用局部RE-KL证明了局部小球质量的绝对、相对和方向性不等式。

Conclusion: 该研究为局部质量行为提供了局部理论解释，并通过实验进行了可控验证。

Abstract: Global objectives, such as KL divergence and ELBO, are widely used in Bayesian inference for measuring distributional discrepancy. This paper studies their local-mass behaviour that is not directly captured by such objectives. We introduce and use two mathematical tools: (1) Mass Index for recording the polynomial and logarithmic decay scales of local mass, and (2) regularised extended KL (RE-KL), a set-localised divergence that can be formulated in the presence of singular components. Mass Indices help characterise how Bayesian updating changes local mass: (1) power-log likelihood factors shift it explicitly, and (2) parameter-dependent supports, or their smooth softenings, may change the local scale through the amount of mass that remains near the parameter value. Using local RE-KL, we prove absolute, relative, and directional inequalities for comparing local small-ball masses under the two KL directions. Together, these results provide a local theoretical account of local mass behaviour. Experiments provide controlled illustrations of the local behaviour. Code is available at https://github.com/Forsythia0604/Local-Mass-Framework.

</details>


### [199] [Ribbon: Scalable Approximation and Robust Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27269)
*Graham Gibson,John Tipton,Kellin Rumsey,Natalie Klein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Ribbon是一种可扩展的近似方法，通过影响函数线性化（围绕单次拟合模型）替代贝叶斯自举或加权似然自举中的重复模型重拟合，实现后验不确定性量化，在保持数据重加权结构的同时显著降低计算成本，并提供了可调浓度的校准不确定度族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂、高维或误指定的模型难以可靠量化预测不确定性；全贝叶斯和自举重采样方法虽原理上严谨，但需要后验采样或重复重拟合，对现代机器学习模型计算开销过大。

Method: 引入Ribbon方法，利用影响函数对单个拟合模型进行线性化，近似狄利克雷重加权自举的不确定性。该方法仅需事后线性代数运算，保留贝叶斯自举的一阶数据重加权结构，并通过一般浓度参数提供可调校准的狄利克雷重加权族。

Result: 在合成回归、MNIST分类和California Housing基准上，Ribbon在避免重复模型训练的同时，提供了具有竞争力的预测性能和多个设置下更好的校准效果。

Conclusion: Ribbon在正确似然设定下渐近等价于平坦先验拉普拉斯近似，在模型误指定下能恢复稳健三明治协方差，是一种高效且理论保证的不确定性量化方法。

Abstract: Reliably quantifying predictive uncertainty is difficult for complex, high-dimensional, or misspecified models. Both fully Bayesian and bootstrap resampling methods provide principled uncertainty estimates but are often too expensive for modern machine-learning models because they require posterior sampling or repeated model refitting. We introduce Ribbon, a scalable approximation to Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap uncertainty. Ribbon replaces repeated refitting with an influence-function linearization around a single fitted model, preserving the first-order data-reweighting structure of the Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Ribbon approximates the Bayesian-bootstrap or weighted-likelihood-bootstrap refitting target. With a general concentration parameter, Ribbon gives a calibrated Dirichlet-reweighting family whose uncertainty scale can be tuned on validation data. We show that Ribbon is asymptotically equivalent to a flat-prior Laplace approximation under correct likelihood specification and recovers the robust sandwich covariance under misspecification. Across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks, Ribbon provides competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in several settings while avoiding repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [200] [When are likely answers right? On Sequence Probability and Correctness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27359)
*Johannes Zenn,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了序列概率（条件概率）与生成正确性之间的关系，发现在固定数据集中，较高的序列概率通常能预测正确性，但这种关系在跨解码方法或超参数调整时并不成立，且对同一提示的多次响应中，序列概率并非正确性的良好指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多解码方法通过将概率质量转移到模型更可能输出的结果来提升性能，但序列概率（给定提示下延续的条件概率）与正确性之间的对齐关系尚不明确。本文旨在量化这种关系，以厘清解码方法在何种情况下能够提升正确性。

Method: 在四个层面系统量化序列概率与正确性之间的关系：（1）跨解码方法；（2）方法内跨超参数；（3）数据集中跨提示-答案对；（4）同一提示的多次响应。涉及多种解码方法、模型和基准测试。

Result: （1）在固定数据集中，较高的序列概率通常能预测正确性；（2）但通过改变超参数或方法来增加序列概率并不能可靠地提升准确率；（3）对于同一提示的多次响应，序列概率不是正确性的良好指标。

Conclusion: 这些发现厘清了解码何时能够以及何时不能提升正确性，为解码策略选择、自一致性方法以及无验证器的自我改进提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Many decoding methods for large language models can be understood as shifting probability mass toward outputs that are more likely under the model, either locally at the token level or globally at the sequence level. Therefore, their success depends on a fundamental question: when does sequence probability, that is, the conditional probability of a continuation given a prompt, actually align with correctness? In this paper, we set out to quantify this relationship across decoding methods, models, and benchmarks at four levels: across decoding methods, across hyperparameters within a method, across prompt-answer pairs within a dataset, and across repeated responses to the same prompt. We find that higher sequence probability is often predictive of correctness across prompt-answer pairs within a fixed dataset. However, this relationship does not generally transfer to decoding decisions: increasing sequence probability by changing hyperparameters or methods does not reliably improve accuracy. Further, sequence probability is not a good indicator of correctness for responses to the same prompt. These findings clarify when decoding can and cannot be expected to improve correctness, and provide practical guidance for decoding, self-consistency, and verifier-free self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Scalable Operator Learning via Nyström Approximation With Denoising Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26652)
*Naveen Gupta,Vaibhav Silmana,S. Sivananthan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对向量值回归问题，提出基于Nyström子采样的高效算子学习算法，在广义源条件下达到极小化最优收敛率，并在信号去噪、图像去噪等多个任务上实现与全核方法相当的性能且显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准核方法因构造和求逆大规模核矩阵导致计算成本过高，限制了其在大型数据集上的可扩展性，亟需高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于Nyström子采样的算子学习算法，适用于向量值再生核希尔伯特空间中的函数输出；在广义源条件（超越经典Hölder型和算子单调框架）下建立收敛率分析。

Result: 在信号去噪、实时音频去噪、图像去噪、逆Radon变换重建及能效预测等数值实验中，所提方法在性能上与全核方法相当，同时显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: Nyström子采样策略能有效克服核方法的计算瓶颈，在保持精度前提下大幅提升可扩展性，为大规模向量值回归和函数去噪问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Nyström subsampling for vector-valued regression in vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces. Standard kernel methods often suffer from prohibitive computational costs due to the construction and inversion of large kernel matrices, which limits their scalability to large datasets. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an efficient operator learning algorithm based on Nyström subsampling that accommodates functional outputs. Under general source conditions characterized by index functions-extending beyond the classical Hölder-type and operator-monotone frameworks-we establish minimax-optimal convergence rates for the proposed estimator. As an application of the proposed framework, we consider function denoising problems. Unlike classical denoising methods, which are typically tailored to specific signal representations or noise models, our approach formulates denoising within a general operator learning framework. Numerical experiments on signal denoising, real-time audio denoising, image denoising, inverse Radon transform reconstruction, and energy-efficiency prediction confirm that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to full kernel methods while substantially reducing computational cost.

</details>


### [202] [On the optimal prediction of extreme events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26270)
*Benjamin Bobbia,Stilian Stoev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多元正则变化分布下，预测响应变量极端值的渐近最优齐次预测器。通过将预测精度表达为尾部依赖系数并转化为变分问题，得到了一般解——基于倾斜分布的非极端条件分位数。在极值理论的阈值峰值框架下，建立了估计量的相合性，并在太阳耀斑预测中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端事件（如自然灾害、金融风险）的预测在科学和工程领域至关重要，但极端数据的稀缺性使得寻找最优预测器成为难点。现有方法仅在少数特例中能得到显式最优解，缺乏针对广泛模型类的一般性理论和方法。

Method: 假设(Y,X)服从多元正则变化分布，预测器为协变量的正齐次函数h(X)。将渐近预测精度等价于尾部依赖系数λ(Y,h(X))，并将其表示为角度测度的积分泛函，从而将最优预测问题转化为变分问题。通过引入倾斜分布的非极端条件分位数得到一般解，并在阈值峰值框架下构造估计量。

Result: 获得了最优齐次预测器的一般解形式，建立了估计量在大类角度测度下的相合性。在数值实验中，所提方法对最优预言机估计器的逼近效果显著，且在极端太阳耀斑预测这一挑战性任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文为多元正则变化分布下的极端值预测提供了统一的理论框架和实用方法，克服了传统方法仅适用于特定模型的局限，在理论和应用层面均有重要贡献。

Abstract: The prediction of the extremely large values of a response variable $Y$ in terms of a vector of covariates $X=(X_i)_{i=1}^d$ is a fundamental problem arising in many scientific and engineering domains. The scarcity of data in the extremes makes the optimal solution of this problem of particular importance. The optimal predictors of such events can be explicitly characterized in just a few cases and it is of fundamental practical and theoretical interest to develop optimal estimators over large classes of models and predictors. In this work, the focus is on the case where $(Y,X)$ have a multivariate regularly varying distribution and one seeks an optimal predictor expressed as a positive homogeneous function $h(X)$ of the covariates. The asymptotic prediction precision in this setting coincides with the tail-dependence coefficient $λ(Y,h(X))$ and it can be expressed as an integral functional of the associated angular measure of $(Y,X)$. Thus, finding asymptotically optimal homogeneous predictors amounts to solving a variational problem. We obtain a general solution to this problem, which is expressed in terms of a non-extreme conditional quantile of a tilted distribution derived from the angular measure. This leads to a general inference methodology for the optimal predictors in the peaks-over-threshold framework form extreme value theory. We establish the universal consistency for these estimators over large classes of angular measures. A general-purpose implementation of the resulting inference procedure is shown to work remarkably well against optimal oracle estimators, as well as in the challenging problem of extreme solar flare prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Statistical and Structural Approaches to Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26200)
*Antonio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代机器学习系统已演变为复杂的社会技术架构，指出当前算法公平性范式存在两个根本局限：依赖确定性点估计进行审计，以及将个体视为缺乏结构背景的孤立实体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统已超越单纯的预测工具，成为积极调节人类机会的社会技术架构，但其内嵌了结构性不平等和偏见，亟需更有效的公平性方法。

Method: 识别并分析当代公平性范式的两个根本局限：依赖确定性点估计进行审计，以及将个体视为脱离结构背景的孤立实体。

Result: 指出现有公平性缓解策略建立在脆弱的简化之上，限制了其在复杂社会技术环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: 需要超越传统的点估计和个体化视角，采用更能体现结构性背景的公平性方法。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems have outgrown their origins as isolated predictive constructs, evolving into complex socio-technical architectures that actively mediate human opportunity. As algorithms increasingly determine access to economic and social opportunities, it has become widely recognized that these systems are deeply embedded with the structural inequalities and prejudices of their environments. The field of algorithmic fairness emerged in response to the growing recognition that models optimized for predictive accuracy can systematically disadvantage marginalized groups. Early mitigation strategies, however, rested on fragile simplifications that limited their effectiveness in complex socio-technical environments. This thesis identifies and addresses two fundamental limitations of contemporary fairness paradigms: the reliance on deterministic point estimates for auditing and the treatment of individuals as isolated entities devoid of structural context.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Probabilistic Filters with Strictly Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26497)
*Eviatar Bach,Ricardo Baptista,Jochen Bröcker,Bohan Chen,Andrew Stuart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于严格适当评分规则和Transformer架构的集成数据同化方法（PSEF），通过监督学习从合成轨迹中逼近贝叶斯滤波分布，在非高斯、非线性场景下优于传统方法和基于MSE的学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯滤波分布是状态推断与不确定性量化的自然对象，但通常无法作为监督学习的目标可用。现有方法在处理非线性、非高斯和多模态后验分布时存在局限性，亟需一种可学习且能准确逼近任意滤波分布的方法。

Method: 提出PSEF方法，使用置换不变的Transformer架构将预报集合和观测映射为分析集合；训练目标采用严格适当评分规则（能量分数），确保概率准确性；通过合成状态-观测轨迹进行训练，并给出有限集合下的经验目标及其与总体目标的关系证明。

Result: 数值实验表明，PSEF能准确逼近非线性、非高斯、多模态的滤波分布；在数据同化任务中优于经典方法和基于MSE的学习方法；对于近高斯问题，学习对EnKF的修正效果最佳；对于强非高斯问题，端到端方法（去除归纳偏置）更为优越。

Conclusion: PSEF通过严格适当评分和置换不变网络架构，首次实现了对任意贝叶斯滤波分布的可学习逼近，为非线性非高斯系统的数据同化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering of partially and noisily observed dynamical systems seeks to infer the evolving conditional distribution of the state of a dynamical system, given observations, in an online fashion. This Bayesian filtering distribution is the natural object for uncertainty quantification, but it is rarely available as a supervised learning target. However, one can often use the forecast model to generate synthetic system trajectories, along with synthetic observations. We introduce the proper scoring ensemble filter (PSEF), an ensemble data assimilation method based on training an analysis map to approximate the filtering distribution using only synthetic state--observation trajectories. The analysis step is represented as a permutation-invariant, transformer-based map that takes as input a forecast ensemble and observations, producing an analysis ensemble. Training is based on strictly proper scoring rules -- with the energy score used in our implementation -- so that probabilistic accuracy is rewarded over the whole probability distribution. We prove that, under a realizability assumption, the population objective is minimized by the true Bayesian filtering distribution. We also derive the finite-ensemble empirical objective used in training and relate its single state--observation trajectory form to the population objective, using a mean-field consistency argument. Numerical experiments show that the learned filter accurately approximates challenging filtering distributions, including nonlinear, non-Gaussian, and multi-modal posteriors, and achieves stronger performance in data assimilation tasks than classical methods or learning-based methods with mean-squared-error objectives. For close-to-Gaussian problems, learning a correction to the EnKF is the best approach, while for highly non-Gaussian problems an end-to-end approach that discards this inductive bias is superior.

</details>


### [205] [Escaping Iterative Parameter-Space Noise: Differentially Private Learning with a Hypernetwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26772)
*Naoki Nishikawa,Shokichi Takakura,Satoshi Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HyperDP的新框架，通过超网络在低维数据集表示上仅注入一次隐私噪声来生成模型参数，避免了DP-SGD中反复添加高维噪声的问题，在合成环境和扩散模型微调实验中均优于DP-SGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私神经网络训练受限于DP-SGD等方法在参数空间中反复注入高维噪声所带来的巨大噪声量，导致模型效用严重下降。

Method: 提出HyperDP框架：1) 将每个私有样本嵌入到低维表示；2) 聚合嵌入并添加差分隐私扰动得到DP数据集嵌入；3) 使用在公开数据集上预训练的超网络从噪声嵌入生成目标模型参数。隐私噪声仅在低维表示上注入一次。

Result: 在合成设定下理论证明在固定隐私预算下模型效用优于DP-SGD；在扩散模型LoRA微调中取得比DP-SGD及其他公开数据引导方法更低的FID分数。

Conclusion: HyperDP通过将隐私噪声注入从高维参数空间转移到低维嵌入空间，显著降低了噪声对模型效用的不利影响，为差分隐私学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training of neural networks is often hindered by the large amount of noise required by gradient-based methods such as DP-SGD, which repeatedly inject high-dimensional noise in parameter space throughout training. In this paper, we propose a new framework for DP learning that avoids iterative optimization in parameter space. Instead of updating the target model using privatized gradients, we employ a hypernetwork trained on public datasets to map a private dataset to the parameters of the target model. Specifically, each example is embedded into a low-dimensional representation, the embeddings are aggregated and perturbed to obtain a DP dataset embedding, and the hypernetwork generates the target model parameters from this noisy embedding. Because privacy noise is injected only once into a low-dimensional dataset representation, our approach can significantly reduce the adverse effect of noise. We theoretically show in a synthetic setting that, under a fixed privacy budget, models produced by our approach achieve higher utility than those trained with DP-SGD. Moreover, we apply our approach to LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion models and show that it achieves lower FID than LoRA models trained with DP-SGD and other public-data-guided methods.

</details>


### [206] [Asymptotically Optimal Learning for Parametric Prophet Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26893)
*Jung-hun Kim,Anna Grebennikova,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了指数型参数族（包含指数分布、帕累托分布等）的i.i.d.奖励先知不等式问题，分别刻画了无界支撑集和有界支撑集情况下的最优渐近竞争比，并提出了一种基于置信度的动态规划在线学习策略，无需离线样本即可达到相同的最优渐近竞争比，同时给出了典型分布的收敛速率和数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先知不等式问题中，现有工作大多假设已知奖励分布或依赖离线样本；本文旨在探索当奖励来自未知参数的指数型参数族时，如何仅通过在线观测实现与完全信息情形相同的最优渐近竞争比。

Method: 首先理论刻画了指数型参数族（指数、帕累托、有界支撑幂族分布）在完全信息下的最优渐近竞争比；然后提出了一种基于置信度的动态规划在线学习策略，利用参数族的显式结构，无需外部离线样本即可在在线观测中逼近该竞争比，并推导了典型分布的收敛速率。

Result: 无界支撑情形下最优渐近竞争比为$(θ/(θ-c_+))^{c_+/θ}/Γ(1-c_+/θ)$，有界支撑情形下极限为1；所提置信度动态规划策略无需离线样本即可达到相同的最优渐近竞争比；数值实验验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用指数型参数族的显式结构，可以在完全在线场景下实现与完全信息情形一致的最优渐近竞争比，为在线学习与先知不等式结合提供了新的理论保证和方法框架。

Abstract: We study learning in prophet inequalities with i.i.d. rewards drawn from an exponential-type parametric family with an unknown parameter $θ$, a class that includes exponential, Pareto, and bounded-support power-family distributions. We first characterize the optimal full-information asymptotic competitive ratio for this family. In the unbounded-support case, the limit is $ {\left(θ/({θ-c_+})\right)^{c_+/θ}}/ {Γ(1-c_+/θ)},$ while in the bounded-support case, the limit is $1$. We then propose a confidence-based dynamic-programming policy for online learning. By exploiting the explicit parametric structure, the policy achieves the same optimal asymptotic competitive ratio using only online observations, without external offline samples. We further derive distribution-specific convergence rates for canonical examples. Finally, numerical experiments on synthetic instances illustrate the performance of our algorithm.

</details>


### [207] [Decision-Aligned Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26990)
*Annika Schneider,Tommy Rochussen,Joshua Stiller,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出现有不确定性估计评估指标（如NLL、ECE）与下游决策效用脱节，提出"决策对齐"标准，并设计先验加权效用度量（一类特殊proper scoring rules），实验证明其比传统指标更能反映实际决策收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前不确定性量化（UQ）评估广泛使用负对数似然、期望校准误差等通用指标，但这些指标表现良好并不保证在下游决策中具有高实用性，因此需要建立评估指标与决策效用之间的对齐关系。

Method: 提出"决策对齐"（decision-alignment）准则来判定评估指标是否与下游效用一致；在此框架下分析发现许多常用不确定性指标要么与常见决策问题错位，要么编码了病态的先验信念；进而提出先验加权效用度量（prior-weighted utility metrics），这是一类特殊的proper scoring rules，实现决策对齐的不确定性评估。

Result: 在基准实验和真实世界案例中，所提出的度量始终与实际决策效用对齐，而传统指标则不能。这揭示了当前UQ评估协议中的缺陷。

Conclusion: 提供了将现有指标向决策相关UQ评估进行原则性扩展的方法，建议采用先验加权效用度量替代通用指标以更好地服务于下游决策任务。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimates in machine learning are typically evaluated using generic metrics such as the negative log-likelihood and expected calibration error, yet good performance on such metrics does not necessarily imply high utility in downstream decisions. We introduce decision-alignment, a criterion that reveals which evaluation metrics meaningfully align with downstream utilities. Applying this framework, we show that many widely used uncertainty metrics are either misaligned with common decision problems or encode pathological prior beliefs about the downstream task. We then propose prior-weighted utility metrics, a special class of proper scoring rules that provides decision-aligned uncertainty evaluation. Across benchmark experiments and real-world case studies, our metrics consistently align with realized decision utility, while conventional metrics do not. Our results surface flaws in the current UQ evaluation protocol and offer a principled extension of existing metrics toward decision-relevant UQ evaluation.

</details>


### [208] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对共享词表的LLM家族，提出FisherSketch方法，通过流式计算头Fisher对齐的余弦相似度（即核均值嵌入在联合激活-误差空间中的余弦），在无需物化Fisher矩阵的情况下实现高效源选择（16KB任务签名+192KB流状态），解决了激活相似度指标（如CKA）在共享输出头设置下不可识别的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列等科学字符串领域，存在大量共享分词器但预测目标不同的候选语料库，需要高效的无训练源选择方法。传统表示相似度指标在共享输出头下不可识别（模型可共享相同表示但头部更新正交），而经典更新几何指标在词汇表规模下计算成本过高。

Method: 核心身份是：头部Fisher对齐恰好等于联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦，分解为激活因子、误差因子和耦合因子。FisherSketch以单次流式传递直接估计该余弦，使用K=128/256的草图，生成16KB任务签名（m=4096）和192KB逐任务流状态。

Result: FisherSketch在K=128/256时即可实现实用化头部Fisher对齐，签名和边际量可用于诊断LLM任务相似性的驱动因素（激活、误差还是耦合）。在共享参数、内部层验证以及Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer移位实验中，当激活相似性无法区分任务时，FisherSketch仍能保持信息量。

Conclusion: FisherSketch为共享词表LLM家族提供了一种计算可行且有效的源选择工具，超越激活相似度指标的局限性，且其轻量级签名可随模型哈希一同存储。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [209] [Physics-guided Convolutional Neural Network for Domain Growth Prediction in Systems with Conserved Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26128)
*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络作为替代模型，用于学习非线性偏微分方程描述的微结构演化。该模型能准确预测Cahn-Hilliard方程控制的双相混合物相分离的全时间演化过程，在长期预测中保持稳定和准确，并符合Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解非线性偏微分方程的计算成本高昂，而深度神经网络替代模型在效率上具有优势。本文旨在开发一种高效、准确的替代模型来学习微结构演化过程。

Method: 提出基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络（attention-based, physics-guided CNN）作为替代模型，用于学习由Cahn-Hilliard方程描述的双相混合物相分离过程。

Result: 训练的替代模型在临界和非临界混合物场景下均能稳定准确地预测长期时间演化，保持混合物的成分守恒，准确捕捉畴尺寸增长，且与Lifshitz-Slyozov畴生长定律一致。

Conclusion: 该框架有效建模了具有守恒动力学的系统，并可扩展应用于其他复杂动力学系统。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [210] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为χισω（ChiSao）的GPU原生种群优化器，通过收敛-反收敛振荡循环机制，在42个Simon Fraser大学优化基准函数上实现了100%的模式恢复，相比传统方法最高获得39倍加速，且在高维（d≥8）和噪声环境下仍保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态黑盒函数优化方法（如盆地跳跃、CMA-ES、多起点梯度下降）均顺序执行，无法利用现代GPU的大规模并行能力，在高维复杂多模态问题上效率低下甚至失效。

Method: 提出χισω算法，核心创新为收敛-反收敛振荡循环：达到真实峰值的样本被冻结（"粘住"）并保留，其余样本通过基于动量的反收敛和随机平滑梯度继续探索；结合两种自适应重采样策略（Repulse Monkey和Golden Rooster）维持种群多样性。

Result: 在Simon Fraser大学优化基准套件的全部42个函数上（维度d∈{2,4,8,16,32,64}），χισω在最难的多模态函数上实现100%模式恢复（而CPU基线方法在d≥8时全部失效）；相比盆地跳跃获得最高34倍加速（Michalewicz d=64），在单峰函数上获得最高39倍加速；在噪声σ_noise高达1.0时仍保持100%模式检测可靠性。

Conclusion: χισω作为GPU原生方法，通过独特的振荡机制有效解决了多模态优化中的局部陷阱问题，在速度、鲁棒性和模式恢复率上均显著超越现有CPU基线方法，且作为开源Python包提供。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout. On all 42 functions of the Simon Fraser University optimization benchmark suite across dimensions $d \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64\}$, \chisao{} achieves \textbf{100\%} mode recovery where all CPU baselines collapse at $d \geq 8$ on the hardest multimodal functions, at up to \textbf{$34\times$} speedup over basin-hopping on functions where all methods succeed (Michalewicz $d=64$) and up to \textbf{$39\times$} on unimodal functions (Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid $d=64$, pure GPU dividend). All benchmarks evaluate the objective by value alone -- gradients come from finite differences -- so the reported speedups are a derivative-free worst case. Under substantial likelihood noise ($σ_{\mathrm{noise}}$ up to 1.0), mode detection remains 100\% reliable. The algorithm is available as a standalone open-source Python package on PyPI.

</details>


### [211] [Implementation of reinforcement learning in chemical reaction networks: application to phototaxis as curiosity-driven exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26168)
*Ruyi Tang,Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信息驱动的感知运动框架，将部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）与生化反应动力学（CRN-ODEs）相结合，重新解释单细胞藻类的趋光运动。通过逆强化学习（IRL）从实验轨迹推断行为目标，证明run-tumble交替是一种信息获取策略，用于解决感官模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有趋光性模型将藻类运动简化为机械的刺激-响应run-tumble过程，忽略了生物体如何主动采样环境以减少感官模糊性。作者从最小认知视角出发，希望将导航行为重新诠释为主观的、信息驱动的感知运动过程。

Method: 构建POMDP框架，将环境变量视为隐藏状态，细胞通过无记忆贝叶斯更新维护最小内部状态；采用CRN-ODEs实现内部动力学；设计光感受的生物物理观测过程及化学可计算的信息增益多项式界；利用逆强化学习（IRL）从30条衣藻实验轨迹推断行为目标函数。

Result: 模型成功再现了实验测得的趋光分布；与基于随机模拟算法（SSA）的客观基线相比性能相当；揭示了run-tumble交替作为信息获取策略的本质——tumble通过重新定向采样新的感官配置以解决传感器模糊性。

Conclusion: 细胞内生化网络可以支持适应性的信息寻求行为，为理解细胞导航中的最小认知机制提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Living systems navigate environments using noisy and incomplete sensory signals. In unicellular algae, phototaxis is often modeled as a mechanistic run--tumble process driven by stimulus--response rules. However, such descriptions overlook how organisms actively sample their environment to reduce sensory ambiguity. From a minimal cognition perspective, we reframe this navigation as a subjective, information-driven sensorimotor process. To this end, we propose a framework linking a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with biochemical reaction dynamics. Environmental variables are hidden, while the cell updates a minimal internal state from each observation through a memoryless Bayesian step. These internal dynamics balance orienting toward light with exploratory reorientation and can be implemented through Chemical-Reaction-Network Ordinary Differential Equations (CRN--ODEs). Our model includes a biophysical observation process for photoreception and a chemically computable polynomial bound on information gain. Using Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) on 30 experimentally recorded Chlamydomonas trajectories, we infer the behavioral objective consistent with observed phototactic motion and benchmark the resulting dynamics with standard Stochastic Simulation Algorithm (SSA) baselines. Our model reproduces the empirical alignment-to-light distribution, comparable to objective SSA baselines on this dataset. Within this framework, run--tumble alternation emerges as an information-acquisition strategy: tumbling reorients the cell to sample new sensory configurations and resolve sensor ambiguity, demonstrating how intracellular biochemical networks can support adaptive information-seeking behavior in cellular navigation.

</details>


### [212] [Neural Architecture Search for Generative Adversarial Networks: A Comprehensive Review and Critical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26169)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了神经架构搜索(NAS)在生成对抗网络(GANs)设计中的应用，系统比较了不同搜索策略、评估指标和性能表现，发现进化算法和基于梯度的方法在特定场景中表现优越，并指出需要超越传统IS和FID指标的更鲁棒评估方法以及多样化数据集的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化GAN设计面临手动设计架构的挑战，NAS作为自动化搜索有效架构的技术应运而生，但现有NAS-GAN方法缺乏系统性比较和综述，需要为研究者提供结构化指导。

Method: 采用综述方法，对NAS应用于GANs的各类方法进行系统分类与对比，基于搜索策略、评估指标和性能结果等维度展开分析。

Result: 进化算法和基于梯度的方法在特定上下文中表现更优；传统评估指标（IS和FID）存在局限性，需要更鲁棒的评估标准；多样化数据集对GAN性能评估至关重要。

Conclusion: 通过结构化对比现有NAS-GAN技术，为研究者开发更有效的NAS方法提供指引，推动GAN领域发展，同时指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a pivotal technique in optimizing the design of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), automating the search for effective architectures while addressing the challenges inherent in manual design. This paper provides a comprehensive review of NAS methods applied to GANs, categorizing and comparing various approaches based on criteria such as search strategies, evaluation metrics, and performance outcomes. The review highlights the benefits of NAS in improving GAN performance, stability, and efficiency, while also identifying limitations and areas for future research. Key findings include the superiority of evolutionary algorithms and gradient-based methods in certain contexts, the importance of robust evaluation metrics beyond traditional scores like Inception Score (IS) and Fréchet Inception Distance (FID), and the need for diverse datasets in assessing GAN performance. By presenting a structured comparison of existing NAS-GAN techniques, this paper aims to guide researchers in developing more effective NAS methods and advancing the field of GANs.

</details>


### [213] [KG-TRACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Mechanistic Grounding in Antimicrobial Resistance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26179)
*Naman Garg,Sarika Jain,Sourav Yadav,Bharat K. Bhargava,Ghanapriya Singh,Abhishek Srivastava,Parimal Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KG-TRACE是一个神经符号框架，通过整合WHO突变知识图谱作为结构化生物学约束来指导神经基因组模型，利用可学习的认知信任门动态加权神经证据与符号生物知识，在结核分枝杆菌耐药性预测中实现高准确率（异烟肼AUROC 0.9760）和92.5%的符号覆盖率，并通过引入生物基础比率（BGR）指标量化神经归因与已有生物学知识的一致性，为临床提供可验证的审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WGS的AMR预测模型虽已达到高准确率，但缺乏将神经归因与已建立的生物学通路相联系的机制，导致模型可解释性和临床可信度不足。

Method: 提出了KG-TRACE框架，融合了基因组特征和基于RotatE的知识图谱嵌入，通过可学习的认知信任门动态加权神经证据与符号生物学知识；并引入BGR指标量化神经归因与已有生物学知识之间的对齐程度。

Result: 在CRyPTIC结核分枝杆菌队列上，异烟肼预测AUROC达到0.9760；异烟肼耐药预测的符号覆盖率达到92.5%；能有效识别MDR共现伪像，并对'UNCERTAIN'案例发出实验室随访标记。

Conclusion: 神经符号基础为临床医生提供了可验证的审计轨迹，弥合了预测准确性与临床信任之间的差距。

Abstract: While WGS-based AMR prediction has reached high accuracy, existing models lack a mechanism to ground neural attributions in established biological pathways. We present KG-TRACE, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that integrates the WHO mutation knowledge graph (KG) as a structured biological constraint on a neural genomic model. Unlike existing methods that learn statistical patterns in isolation, KG-TRACE fuses genomic features and RotatE-based KG embeddings through a learned epistemic trust gate, dynamically weighting neural evidence against symbolic biological knowledge.
  Evaluated on the CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis cohort, KG-TRACE achieves an AUROC of 0.9760 for isoniazid, achieving competitive accuracy while its primary value lies in symbolic grounding, not predictive uplift. More importantly, we introduce the Biological Grounding Ratio (BGR), a dataset-level metric that quantifies alignment between neural attributions and established biology. Our framework achieves a 92.5% symbolic coverage of isoniazid-resistant predictions and effectively identifies MDR co-occurrence artifacts by issuing laboratory follow-up flags for 'UNCERTAIN' cases. We demonstrate that neuro-symbolic grounding provides a verifiable audit trail for clinicians, bridging the gap between predictive accuracy and clinical trust.

</details>


### [214] [Necessary but Not Sufficient: Temperature Control and Reproducibility in LLM-as-Judge Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26185)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估框架中的LLM裁判组件即使在温度=0设置下也存在不可复现性（通过/不通过判定翻转），暴露了安全评估的结构性漏洞，建议将裁判不一致性作为首要健康指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"温度=0可使裁判判定确定性"这一广泛假设，验证其在真实安全评估代码库中的有效性。

Method: 对日本AISI开源aisev代码库进行实证测试：(1) 检查默认温度设置（实际为1.0而非假定）；(2) 强制温度=0 + top_k=1贪心解码下的重复性测试（690次API调用，涵盖2个提供商、3个模型层级、5种采样配置）。

Result: (1) 默认温度=1.0导致决策边界附近项目在20次运行中通过/不通过不一致性达~50%；(2) 强制温度=0 + top_k=1下，7个边界项目中有1-2个仍不可复现；(3) Claude Opus 4.7/4.8已弃用温度参数，使主要缓解措施对新模型不可用。

Conclusion: 评估框架报告单次判定结果而不报告方差或裁判不一致性指标，会将噪声伪装成安全属性。建议将裁判不一致性作为与分数同等重要的首要健康指标。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge ("grader") components are now standard in evaluation harnesses, including safety evaluations where a pass/fail verdict may gate downstream deployment decisions. A widespread assumption is that setting the grader's sampling temperature to 0 makes grading deterministic. We test this assumption against a real safety-evaluation codebase (Japan AISI's open-source aisev) and show it fails on two levels. First, the harness invokes its grader without setting temperature or seed; the underlying provider silently applies its default of 1.0, so items near the decision boundary flip pass/fail across identical runs (per-item disagreement up to ~50% over 20 runs). Second, pinning temperature=0 reduces but does not eliminate flips: across 690 API calls spanning two providers, three model tiers, and five sampling configurations, 1-2 of 7 borderline items remain non-reproducible even under forced greedy decoding (top_k=1). Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 has since deprecated temperature entirely, rendering the primary mitigation inapplicable to newer model generations. These findings expose a structural gap: evaluation harnesses that report single-run verdicts without variance or grader-disagreement metrics can present noise as a safety property. We release a reproduction harness (690 calls, 7 conditions) and recommend that harnesses treat grader disagreement as a first-class health metric alongside the scores themselves.

</details>


### [215] [Clue-Guided Money Laundering Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26189)
*Boyang Wang,Jianing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Clue2Group框架，通过线索引导的逐步扩展方式，在金融网络中恢复完整的洗钱团伙结构，弥补了现有图异常检测方法（节点级风险告警）与全局团伙发现方法（被动全网络搜索）与实际反洗钱调查流程之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法仅产生节点级风险告警，全局团伙发现方法被动地在全网络中搜索可疑群体，两者均与实际反洗钱调查流程不匹配——调查人员通常从一个具体线索出发逐步扩展调查以恢复责任团伙。

Method: 提出Clue2Group框架：首先构建紧凑的局部调查上下文以减少噪声并保留链式和循环式洗钱结构；然后使用多语义局部时序图神经网络估计线索条件化的局部风险场；最后整合风险、结构和先验模式证据以恢复连贯的洗钱团伙。

Result: 在两个大规模反洗钱基准数据集上的实验表明，Clue2Group为反洗钱调查提供了一个实用的线索驱动分析框架。

Conclusion: Clue2Group是弥合基于图的反洗钱研究与真实调查工作流程之间差距的可行一步。

Abstract: Money Laundering Group Discovery (MLGD) aims to identify hidden criminal groups and recover their complete structures in large-scale financial networks. Existing graph anomaly detection methods mainly produce node-level risk alerts, while global group discovery methods passively search for suspicious groups over the whole network. Both are mismatched with real Anti-money-laundering (AML) investigations, where analysts usually start from a concrete clue and gradually expand the investigation to recover the responsible group. To address this gap, we propose Clue-Guided Group Discovery (CGGD), where a laundering group is progressively recovered from an initial clue set through analyst interaction. We further propose Clue2Group, a framework that first constructs a compact local investigation context to reduce noise and preserve chain-like and cycle-like laundering structures. It then estimates a clue-conditioned local risk field with a multi-semantic local-temporal GNN, and finally integrates risk, structural, and prior-pattern evidence to recover a coherent laundering group. Experiments on two large-scale AML benchmarks show that Clue2Group provides a practical clue-driven analysis framework for AML investigations, offering a feasible step toward bridging the gap between graph-based AML research and real investigation workflows.

</details>


### [216] [Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26192)
*Jialan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将哈希学习融入联邦学习的FHPLF模型，通过二值梯度矩阵、投影汉明距离和安全二值梯度重组策略，在准确率、效率和隐私保护间取得优越平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希学习需上传原始数据至中心服务器，违反数据安全法规；联邦学习虽保护数据隐私，但传输大规模实值梯度导致高通信开销与隐私风险；现有哈希方法二值编码表示能力有限，降低模型精度。

Method: (a)用二值梯度矩阵替代实值梯度矩阵，降低计算/存储/通信成本并增强隐私；(b)提出投影汉明距离进行相似性建模，捕捉各二进制位的重要性以提升表示能力；(c)设计SBG-PEU策略降低传输过程中的用户交互泄露风险。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，FHPLF一致优于最先进的哈希学习和联邦学习方法。

Conclusion: FHPLF在准确率、效率和隐私保护之间实现了良好的平衡，为联邦哈希学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Hash Learning (HL) is an efficient representation learning approach that maps real-valued data into compact binary representations. Traditional HL methods typically require users to upload personal data to a central server, which is incompatible with increasingly stringent data security regulations. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized paradigm for learning globally optimal models without centralizing private data. However, most FL methods rely on transmitting large-scale real-valued gradient information, leading to high communication overhead and potential privacy risks. Integrating HL into FL is a promising solution. Nevertheless, existing HL methods suffer from limited representational capacity of binary codes, which may degrade model accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose a Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor (FHPLF) model. FHPLF introduces three key innovations: (a) replacing real-valued gradient matrices with binary gradient-like matrices, significantly reducing computation, storage, and communication costs while enhancing privacy protection; (b) leveraging Projected Hamming Distance for similarity modeling, which captures the importance of individual binary bits to improve representation capability; and (c) proposing a Secure Binary Gradient Reassembly and Privacy-Enhanced Upload (SBG-PEU) strategy to further reduce the risk of user interaction leakage during transmission. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FHPLF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HL and FL methods, achieving a favorable trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and privacy preservation.

</details>


### [217] [Topology-Informed Neural Networks for Flood Detection in Optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26204)
*Sophia Li,Max Zhao,Raghu G. Raj,Tianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）从卫星图像中提取拓扑描述符，并将其融入神经网络，在SEN12-FLOOD数据集上进行洪水检测。结果表明，拓扑特征本身携带有效的洪水信号，并能增强现有网络的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的洪水检测模型（如ResNet+GRU、ViT）虽然性能优异，但通常是黑箱模型，难以解释其决策边界和内部推理过程。在遥感等安全关键领域，模型的可解释性至关重要。作者尝试利用拓扑数据分析提供一种数学上严谨的可解释框架。

Method: 使用SEN12-FLOOD数据集，从每张图像中提取拓扑特征（拓扑描述符），然后将这些特征融入神经网络中。系统评估了拓扑描述符单独使用以及结合现有网络（如卷积神经网络）的性能。

Result: 拓扑描述符本身携带了有意义的洪水信号，能够独立完成洪水检测任务；同时，将拓扑特征与现有神经网络结合，能够提升检测的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析是一种有效的洪水检测辅助手段，能够提升模型的鲁棒性和可解释性，为遥感领域的灾害监测提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Floods frequently impact regions around the world. Rapid and accurate flood detection is crucial for emergency response and timely mitigation of human and economic loss. The expanding availability of satellite data and advances in artificial intelligence have enhanced monitoring of environmental hazards, but many flood events remain challenging to detect because cloud cover obscures optical satellite imagery. Rambour et al. introduced the SEN12-FLOOD dataset and extracted per-image features using a ResNet-50 convolutional neural network backbone, then fed these features into a gated recurrent unit network to show that temporal information can substantially improve accuracy compared to single-image baselines. More recently, Chamatidis et al. showed that a vision transformer can achieve strong performance with popular convolutional architectures. However, these models typically function as opaque black boxes, making it difficult to interpret their decision boundaries, learned features, and internal reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like remote sensing. In contrast, topological data analysis (TDA) provides a mathematically grounded framework for capturing global structural features of data. TDA has emerged as a powerful tool for analyzing complex imagery, especially imagery with geometrically interpretable structures, of which floods are a prime candidate. In this work, we systematically evaluate topological descriptors for flood detection using the open-source SEN12-FLOOD dataset. By extracting topological features from each image and incorporating them into neural networks, we demonstrate that topological descriptors carry meaningful flood signals independently and complement existing networks to yield more robust and interpretable flood detection systems.

</details>


### [218] [A General Framework for Learning Algebraic Properties from Cayley Graphs using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26212)
*Tal Weissblat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的通用框架，用于直接从Cayley图中学习有限群的代数性质（如阿贝尔性、幂零性和可解性），实验证明该框架能成功区分多种代数性质，表明图表示中蕴含了丰富的代数信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作[1]提出了用GNN预测有限群可解性的方法，但缺乏一个通用的、与具体性质无关的学习框架。本文旨在开发一个属性无关的框架，直接从Cayley图中学习有限群的多种代数性质。

Method: 采用统一的GNN架构和训练流程，以有限群的Cayley图表示作为输入，分别以阿贝尔性、幂零性和可解性作为学习目标，对来自多个群族的有限群数据集进行训练和评估。

Result: 在多个群族的有限群数据集上，该框架成功学习并区分了多种代数性质，验证了从图表示中提取代数信息的可行性。

Conclusion: 有限群的图表示中编码了丰富的代数结构信息，GNN能够有效提取这些信息。该工作为将图表示学习应用于有限群代数性质的研究提供了概念验证。

Abstract: A Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for predicting the solvability of finite groups from their Cayley graph representations was introduced in [1]. In the present work, we generalize this approach and develop a property-independent framework for learning algebraic properties of finite groups directly from Cayley graphs. As representative case studies, we consider abelianity, nilpotency, and solvability. Using a common GNN architecture and training pipeline, we investigate the extent to which algebraic structure can be recovered from graph-based representations alone. Results on a collection of finite groups drawn from several families demonstrate that the framework successfully learns and distinguishes multiple algebraic properties from their associated Cayley graphs. These findings suggest that substantial algebraic information is encoded in graph representations and can be extracted through GNNs. More broadly, the proposed framework provides a proof of concept for applying graph representation learning to the study of algebraic properties of finite groups.

</details>


### [219] [Fast LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26217)
*Yuntian Gao,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fast-LeWM通过动作前缀并行预测替代LeWM的自回归逐步 rollout，在保持甚至提升规划成功率的同时大幅降低规划时间，并有效缓解了长时域下的潜在误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWM等JEPA类模型在进行视觉规划时，需要反复应用单步潜在状态转移模型进行自回归展开，这不仅计算开销大，而且随着规划视域增长会累积大量潜在误差，导致规划质量下降。

Method: 提出Fast-LeWM，将动作前缀作为基本预测单元。给定当前潜在状态和候选动作序列，模型编码动作前缀并并行预测执行这些前缀后到达的未来潜在状态，从而直接建模多步动作的累积效应，无需逐中间状态滚动推理。

Result: 在多个任务上，Fast-LeWM相比LeWM提升了平均成功率，同时显著减少了规划时间，其开环潜在损失更低且随视域增长的速度明显减缓。

Conclusion: 动作前缀级并行预测是一种高效且有效的视觉世界模型规划方案，能够替代传统的自回归逐步展开，在提升性能的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), including recent LeWorldModel (LeWM), have become a promising foundation for reconstruction-free visual world models. For visual planning, however, LeWM evaluates candidate action sequences by repeatedly applying a local one-step latent transition model. This autoregressive rollout makes planning computationally expensive and exposes the predicted trajectory to accumulated latent errors as the horizon grows. We propose Fast LeWorldModel (Fast-LeWM), a fast latent world model that replaces repeated local rollout with action-prefix prediction. Given the current latent and a candidate action sequence, Fast-LeWM encodes its prefixes and predicts the future latents reached after executing those prefixes in parallel. By making action prefixes the basic prediction unit, Fast-LeWM directly models action effects accumulated to different extents over multiple horizons. This prefix-level supervision forces the model to learn how states continuously evolve under different action prefixes, rather than only fitting one-step state transitions. During planning, the predictor can use the last prefix token from the encoded action sequence to evaluate the corresponding future latent without explicitly rolling through each intermediate imagined state. Across multiple tasks, Fast-LeWM improves average success over LeWM while substantially reducing planning time, achieving lower open-loop latent loss whose growth becomes significantly slower as the rollout horizon increases.

</details>


### [220] [Dataset Usage Inference without Shadow Models or Held-out Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26257)
