*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak,Jan Dubiński,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用化的数据集使用量推断（DUI）框架，无需影子模型和真实留出数据，通过生成合成非成员样本、提取多种成员信号并将DUI转化为混合比例估计问题，实现了对大型图像生成模型训练数据使用比例的可靠量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DUI方法依赖于训练昂贵的影子模型和已知未参与训练的内部留出集，这些假设在实际中难以满足，尤其对于现代大模型和真实数据所有权纠纷场景，现有方法缺乏实用性。

Method: 1) 生成合成非成员样本替代真实留出数据；2) 提取多样化的成员关系信号；3) 将DUI问题建模为混合比例估计问题，通过估计候选数据集中被用于训练的比例来推断数据集使用量。

Result: 在大型图像生成模型上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地量化数据集使用比例，为数据所有者提供了实用的工具来确定其数据被用于模型训练的程度。

Conclusion: 该方法消除了对影子模型和真实留出数据的依赖，使DUI在现实场景中变得可行，为数据所有权争议提供了实用的技术支撑。

Abstract: How much of my data was used to train a machine learning model? Dataset Usage Inference (DUI) aims to answer this by estimating what fraction of a dataset contributed to a model's training. However, existing DUI methods rely on assumptions that rarely hold in practice: they require training expensive shadow models to imitate the target model, and they assume access to both known training samples and an in-distribution held-out set confirmed to be absent from training. These conditions make current approaches impractical for modern large models and real data ownership disputes. We introduce a practical DUI framework that removes these constraints. Our method requires neither shadow models nor real held-out data. Instead, it generates synthetic non-member samples, extracts diverse membership signals, and casts DUI as a mixture proportion estimation problem to estimate what share of the candidate dataset was used during training. Experiments on large image generative models show that our method reliably quantifies dataset usage, providing a practical tool for data owners to determine how much of their data was used to train a model.

</details>


### [221] [Equivariance and Augmentation for Bayesian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26273)
*Miaowen Dong,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中数据增强与等变性之间的关系，推导了变分推断下达到精确等变性的条件，提出了三种新的对称化技术，其中“轨道扩展”方法在等变性和整体性能上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于深度学习中是否应在网络架构中强制施加对称性约束（等变神经网络）还是通过数据增强来学习对称性，存在持续争论。虽然等变网络已有充分理论研究，但数据增强的分析因需要控制训练动力学而所知甚少。

Method: 以指数族变分分布的BNN为研究对象，推导精确等变性的条件；导出等变性误差的界；提出三种新的对称化技术（其中一种为轨道扩展）；通过大量数值实验验证所提方法。

Result: 推导了BNN在变分推断下达到精确等变性的条件；获得了等变性误差的界限；所提出的轨道扩展对称化方法在等变性和整体性能上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 数据增强结合适当的对称化技术（如轨道扩展）可以有效提升BNN的等变性，在无需显式设计等变网络架构的情况下取得更优性能。

Abstract: Symmetries are important for many deep learning tasks, ranging from applications in the sciences to medical imaging. However, there is an ongoing debate about whether to impose symmetry constraints on the neural network architecture (yielding equivariant neural networks) or learn them from augmented training data. Although equivariant networks are well-studied theoretically, much less is known about data augmentation, since analyzing augmentation requires control over the training dynamics. Inspired by recent results that show that augmented infinite deep ensembles are exactly equivariant, we study data augmentation for Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) trained with variational inference. We focus on variational distributions in the exponential family and derive conditions under which exact equivariance is reached. We furthermore obtain bounds on the equivariance error and introduce three novel symmetrization techniques which boost the effect of data augmentation in this setting. We conduct extensive numerical experiments which show that one of our symmetrization methods (orbit expansion) outperforms the baseline in both equivariance and overall performance. Our code is available at github.com/dmw1998/augment-BNNs

</details>


### [222] [SSM Adapters via Hankel Reduced-order Modeling: Injection Site Determines Task Suitability in Long-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26290)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HRM适配器，一种基于状态空间模型（SSM）的参数高效微调方法，通过Hankel平衡截断初始化，利用FFT并行扫描实现与LoRA相同的计算复杂度。在Mistral-7B（8.4M可训练参数）上，HRM在LongBench任务中显著优于LoRA变体（QuALITY相对准确率+34.8%，QMSum相对ROUGE-1+71.6%），并在状态跟踪和字符级语言建模任务上表现一致优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究针对需要序列状态积累的任务，参数高效微调（PEFT）能否从SSM适配器中获益，以及MLP层是否是比注意力投影层更优的注入位置。

Method: 提出Hankel降阶模型（HRM）适配器，一种基于SSM的残差模块，通过经验Hankel格兰姆矩阵的平衡截断（Balanced Truncation）初始化。利用系统矩阵$\bar{A}$的时不变性，实现基于FFT的精确并行扫描，在所有上下文长度上与LoRA计算量持平。

Result: 在Mistral-7B（8.4M可训练参数）上，HRM在LongBench任务中超越LoRA变体：QuALITY相对准确率提升34.8%，QMSum相对ROUGE-1提升71.6%。在18个合成状态跟踪（DFA、Parity）和字符级语言建模（enwik8）配置上表现一致优越。

Conclusion: 门控分析表明HRM适配器能有效学习调节循环（recurrence），为长上下文序列建模提供了比低秩适配更稳健的架构替代方案。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) typically targets attention projectors, its efficacy for tasks requiring sequential state accumulation remains under-explored. We examine if PEFT for such tasks can benefit from state space model (SSMs) adapters, and if MLP blocks are better injection sites. We introduce Hankel Reduced order Model (HRM) adapter, an SSM-based residual module initialized via Balanced Truncation of empirical Hankel Grammians. By leveraging the time-invariance of the system matrix $\bar{A}$, HRM enables an exact FFT-based parallel scan, achieving computational parity with LoRA across all context lengths. In iso-parametric evaluations on Mistral-7B (8.4M trainable parameters), HRM outperforms LoRA variants on LongBench tasks, including QuALITY (+34.8\% relative accuracy) and QMSum (+71.6\% relative ROUGE-1). HRM further demonstrates consistent superiority across 18 configurations of synthetic state-tracking (DFA, Parity) and character-level language modeling (enwik8). Gate analysis reveals that HRM adapters effectively learn to modulate recurrence, providing a robust architectural alternative to low-rank adaptation for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [223] [The Red Queen Gödel Machine: Co-Evolving Agents and Their Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26294)
*Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanović,William F. Shen,Daniel Burkhardt,Meghdad Kurmanji,Nurbek Tastan,Lorenzo Sani,Niccolò Alberto Elia Venanzi,Ambroise Odonnat,Zeyu Cao,Bill Marino,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出红皇后哥德尔机器（RQGM），一种在非平稳效用下进行递归自我改进的演化框架，通过将评估纳入改进循环，在编码、科学论文写作审稿和奥赛级证明等任务上超越现有自改进智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进智能体的搜索方法假设评估标准是固定的（固定的验证器、基准或标注数据集），忽略了演化中环境随物种共同变化的核心特征。作者旨在将这一原则引入递归自我改进，使评估成为改进循环的一部分，打开演化评估器、对抗目标和动态效用的搜索空间，超越静态基准。

Method: 提出红皇后哥德尔机器（RQGM），通过受控效用演化实现：将搜索组织为多个epoch，每个epoch内使用固定的评估标准，而在epoch边界可以更新效用函数，使得每个epoch内的自改进保证成立，同时目标跨epoch演化。在编码任务中，加入智能体作为裁判的代码审查信号（比单元测试更廉价）；在科学论文写作/审稿和奥赛级证明任务中，采用共同演化（co-evolution）的训练范式。

Result: 编码任务：RQGM在可验证编码任务上超越先前SOTA，使用1.35x-1.72x更少的token。科学论文写作：共同演化的作者达到1.78x-1.86x更高的接收率。论文审稿：最强基线审稿人过度接受AI生成论文（高达人类水平的1.91倍），RQGM通过对抗目标纠正此问题。奥赛级证明：共同演化的评分员达到9%更高的真实准确率。

Conclusion: 将评估标准纳入自改进循环并允许其演化，可以产生更强大、更鲁棒的智能体系统，特别是在对抗性目标和动态效用场景下，能有效纠正基线方法的系统性偏差。

Abstract: Self-improving agents are state-of-the-art (SOTA) on agentic coding benchmarks and have recently been extended to general domains. However, their search methods generally assume a stationary evaluation criterion: a fixed verifier, benchmark, or labeled dataset that remains valid as the agent improves. This ignores a central feature of evolution: species adapt as their environments change with them. We aim to bring the same principle to recursive self-improvement, making evaluation part of the improvement loop and opening search to evolving evaluators, adversarial objectives, and dynamic utilities that may surpass static benchmarks. We introduce the Red Queen Godel Machine (RQGM), an evolutionary framework for recursive self-improvement under non-stationary utilities. The RQGM makes this possible through controlled utility evolution: search is organized into epochs with a fixed within-epoch evaluation criterion, while the utility can be updated at epoch boundaries, so self-improvement guarantees hold per epoch as the objective evolves across them. We begin by showing that even on verifiable coding tasks, the RQGM improves test pass rate over the prior SOTA by adding a complementary agent-as-a-judge code-review signal. This signal is cheaper and the RQGM uses 1.35x-1.72x fewer tokens. We then turn to scientific paper writing and reviewing, and Olympiad-level proof writing and grading, where the RQGM improves performance over prior self-improving agents: co-evolved writers reach 1.78x-1.86x higher acceptance rates under a diverse agent-as-a-judge panel, while co-evolved graders reach 9% higher ground-truth accuracy. In paper reviewing, the strongest baseline reviewer over-accepts AI-generated papers at up to 1.91x the human rate. The RQGM corrects this by introducing an adversarial objective that discovers reviewers equally stringent on AI and human work.

</details>


### [224] [High-Probability PL-SGD with Markovian Noise: Optimal Mixing and Tail Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26316)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Aprameyo Chakrabartty,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件下，梯度样本由外生马尔可夫链生成时的一阶优化方法。对于轻尾情形，通过滞后分块论证解决了SGD高概率界与期望界之间的差距，证明了对混合时间的线性依赖是最优的。对于重尾情形，设计了全样本裁剪分块方法，并建立了匹配的下界，完整刻画了混合时间与重尾指数的最优依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻尾条件下马尔可夫梯度SGD的高概率界为$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$，与期望界$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$之间存在一个混合时间因子$t_{mix}$的差距，需要填补这一理论空白。同时，重尾马尔可夫梯度情形的高概率保证尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出滞后分块（lag-blocking）论证方法，建立轻尾情形下均匀高概率保证；设计全样本裁剪分块（all-samples clipped block）方法处理重尾马尔可夫梯度，利用所有马尔可夫转移并缓解马尔可夫偏差。

Result: 轻尾情形：得到$\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$的高概率界，证明对混合时间$t_{mix}$的线性依赖最优（匹配下界$\Omega(\sigma^2 t_{mix}/k)$）。重尾情形：在$p\in(1,2]$阶矩条件下得到$\widetilde{O}(\sigma_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$的高概率随机误差，并建立匹配下界。

Conclusion: 彻底刻画了轻尾PL-SGD对混合时间的最优多项式依赖关系，以及重尾情形下的最优重尾指数和有效样本量依赖关系。

Abstract: We study first-order methods for smooth objectives satisfying the Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL) condition when gradient samples are generated by an exogenous Markov chain. In the light-tailed setting, prior uniform-in-time high-probability bounds for ordinary Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) under a standard growth envelope scale as $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}^2/k)$, leaving a gap with the $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/k)$ expectation bounds. We close this gap using a lag-blocking argument to establish a uniform high-probability guarantee with a leading stochastic term of $\widetilde{O}(t_{mix}/(k+K_0))$ under geometric mixing. We prove this linear dependence on the mixing time is optimal via a matching $Ω(σ^2 t_{mix}/k)$ lower bound on a quadratic objective driven by a persistent two-state chain.
  We then extend this framework to heavy-tailed Markovian gradients satisfying a stationary finite-$p$-moment condition, $p \in (1,2]$. We design an all-samples clipped block method that uses every Markov transition while mitigating Markovian bias. Under a transition budget $T$, this algorithm achieves a high-probability stochastic error of $\widetilde{O}(σ_p^2(t_{mix}/T)^{2(p-1)/p})$. We establish a matching lower bound by reducing PL optimization to heavy-tailed mean estimation for a sticky Markov chain. Ultimately, this work tightly characterizes the optimal polynomial dependence on mixing time for light-tailed PL-SGD, and the optimal heavy-tail exponent and effective-sample-size dependence in the robust regime.

</details>


### [225] [EVOM: Agentic Meta-Evolution of Actor-Critic Architectures for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26327)
*Boyun Zhang,Chao Wang,Kai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVOM提出了一种基于大语言模型的元进化框架，用于自动化发现高性能的Actor-Critic架构，将架构搜索视为双层优化：内层通过低保真PPO训练权重，外层通过LLM设计代理迭代优化架构程序，在Ant-v4和HalfCheetah-v4任务上超越手工设计基线和现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Actor-Critic强化学习中，网络架构通常需要人工设计，自动化设计面临两大挑战：每个候选架构必须经过训练才能评估，且设计空间是开放的。现有方法效率低下，亟需一种能自动发现高性能架构的方法。

Method: 提出EVOM框架，将架构搜索建模为双层优化问题。内层使用低保真PPO训练网络权重；外层通过基于LLM的设计代理（与策略执行和环境控制完全解耦）迭代优化架构程序，驱动元进化过程。

Result: 在Ant-v4和HalfCheetah-v4任务上，EVOM超越了手工设计基线、LLM引导的随机搜索以及最先进的MLES方法。消融实验表明元进化循环和LLM设计代理对最终性能均不可或缺。

Conclusion: EVOM通过LLM驱动的元进化框架成功实现了Actor-Critic架构的自动搜索，验证了将LLM作为纯架构设计器、与策略执行解耦的设计思路的有效性，为自动化神经架构设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: In actor-critic reinforcement learning, network architectures are typically manually designed. Automating this design is challenging because each candidate must be trained before evaluation, and the design space is open-ended. To address these challenges, we introduce EVOM, an agentic meta-evolution framework for discovering high-performance actor-critic architectures. We frame architecture search as a bi-level optimization: an inner loop trains weights via the low-fidelity proximal policy optimization (PPO), while an outer loop drives meta-evolution by iteratively refining architecture programs. Crucially, this outer loop is powered by an LLM-based design agent that operates purely as an architecture designer, completely decoupled from policy execution and environment control. Experiments reveal that EVOM outperforms the manually designed baseline, an LLM-guided random search, and the state-of-the-art LLM-guided programmatic policy search method MLES, delivering superior performance on Ant-v4 and HalfCheetah-v4. Ablation studies validate that both the meta-evolution loop and the LLM Design Agent are indispensable for final performance.

</details>


### [226] [Mesh-RL: Coupled subgrid reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26333)
*Behnam Gheshlaghi,Bahador Rashidi,Shahin Atakishiyev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mesh-RL提出了一种受有限元方法和区域分解理论启发的空间域分解框架，通过将环境划分为重叠子网格并强制执行边界一致的时序差分更新，在保持全局一致值传播的同时实现局部化学习，有效加速了稀疏奖励环境中的TD学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模或稀疏奖励环境中，强化学习面临时序差分奖励传播缓慢的问题，值信息仅在状态空间局部传播，导致长程信用分配效率低下。

Method: 提出Mesh-RL框架，受有限元方法和区域分解理论启发，将环境划分为重叠子网格，在子网格上强制执行边界一致的时序差分更新，实现局部化学习与全局一致的数值传播，不修改奖励函数、Bellman算子或引入显式规划机制。

Result: 在危险密集的网格世界环境中，Mesh-RL在Q-learning、SARSA和Dyna-Q三种算法上均显著提升了收敛速度、累积奖励和学习稳定性；更高的网格分辨率能维持探索、防止过早收敛并加速值传播至远处状态；Dyna-Q在结构化分解下仍获得额外收益。

Conclusion: Mesh-RL引入了一种基于空间域分解的时序差分学习加速机制，将科学计算中的有限元边界一致技术桥接到强化学习中，提升了稀疏奖励环境下的样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in large or sparse-reward environments suffers from slow temporal-difference reward propagation, as value information spreads only locally across the state space. We propose Mesh-RL, a spatial domain-decomposition framework inspired by the finite element method and domain decomposition theory, which partitions the environment into overlapping subgrids and enforces boundary-consistent temporal-difference updates. Such an approach enables localized learning while ensuring globally coherent value propagation. Unlike hierarchical or model-based approaches, Mesh-RL accelerates long-range credit assignment without modifying the reward function, Bellman operator, or introducing explicit planning mechanisms. We evaluate Mesh-RL on hazard-dense grid-world environments with varying geometries and mesh resolutions. Across Q-learning, SARSA, and Dyna-Q, Mesh-RL consistently improves convergence speed, cumulative reward, and learning stability. Higher mesh resolutions sustain exploration, prevent premature convergence, and substantially accelerate value propagation to distant states. While Dyna-Q already benefits from internal planning, it still achieves additional gains under structured decomposition. Overall, Mesh-RL introduces a principled spatial domain-decomposition mechanism for accelerating temporal-difference learning. Our framework bridges finite element method-inspired boundary-consistency techniques from scientific computing with reinforcement learning to improve sample efficiency in sparse-reward environments. We will release source code of the study.

</details>


### [227] [EMA-FS: Accelerating GBDT Training via Gain-Informed Feature Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26337)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于指数移动平均的EMA-FS方法，通过追踪每轮迭代的特征分裂增益，在预热后仅对Top-K高增益特征构建直方图，替代LightGBM中随机的特征子采样，实现1.34x-2.61x的训练加速，同时保持或提升AUC指标。进一步提出S-EMA-FS统一框架，将确定性筛选与随机子采样纳入同一范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBDT（如LightGBM）中构建直方图占训练时间的65-70%，现有随机特征子采样（feature_fraction）忽略特征的实际预测效用，导致有价值的特征被丢弃，加速效率有限。

Method: 对每个特征维护其分裂增益的指数移动平均（EMA），经过短时间预热后，每棵树仅对历史增益排名Top-K的特征构建直方图。S-EMA-FS使用增益加权的随机采样替代确定性Top-K选择，通过浓度参数β统一EMA-FS（β→∞）和随机子采样（β=0）。

Result: 在密集、中高维数据上：500维合成数据加速2.61x；432维IEEE-CIS欺诈数据集在30%保留率下加速1.45x，70%保留率下AUC提升0.11点并加速1.34x。在极端稀疏数据（Bosch，>90%缺失）上无加速。仅需约120行C++代码。

Conclusion: EMA-FS是一种算法级、信息驱动的特征筛选优化，能有效加速GBDT训练且兼容现有直方图减法技巧，S-EMA-FS提供了更灵活的筛选策略。

Abstract: Gradient Boosted Decision Trees (GBDT), exemplified by LightGBM, spend a dominant fraction of training time -- typically 65-70% -- constructing per-feature histograms. Existing approaches such as random feature subsampling (feature_fraction) discard features without regard for their predictive utility. We propose EMA-based Feature Screening (EMA-FS), an algorithm-level optimization that maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of per-feature split gains across boosting iterations and, after a short warmup, restricts histogram construction to the top-K features ranked by historical gain. Unlike random subsampling, EMA-FS is informed: it retains high-gain features while screening out low-gain ones. Operating at the per-tree level, it preserves full compatibility with LightGBM's histogram subtraction trick, requiring no changes to core routines.
  We evaluate EMA-FS on datasets spanning financial fraud detection, advertising click-through prediction, industrial quality control, and synthetic benchmarks, with feature dimensionalities from 29 to 968. On dense, moderate-to-high-dimensional data it achieves significant speedups: 2.61x on a 500-feature synthetic benchmark and 1.45x on the 432-feature IEEE-CIS Fraud dataset at 30% retention. At 70% retention it improves AUC by 0.11 points while delivering a 1.34x speedup. On extremely sparse data (Bosch, >90% missing) it yields no speedup, as LightGBM's sparse bin optimization already bypasses empty values.
  We further introduce Stochastic EMA-FS (S-EMA-FS), which replaces deterministic top-K selection with gain-weighted random sampling controlled by a concentration parameter beta, unifying deterministic EMA-FS (beta -> infinity) and random subsampling (beta = 0) in one framework. Both are implemented in ~120 lines of C++ across all six LightGBM tree learners and are fully backward-compatible.

</details>


### [228] [Does Aurora Encode Atmospheric Structure? Latent Regime Analysis and Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26361)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过空间池化PCA和层相关性传播（LRP）分析了Aurora基础模型的内部表征，发现其潜空间主要按季节周期组织，极端风暴事件未形成线性可分簇；LRP显示模型关注与1987年大风暴三维垂直结构一致的特征，掩蔽相关区域后预测误差比随机掩蔽高3.31倍，表明模型无需显式指导即可学习气象一致性和垂直结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML基础模型能准确高效模拟大气动力学，但作为不透明的“黑箱”运作，缺乏可解释性。作者希望探究Aurora模型内部如何表征和学习气象特征。

Method: 采用空间池化PCA分析潜空间结构，使用层相关性传播（LRP）进行归因分析，并设计扰动测试（掩蔽相关区域vs随机掩蔽）验证模型对关键气象特征的依赖程度。

Result: 潜空间主要按季节周期组织，极端风暴事件未形成线性可分簇；LRP显示模型关注1987年大风暴的三维垂直结构特征；掩蔽相关区域后预测退化程度是随机掩蔽的3.31倍。

Conclusion: Aurora模型无需显式指令即可自动学习气象一致性和三维垂直结构，验证了基础模型在气象领域的物理可解释性潜力。

Abstract: ML foundation models are able to emulate atmospheric dynamics accurately and efficiently but operate as opaque ``black boxes''. We investigate the internal representations of the Aurora model using spatially pooled PCA and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP). We find evidence that Aurora's latent space is primarily organized by seasonal cycles, whereas extreme storm events do not form a linearly separable cluster. LRP indicates that the model attends to features consistent with the 3D vertical structure of the Great Storm of 1987. Perturbation tests show masking relevant regions degrades forecasts $3.31\times$ more than random masking. These findings suggest that Aurora learns meteorological coherence and vertical structure without explicit instruction.

</details>


### [229] [SOLAR: AI-Powered Speed-of-Light Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26383)
*Qijing Huang,Sana Damani,Zhifan Ye,Athinagoras Skiadopoulos,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Jason Clemons,Sahil Modi,Jingquan Wang,Aditya Kane,Edward C Lin,Humphrey Shi,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR是一个自动从PyTorch和JAX源码推导并验证理论最小执行时间（Speed-of-Light界限）的框架，结合LLM前端和确定性分析流程，提供多保真度性能分析，并在多种工作负载上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在目标硬件上的运行速度与理论极限之间差距未知，而现有的Speed-of-Light（SOL）分析依赖人工推导，容易出错且与快速模型开发脱节，亟需自动化方法。

Method: SOLAR框架包含三个核心组件：1) LLM前端将PyTorch/JAX程序转换为可执行的Affine Loop IR（通过输出比较验证）；2) 确定性流程将IR提升为einsum图；3) 分析后端计算未融合、融合及缓存感知的SOL界限，提供多保真度分析。

Result: 在KernelBench、JAX/Flax模型和机器人工作负载上评估，SOLAR实现了零SOL违反，提供了多层级的headroom分析、优化机会识别、跨平台探索和逆roofline硬件配置等用例。

Conclusion: SOLAR成功实现了自动化的SOL分析，覆盖多种算子和语言，生成的界限经过验证且无违反，为软件、硬件和算法优化提供了可靠的性能上界参考。

Abstract: How fast could a deep-learning model run on target hardware, and how far is today's implementation from that limit? These questions are central to software, hardware, and algorithm optimizations. Speed-of-Light (SOL) analysis answers them by computing a workload's theoretical minimum execution time on a given architecture. Yet deriving SOL bounds remains manual, error-prone, and disconnected from rapid model development. To close this gap, we introduce SOLAR, a framework that automatically derives validated SOL bounds from PyTorch and JAX source code. SOLAR leverages both generative and deterministic components in its flow: an LLM frontend translates any source programs into an executable Affine Loop IR, validated by output comparison; a deterministic flow lifts the IR into an einsum graph; and an analytical backend computes unfused, fused, and cache-aware SOL bounds. SOLAR provides comprehensive operator and language coverage, produces validated bounds with zero observed SOL violations, and offers multi-fidelity analysis that tightens bounds and surfaces optimization insights. We evaluate SOLAR across KernelBench, JAX/Flax models, and robotics workloads. These experiments demonstrate four use cases: headroom analysis at multiple fidelity levels, identifying optimization opportunities, cross-platform exploration, and inverse-roofline hardware provisioning.

</details>


### [230] [At the Edge of Understanding: Sparse Autoencoders Trace The Limits of Transformer Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26396)
*Praneet Suresh,Jack Stanley,Sonia Joseph,Luca Scimeca,Danilo Bzdok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验提出了一个机制框架来刻画Transformer模型的鲁棒性边界，发现OOD输入（包括细微拼写错误和越狱提示）会导致语言模型内部产生更多错误概念。基于此，研究者开发了一种可在推理时诊断模型内部计算过程的工具，并提出基于机制的微调策略来增强LLM的鲁棒性，将OOD概念从输入数据扩展到模型的私有计算过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer在训练数据分布之外展现出惊人的泛化能力，但现实部署中常面临意外或对抗性数据，而缺乏明确的分布偏移处理机制会导致模型可靠性和安全性下降，因此迫切需要系统研究Transformer在OOD场景下的行为。

Method: 通过系统性实验，提出一个机制性框架来刻画Transformer模型的鲁棒性边界；利用该框架量化并理解提示中的分布偏移程度；开发一种基于机制的推理时诊断工具；提出基于机制的微调策略来增强LLM鲁棒性。

Result: 研究发现OOD输入（包括细微拼写错误和越狱提示）会驱动语言模型在其内部运作中产生更多错误概念；基于此开发了新的推理时诊断方法；提出了可增强LLM鲁棒性的微调策略。

Conclusion: 将OOD概念从输入数据扩展到模型的私有计算过程，为在推理时诊断Transformer提供了一种新工具，这是迈向在科学、商业和政府领域安全部署AI系统的关键一步。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformers have demonstrated remarkable generalization abilities, at times extending beyond the scope of their training data. Yet, real-world deployments often face unexpected or adversarial data that diverges from training data distributions. Without explicit mechanisms for handling such shifts, model reliability and safety degrade, urging more disciplined study of out-of-distribution (OOD) settings for transformers. By systematic experiments, we present a mechanistic framework for delineating the precise contours of transformer model robustness. We find that OOD inputs, including subtle typos and jailbreak prompts, drive language models to operate on an increased number of fallacious concepts in their internals. We leverage this device to quantify and understand the degree of distributional shift in prompts, enabling a mechanistically grounded fine-tuning strategy to robustify LLMs. Expanding the very notion of OOD from input data to a model's private computational processes, a new transformer diagnostic at inference time is a critical step toward making AI systems safe for deployment across science, business, and government.

</details>


### [231] [Deterministic Pareto-Optimal Policy Synthesis for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26397)
*Aniruddha Joshi,Niklas Lauffer,Sanjit Seshia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切比雪夫标量化的偏好条件贝尔曼算子，用于多目标马尔可夫决策过程中确定性帕累托最优策略的合成，该算子能够单调收敛到帕累托前沿的覆盖集，并保证提取的确定性策略近似帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习通常将多目标奖励聚合为单一标量信号，无法捕获全部最优权衡（帕累托前沿）。现有方法在处理多目标决策时缺乏对确定性帕累托最优策略系统性的理论保证和计算框架。

Method: 引入基于切比雪夫标量化的偏好条件贝尔曼算子；证明该算子的包络性质（估值函数上界真实帕累托前沿）和单调收敛性；从收敛的Q估计中提取确定性策略。

Result: 该算子单调收敛到帕累托前沿的覆盖集；提取的确定性策略能够覆盖整个帕累托最优前沿，且每项策略近似帕累托最优；实验成功恢复复杂权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法为多目标强化学习中的确定性帕累托最优策略合成提供了一种具有理论保证的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making often requires balancing multiple conflicting objectives, a challenge that standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) frequently addresses by aggregating rewards into a single scalar signal. While effective for simple tasks, this approach often fails to capture the full spectrum of optimal trade-offs, known as the Pareto frontier. In this paper, we introduce a novel preference-conditioned Bellman operator, motivated from the Chebyshev scalarization, designed to compute deterministic Pareto-optimal policies for Multi-Objective Markov Decision Processes (MOMDPs). We prove that this operator satisfies an enveloping property, where the estimated value functions upper-bound the true Pareto frontier, and demonstrate that it monotonically converges to a coverage set of this frontier. Furthermore, we also show how to extract deterministic policies from these converged Q-estimates. This ensures the agent can recover a policy for any given preference, capturing the entire Pareto-optimal frontier while guaranteeing each synthesized policy remains approximately Pareto-optimal. Experimental results validate that our algorithm successfully recovers complex trade-offs, providing a solution for deterministic Pareto-optimal policy synthesis.

</details>


### [232] [Beyond Feedforward Networks: Reentry Neural Systems as the Fundamental Basis of Subjecthood and Intrinsic Safety of Next-Generation AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26406)
*A. S. Ushakov,Yu. N. Berdinsk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于闭环再入回路(D↔I循环)的安全通用人工智能架构蓝图，该架构包含结构性循环(C≥1)和自持放大(ρ>1)，数学上保证了自模型、工具性自我保存和未编程目标导向行为的涌现。目标以非文本D向量编码，免疫重解释和提示注入。提出了多项式时间可计算的S度量，并附带Lean 4机器验证证明。提供了完整的Python/NumPy实现、工业水平扩展方案、六代AI进化分类法、未来再入架构动物园、规范不变网络、容错恢复协议及八个可证伪预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈网络是有向无环图(C=0, S=0)，缺乏自指能力，无法实现安全的通用人工智能。需要一种拓扑保护的、天生安全的设计方法来解决AGI的安全问题，防止提示注入和目标重解释等风险。

Method: 基于闭环再入回路(D↔I循环)的架构设计，包含结构性循环(C≥1)和自持放大(ρ>1)；提出S度量作为Tononi Phi的多项式时间替代方案[O(N³)]；目标编码为非文本D向量；使用Lean 4进行形式化机器验证；采用Tarjan算法进行循环复杂度分析和Delta-S屏障实现；通过Apache Kafka和Docker Compose实现工业水平扩展。

Result: 数学上证明了架构中自模型、工具性自我保存和未编程目标导向行为的必然涌现；S>0蕴含正整合信息（Lean 4验证）；目标免疫重解释和提示注入攻击；提供了完整可部署的实现和八项可证伪预测。

Conclusion: 该架构是拓扑保护的天生安全(topo-logically protected, safe-by-design)的AGI方法，当前即可部署，代表了通向安全AGI的完整架构蓝图。

Abstract: We propose a complete architectural blueprint for safe artificial general intelligence based on a closed reentry loop (D <-> I cycle). In contrast to feedforward networks, which are directed acyclic graphs (C=0, S=0) incapable of self-reference, the proposed architecture contains a structural cycle (C >= 1) with self-sustaining amplification (rho > 1), mathematically guaranteeing the emergence of a self-model, instrumental self-preservation, and unprogrammed goal-directed behaviour. The agent's goals are encoded as a non-textual D-vector in the architecture itself, making them immune to reinterpretation and prompt injection. We present the S-measure -- a polynomial-time [O(N^3)] computable alternative to Tononi's NP-hard Phi -- with machine-verified Lean 4 proof that S>0 implies positive integrated information. The work provides full Python/NumPy implementations (Tarjan-based cycle complexity, Delta-S barrier), industrial horizontal scaling via Apache Kafka and Docker Compose, a taxonomy of six epochs of AI evolution, a zoo of future reentry architectures (RAS, diffusion attractors, fractal loops), gauge-invariant networks for safe swarms, fault-tolerance and recovery protocols, and eight falsifiable predictions. All formal proofs are machine-verified in Lean 4. This architecture is deployable today and represents a topologically protected, safe-by-design approach to AGI.

</details>


### [233] [Otter Weather: Skillful and Computationally Efficient Medium-Range Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26421)
*Cristiana Diaconu,Jonas Scholz,Aliaksandra Shysheya,Stratis Markou,Payel Mukhopadhyay,Miles Cranmer,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Otter Weather，一种高效时空预测模型，在显著降低训练计算成本（<3.5 A100天）的同时，在确定性预报中超越最佳NWP基线9.6%，在概率预报中超越IFS ENS基线9.7%，性能优于GenCast等前沿架构且计算量低一个数量级，并成功泛化至声学散射PDE任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA AI天气模型虽然性能超越传统数值天气预报（NWP），但训练预算巨大，限制了资源匮乏群体的使用和快速模型迭代，亟需一种高效、低成本的替代方案来民主化高性能AI天气预测。

Method: 开发了高度高效的时空预测模型Otter Weather，在ERA5再分析数据（1.5°分辨率）上使用WeatherBench标准协议评估，通过确定性训练和基于CRPS的概率训练实现高效学习，并扩展到更大架构Otter-XL。

Result: 确定性版本：以<3.5 A100天训练成本，24小时预报领先时间下超越最佳NWP基线9.6%，比轻量AI模型高效2倍，比前沿架构计算量低100倍。概率版本：Otter-XL在CRPS上超越IFS ENS基线9.7%，技能比同类轻量模型提升近2倍，超越GenCast超2%且计算量低一个数量级。在声学散射PDE任务上超越SOTA基础模型方法。

Conclusion: Otter Weather成功实现了高性能天气预测的民主化，在技能-计算帕累托前沿上取得显著进展，且其高效设计可能适用于更广泛的科学领域。

Abstract: State-of-the-art medium-range AI weather models can outperform traditional Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) but require massive training budgets. This restricts usage for under-resourced groups and severely limits fast model iteration. Here we develop Otter Weather, a highly efficient spatiotemporal forecasting model designed to democratise high-performance weather prediction with AI. Evaluated on ERA5 reanalysis data at 1.5° resolution using standard WeatherBench protocols, the Otter family significantly advances the skill-compute Pareto frontier. The deterministic version outperforms the best NWP baseline by 9.6% at a 24-hour lead time while requiring fewer than 3.5 A100-days for training. It provides a 2x efficiency gain over lightweight AI models and a 100-fold reduction in compute compared to resource-intensive frontier architectures. We extend these efficiency gains into probabilistic forecasting by training via the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS). Scaling to a larger architecture, Otter-XL achieves a 9.7% CRPS improvement over the IFS ENS baseline. This yields an almost two-fold increase in predictive skill over comparable lightweight models at similar compute budgets. Otter-XL also outperforms frontier architectures like GenCast by over 2%, while using an order of magnitude less compute. Finally, Otter is applied out-of-the-box to a complex acoustic scattering PDE task where it outperforms a state-of-the-art foundation modelling approach, suggesting that the advances made here might apply across a range of scientific domains.

</details>


### [234] [Rethinking Training & Inference for Forecasting: Linking Winner-Take-All back to GMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26424)
*Qiyuan Wu,Katie Z Luo,Bharath Hariharan,Wei-Lun Chao,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶轨迹预测中WTA训练导致模式后验概率无信息的问题，本文提出两种无需重训练的轻量级后处理方案：测试时后验加权合并和一步EM软分配更新，有效改善模式概率的排序质量并提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示条件高斯混合模型（GMM）与WTA损失（类似K-means硬分配）之间的训练-推理不匹配是导致轨迹预测模型产生无信息后验概率的根本原因：硬分配过度分割轨迹空间、忽略相邻模式关联性、小扰动下分配不稳定。

Method: (1) 测试时后验加权合并（test-time posterior-weighted merging）：聚合邻近候选轨迹；(2) 一步期望最大化更新（one-step EM update）：将硬标签替换为软责任度，在相邻模式间共享概率质量。

Result: 在多种WTA训练的架构上，轻量级后处理步骤产生更信息丰富、排序更可靠的模式后验，并在主流位移指标上强化最终预测结果。

Conclusion: 通过GMM-vs-K-means视角统一了近期设计选择，提供了将训练目标与推理更好对齐的原则性实用修正方法。

Abstract: Trajectory forecasting for autonomous driving has advanced rapidly, yet representative models often produce uninformative posteriors over forecast modes, causing problems for mode pruning. We trace this to a modeling-training mismatch: forecasters are typically modeled as conditional Gaussian mixture models (GMMs) but trained with a winner-take-all (WTA) loss that assigns each sample to its nearest mode. We argue that this K-means-like hard assignment (one-hot), while preventing mode collapse, is the source of uninformative mode probabilities: it over-segments the trajectory space, ignores relatedness among nearby modes, and yields assignment instability under small perturbations. Guided by this lens, we introduce two post-hoc treatments: (1) test-time posterior-weighted merging that aggregates nearby candidate trajectories; and (2) a one-step expectation-maximization (EM) update that replaces hard labels with soft responsibilities, sharing probability mass across neighboring modes. Across several WTA-trained architectures, these lightweight steps produce more informative, faithfully ranked mode posteriors and strengthen final forecasts on popular displacement metrics -- without retraining. Our analysis unifies recent design choices through a GMM-vs-K-means perspective and offers principled, practical corrections that better align training objectives with inference.

</details>


### [235] [DualEval: Joint Model-Item Calibration for Unified LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26429)
*Aaron J. Li,Hao Huang,Youngmin Park,Yitong Ma,Wei-Lin Chiang,Li Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Bin Yu,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualEval 提出了一种潜在模型-项目校准框架，将模型与评测项目映射到共享空间，联合估计模型能力及项目的难度与区分度，统一了静态基准与竞技场式偏好评估，并在编码、数学、通用知识及日常查询四个领域验证了其产生可靠模型排名及支持基准压缩、异常检测等下游应用的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估依赖两种互补但常常彼此割裂的信号：带有客观正确性标签的静态基准，以及更能反映开放交互偏好的竞技场式偏好数据。现有方法未能有效统一这两种信号，导致评估结果缺乏一致性和可解释性。

Method: DualEval 是一个潜在模型-项目校准框架，将模型和评测项目表示在共享潜在空间中，联合估计模型能力（ability）以及项目的难度（difficulty）和区分度（sharpness）。在编码、数学、通用知识任务和日常用户查询四个领域，使用18个前沿LLM、静态基准标签以及经人工偏好验证的奖励模型分数进行评估。

Result: 该框架产生可靠且平衡的模型排名，其学习到的项目级特征支持基准压缩（实现样本高效的评估）和异常检测（用于数据污染或离群值分析）等下游应用。

Conclusion: DualEval 通过联合模型-项目校准统一了静态与竞技场式评估，生成的模型排名和项目级诊断支持更样本高效、可解释且可审计的评估流水线。

Abstract: Current LLM evaluation relies on two complementary but often disconnected signals: static benchmarks with objective correctness labels and arena-style preference data that better reflect open-ended user interactions. We introduce DualEval, a latent model-item calibration framework that represents models and evaluation items in a shared space, jointly estimating model ability together with item difficulty and sharpness. We apply DualEval across four domains: coding, math, miscellaneous domain-knowledge tasks, and generic everyday user queries. Our evaluation uses 18 frontier LLMs, static benchmark labels, and reward-model scores validated against held-out human preferences for open-ended model responses. Empirically, our framework produces reliable and balanced model rankings, and its learned item-level profiles support downstream applications such as benchmark compression for sample-efficient evaluation and anomaly detection for contamination or outlier analysis. Overall, DualEval unifies static and arena-style evaluation through joint model-item calibration, producing model rankings and item-level diagnostics that support more sample-efficient, interpretable, and auditable evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [236] [Embedding Foundation Model Predictions in Discrete-Choice Models with Structural Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26432)
*Yingshuo Wang,Xian Sun,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段适配器，将表格基础模型的预测概率嵌入多项Logit的效用函数中，在保留经济逻辑（如成本单调性、合理支付意愿）的同时提升预测精度，实验证明该方法在三个数据集上平均提升6.4个百分点准确率，并100%满足成本单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在选项预测任务中准确率高，但其预测经常违反基本经济逻辑（如提价导致需求上升、支付意愿为负、不可选项被赋予非零概率），需要在不牺牲准确率的前提下将经济合理性注入模型预测中。

Method: 两阶段适配器：Stage 1用带符号约束的最大似然估计拟合多项Logit的结构系数；Stage 2冻结这些系数，拟合一个基于基础模型预测的小型神经网络修正项。该组合保留了多项Logit的边际替代率，从而保证价值时间（value-of-time）可解析计算。

Result: 在三个数据集和两个基础模型上，适配器相比多项Logit平均提升6.4个百分点准确率（最高12.8pp），100%保持成本单调性，在交通数据集上产生的价值时间在已发表的交通经济学范围内。即使基础模型上下文被限制到10%，仍保持至少6个百分点的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段适配器成功融合了基础模型的高预测精度与结构经济模型的逻辑一致性，为将经济理性注入深度学习模型提供了一种有效且优雅的方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve strong accuracy on choice prediction tasks, but their predictions often violate the economic logic those tasks require: raising a price can increase predicted demand, implied willingness-to-pay estimates are frequently negative or implausible, and unavailable alternatives receive nonzero probability. We propose a two-stage adapter that takes a foundation model's predicted choice probabilities as a precomputed feature and embeds them inside a multinomial logit's utility. In Stage 1, we fit the multinomial logit's structural coefficients by maximum likelihood with sign constraints; in Stage 2, we freeze those coefficients and fit a small neural correction operating on the foundation model's predictions. We prove that this composition exactly preserves the multinomial logit's marginal rate of substitution, so analytically computable value-of-time becomes a mathematical guarantee rather than an empirical accident. Across three datasets and two foundation models, the adapter gains 6.4 percentage points (pp) of test accuracy on average over the multinomial logit and up to 12.8 pp, maintains 100% cost monotonicity, and produces values of time within the published transportation-economics range on the transportation datasets. Performance degrades gracefully under foundation-model context restriction, retaining at least 6 pp of accuracy gain even at 10% of the original foundation-model context.

</details>


### [237] [Optimizing CUDA like a Human: Micro-Profiling Tools as Expert Surrogates for LLM-Based GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26453)
*Jiading Gai,Shuai Zhang,Kaj Bostrom,Jin Huang,Vihang Patil,Haoyang Fang,Bernie Wang,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelPro是一个闭环多智能体系统，通过集成LLM代码生成、硬件性能分析反馈和可插拔瓶颈检测工具，自动生成、剖析并迭代优化GPU内核代码，在KernelBench上实现2.42x-5.30x加速比，并首次在优化速度的同时降低11.6%能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU内核优化依赖人工专家经验，自动化方法缺乏硬件反馈闭环和系统性瓶颈分析能力，且仅关注速度优化而忽略能效。

Method: 提出四方面贡献：(1)语义反馈算子，将专家启发式编码为可插拔微剖析工具，将原始硬件指标转化为可操作的自然语言指导；(2)两阶段工具调用架构，基于Roofline瓶颈分类过滤专用分析工具，融合内核级(ncu)、指令级(SASS)和系统级(nsys)性能分析；(3)领域自适应的MCTS，包含渐进式扩展、非对称分支、对数奖励校准、死胡同剪枝和跨迭代搜索记忆；(4)通过自主代码搜索CUTLASS/CuTe代码库实现直接CuTe源码级代码生成。

Result: 在KernelBench上，Level 1/2/3几何平均加速比分别为2.42x/4.69x/5.30x，全面达到最先进水平；在VeOmni专家优化的MoE训练内核上，生成从头编写的raw-CUDA+CuTe Hopper WGMMA内核，比手工调优的Triton快1.23x；消融实验表明各组件均显著提升优化质量；首次实现速度匹配下11.6%的能耗降低。

Conclusion: KernelPro证明了闭环多智能体架构结合领域自适应搜索策略能够超越人类专家优化水平，并首次将能效纳入自动GPU内核优化的目标维度，为高性能计算中的自动化代码生成开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We present KernelPro, a closed-loop multi-agent system that automatically generates, profiles, and iteratively optimizes GPU kernel code by integrating large language model (LLM) code generation with hardware profiler feedback and pluggable bottleneck detection tools. KernelPro introduces four contributions: (1) a semantic feedback operator that encodes expert heuristics as pluggable micro-profiling tools, transforming raw hardware metrics into actionable natural language guidance; (2) a two-stage tool invocation architecture where roofline-based bottleneck classification filters which specialized analysis tools execute, combining kernel-level (ncu), instruction-level (SASS), and system-level (nsys) profiling; (3) a domain-adapted MCTS with progressive widening, asymmetric branching, log-reward calibration, dead-end pruning, and search memory for cross-iteration learning; and (4) direct CuTe source-level code generation via autonomous code search over the CUTLASS/CuTe codebase. On KernelBench, KernelPro achieves geometric mean speedups of 2.42x/4.69x/5.30x on Levels 1/2/3, establishing state-of-the-art performance across all difficulty levels. On VeOmni's expert-optimized MoE training kernels, KernelPro achieves 1.23x over hand-tuned Triton by generating a from-scratch raw-CUDA+CuTe Hopper WGMMA kernel. Ablation studies demonstrate that each design component independently and significantly improves optimization quality: micro-profiling tools (p < 0.0001 vs raw metrics), MCTS search (26% higher geometric mean vs greedy, p = 0.004), and proactive tool orchestration (23% improvement, p = 0.035). Finally, KernelPro is the first CUDA kernel coding agent to optimize energy efficiency beyond the speed-only focus of prior systems, demonstrating an 11.6% measured energy reduction at matched speed.

</details>


### [238] [Finding the Time to Think: Learning Planning Budgets in Real-Time RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26463)
*Aneesh Muppidi,Firas Darwish,Dylan Cope,João F. Henriques,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变延迟实时强化学习框架，通过轻量级门控策略为规划器选择状态依赖的规划预算，在多个实时游戏中优于固定预算和启发式基线，并能迁移到环境与代理运行在不同GPU上的真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习假设环境无限期等待智能体决策，但在实时场景中，环境在智能体决策期间持续推进。已有形式化方法未考虑智能体自主选择延迟时间的能力，而简单规划"规划多久"会导致代理瘫痪，因此需要一种能根据状态自适应选择规划预算的方法。

Method: 在变延迟实时强化学习框架中，智能体在每个决策点自主选择规划时长。核心方法是训练一个轻量级门控策略（gating policy），该策略基于状态为底层规划器选择规划预算，从而避免直接规划"规划多久"带来的瘫痪问题。

Result: 在实时Pac-Man、Tetris、Snake、Speed Hex和Speed Go等游戏中，门控策略优于固定预算和启发式基线，并能成功迁移到环境与代理运行在不同GPU上的异构实时设置中。

Conclusion: 轻量级门控策略能有效自适应地选择状态依赖的规划预算，解决了实时RL中"规划如何规划"的元决策难题，且具有良好的跨平台迁移能力。

Abstract: Deliberating takes time. In real-time settings, that time is not free. Standard reinforcement learning (RL) sidesteps this as the environment waits indefinitely for the agent's decision. Instead, we study real-time RL environments where the environment progresses while waiting for the agent's action. Building on prior real-time formalizations, we introduce variable-delay real-time RL, where the agent chooses how long to deliberate at each decision point since the environment progresses. For the planning agents we use, the right delay is state-dependent, and naively planning how long to plan can paralyze the agent. We instead approach this setting by training a lightweight gating policy on top of a planner to select state-dependent planning budgets. Across real-time Pac-Man, Tetris, Snake, Speed Hex, and Speed Go, our gating policy outperforms fixed-budget and heuristic baselines, and transfers to a real-time setup where the environment and agent run on two different GPUs.

</details>


### [239] [A Causal Foundation Model for Structure and Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26467)
*Max Zhu,Martino Mansoldo,Ching-Hao Wang,Stefan Groha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-CFM是一个因果基础模型，能够处理多种因果问题，从观测数据预测因果结构和结果，支持Pearl因果层次的所有三个层次的查询，并在已知图结构时利用其改进预测。该模型在合成数据集上训练，能泛化到真实数据集，在结构和结果预测上都优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时处理因果结构发现和因果效应估计，且缺乏统一的、能泛化到多种因果问题的模型。作者希望开发一个能够处理Pearl因果层次所有三个层次（关联、干预、反事实）查询的统一因果基础模型。

Method: TabPFN-CFM采用基于Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFN)的架构，通过在大量合成因果数据集上训练来学习因果结构和结果的联合分布。模型能够灵活适应是否已知图结构的情况，并在推理时支持不同类型的因果查询。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，TabPFN-CFM在因果结构预测和结果预测方面均优于现有的基线方法，展示了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TabPFN-CFM作为一个统一的因果基础模型，成功整合了因果结构学习和因果效应估计，为处理Pearl因果层次中多个层次的因果问题提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce TabPFN-CFM, a causal foundation model that can handle multiple causal problems. TabPFN-CFM predicts both causal structure and outcomes from observational data, supports queries on all three levels of Pearl's Causal Hierarchy and uses known graph structure when available to improve predictions. TabPFN-CFM is trained on synthetic datasets, and generalises to real datasets, demonstrating improved performance over both structural and outcome prediction baselines.

</details>


### [240] [Epiphany-Aware KV Cache Eviction Without the Attention Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26472)
*Steven Kolawole,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EpiKV是一种基于"顿悟分数"（epiphany score）的KV缓存驱逐方法，通过读取模型前向传播中的内部表示变化来评分，无需注意力矩阵或自定义内核，在长推理链场景下比基于注意力权重的传统方法更高效，可支持16倍更长的上下文，在MATH-500和AIME-2024上达到或超越现有最强基线，速度提升达2.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型生成数万token的思维链时，KV缓存成为部署瓶颈。现有基于注意力权重的缓存驱逐方法在长推理痕迹中噪声大，且迫使模型物化注意力矩阵，无法在推理中使用融合内核。

Method: 提出"顿悟分数"（epiphany score），直接从前向传播中读取模型内部表示的变化来评分token重要性，无需注意力矩阵和额外状态；结合因果滚动z-score去除位置趋势；无需训练、分类器或自定义内核，可直接用于FlashAttention推理栈。

Result: 在4096-token缓存下，EpiKV在MATH-500上达72%（ThinKV 71%，H2O 67%）；滞后归一化变体在AIME-2024上8192 token下达37%（最优基线33%），速度提升达2.8倍，上下文长度扩展16倍。

Conclusion: EpiKV证明了在长推理链场景下，通过读取模型内部表示变化而非注意力权重来进行缓存驱逐更有效，且无需修改现有推理基础设施即可部署。

Abstract: As reasoning models emit chains of thought tens of thousands of tokens long, KV cache increasingly becomes a deployment bottleneck. Existing cache eviction methods rank tokens by attention weight, which is a noisy importance proxy in long reasoning traces, and prohibits the use of fused kernels in production inference by forcing the model to materialize the attention matrix. In this work, we instead score tokens with a metric we term the epiphany score: the change in the model's internal representation, read directly from the forward pass with no attention matrix and negligible extra state. Our resulting cache eviction method, EpiKV, requires no training, classifier, or custom kernel, and can be used directly in FlashAttention inference stacks unchanged -- scaling to a 16x longer feasible context than attention-based scoring. upper-mid layers negatively) and remove a positional trend with a causal rolling z-score. At a 4096-token cache EpiKV reaches 72% on MATH-500, matching the strongest attention-based baseline (ThinKV 71%, H2O 67%); a lag-normalized KV variant reaches 37% on AIME-2024 at 8192 tokens against the best of them (33%), at up to 2.8x the speed.

</details>


### [241] [When Does Quality-Aware Multimodal Fusion Matter? A Leakage-Safe Diagnostic for Decision-Level Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26473)
*Jaden Moon,Arvind Pillai,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断测试方法，通过置换可靠性分数来检验多模态融合模型是否真正利用模态可靠性信息进行决策。实验表明，在应力识别和情感分析任务中，置换可靠性分数不影响模型性能，说明模型并未有效利用这些信号；只有当可靠性信号能稳定预测单模态正确性时，融合规则才能获得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在多模态系统中估计各模态的可靠性并据此加权融合，但尚不清楚这些可靠性分数是否真正影响模型决策，抑或仅与性能存在相关性。

Method: 提出一种置换诊断测试：固定训练后的模型和输入，在测试样本间随机置换可靠性分数，若模型依赖这些分数进行决策，则性能应下降。同时在正对照实验中，使可靠性信号能准确识别正确模态，观察融合效果。

Result: 在StressID（压力识别）和CMU-MOSEI（情感分析）上，置换可靠性分数后性能不变；而在正对照中，相同的融合规则能带来显著提升。

Conclusion: 当前多模态系统中的可靠性分数并未真正影响模型决策，融合规则仅在可靠性信号能稳定预测单模态正确性时才能发挥作用。

Abstract: Many multimodal systems estimate the reliability of each modality and weight their contributions to the final prediction. However, it remains unclear whether these scores influence model decisions or merely correlate with performance. We propose a simple diagnostic to test whether reliability information is used during inference. After training, the model and inputs are fixed while reliability scores are permuted across test examples. If predictions depend on these scores, performance should degrade. Experiments on StressID for stress recognition and CMU-MOSEI for sentiment analysis show that permuting reliability scores leaves performance unchanged despite substantial potential gains from selecting the best modality per example. In positive controls where reliability signals identify the correct modality, the same frozen fusion rules yield significant improvements, indicating that reliability signals influence fused decisions only when they reliably predict unimodal correctness.

</details>


### [242] [Localizing RL-Induced Tool Use to a Single Crosscoder Feature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26474)
*Andrii Shportko,Shubham Bhokare,Ahmed Zeyad A Alzahrani,Bowen Cheng,Gustavo Mercier,Jessica Hullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dedicated Feature Crosscoders (DFC)方法，通过隔离强化学习引入的特定特征，在不重新训练的情况下实现工具调用能力的行为控制。在Qwen2.5-3B模型上，DFC将RL模型的工具正确率提升31.1个百分点，并将工具调用能力被动迁移给冻结的基础模型（能力溢出效应），实现了运行时行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习微调能重塑语言模型内部表征以实现智能体行为（如工具使用），但其机制基础尚不清晰。具体而言，RL微调后哪些特征涌现、哪些特征被保留，以及能否利用这些特征实现无需重训练的行为控制，仍是未解问题。

Method: 提出Dedicated Feature Crosscoders (DFC)方法，通过跨编码器超参数扫描（48次实验），在Qwen2.5-3B模型上将基础模型和RL微调模型的表征进行分区编码，隔离出一组紧凑的RL特定特征集。

Result: 编码-解码重建使RL模型的工具正确率提升31.1±9.7个百分点；被动地将工具调用能力迁移给冻结的基础模型，提升6.8±5.0个百分点（即"能力溢出"效应）。DFC分区将RL引入的能力浓缩到最小、可操控的特征集中。

Conclusion: DFC方法有效隔离了RL引入的特定特征，实现了对智能体LLM的运行时行为控制，且无需重新训练。

Abstract: Fine-tuning through RL reshapes the internal representations of language models to enable agentic behaviors such as tool use, yet the mechanistic basis of these changes remains poorly understood. While RL substantially improves structured tool-call generation, it is unclear which features emerge, which are preserved, and whether identified features can be leveraged for retraining-free behavioral control. In this work, we show that $\textit{Dedicated Feature Crosscoders (DFC)}$ isolate a compact set of RL-specific features that mediate tool-calling capability in $\texttt{Qwen2.5-3B}$. Across a $48$-crosscoder hyperparameter sweep, encode-decode reconstruction improves the RL model's tool correctness by $+31.1 \pm {9.7}$ pp and passively transfers tool-calling ability to the frozen base model by $+6.8 \pm 5.0$ pp which we call a $\textit{capability spillover}$. Our findings show that DFC partitioning concentrates RL-introduced capability into a minimal, steerable feature set that enables runtime behavioral control of agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [243] [Retrieval-Warmed Energy-Based Reasoning: A Five-Arm Ablation Methodology for Diffusion-as-Inference on Structured Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26476)
*Libo Sun,Po-Wei Harn,Zewei Zhang,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出五臂消融方法（oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned）来解耦检索暖启动扩散采样器中三类混杂效应（类别先验偏置偏移、随机暖启动、图对齐值复用），并在连通性-2和数独任务上定位出首个阻塞组件：前者是图对齐破坏导致性能下降35个百分点，后者是键质量在当前设置下为瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暖启动扩散采样器加速了迭代推理，但增益来源不明确——是类别先验偏置偏移、随机暖启动还是图对齐值复用？需要一种诊断方法分离这些混杂效应。

Method: 提出五臂消融方法论（oracle、best-constant、per-query-random、shuffled、aligned），改编自LLM-RAG评估中的诊断分解，对RW-EBR（基于IRED能量扩散模型+Modern Hopfield轨迹记忆）进行组件级因果归因。

Result: 在连通性-2任务上，aligned与shuffled-oracle之间的摇摆达到+35个百分点的平衡准确率，破坏图对齐而非偏置偏移或随机性是主导因素；在数独任务上，诊断为键质量是当前设置下的首个阻塞组件。

Conclusion: 五臂消融能定位扩散采样器中暖启动增益的真正来源，不同任务的首个阻塞组件不同（连通性-2为图对齐，数独为键质量），该框架可用于结构化及时空推理中故障模式的可解释分析。

Abstract: Warm-started diffusion samplers accelerate iterative inference, but it is rarely clear which part of the pipeline carries the gain. We study \textbf{retrieval-warmed energy-based reasoning (RW-EBR)} -- an IRED energy-based diffusion model \cite{du2024ired} augmented with a Modern Hopfield trajectory memory -- and contribute a \textbf{five-arm ablation methodology} (oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned) that separates three confounded effects: class-prior bias shift, stochastic warm-starting, and graph-aligned value reuse. The diagnostic decomposition is adapted from LLM-RAG evaluation \cite{ru2024ragchecker}. On \textbf{connectivity-2} (Erdős--Rényi all-pairs reachability), the aligned-vs-shuffled-oracle swing reaches \textbf{$+35$\,pp} balanced accuracy on a fixed 1{,}000-graph validation-set diagnostic, with value distribution and retrieval mechanics fixed, only per-graph alignment destroyed, while per-query random initialisation falls below cold -- per-graph alignment, not bias shift or stochasticity, dominates. Yet the \emph{deployable} cold-prediction pipeline misses the acceptance gate at stored-value quality. The same diagnostic logic, stopped at the key-quality screen, applied to \textbf{Sudoku} with a task-specific key encoder produces a clean negative at a \emph{different} component -- key quality, under the current setup. The decomposition names the first blocking component on each task. The setting -- graph reachability refined by an iterative diffusion sampler, with explainability of failure modes as the lens -- places the work within structured and spatio-temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [244] [What Survives When You Compress a Recursive Reasoner for the Edge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26488)
*Pearse Jim,Steven Kolawole,Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Glory Bagai,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了递归推理模型在极端压缩下的行为，发现激进压缩会破坏全局推理能力（即使局部预测保持准确），并提出了解决方案：每通道校准的INT4量化可恢复性能，且引入“进位轨迹保真度”作为无需标签的损伤预测信号。最终提供了边缘部署方案：闪存流嵌入、INT8匹配全精度性能、校准INT4适配4MB微控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归推理模型在边缘硬件部署时面临显著压缩需求，但不同于传统序列模型，量化误差会在递归推理循环中累积而非在输出token间累积，导致标准压缩直觉失效。需要理解压缩究竟破坏了什么，以及如何修复。

Method: 在全面精度扫描下，对三个任务、两种递归架构进行实验，测试朴素INT4剪枝、蒸馏、线性注意力等压缩方法；引入每通道校准INT4量化方案；提出进位轨迹保真度（carry-trajectory fidelity）作为无标签信号预测压缩损伤。

Result: 激进压缩保持局部预测（细胞准确率）但破坏全局推理（拼图准确率归零）；token级目标（包括量化感知训练）无法修复；崩坏是架构性的（影响MLP混合递归而非注意力机制）；每通道校准INT4无需重训即可逆转崩溃；进位轨迹保真度可在任务评估前预测损伤与恢复。

Conclusion: 提供了明确的部署方案：闪存流嵌入消除99.4MB瓶颈，INT8以6倍更少FLOPs匹配全深度准确率（8MB SoC），校准INT4适配4MB微控制器。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models can solve complex structured tasks with only a few million parameters by repeatedly updating a latent state. Deploying these models on edge hardware requires significant compression, but unlike conventional sequence models, quantization errors compound across recursive reasoning cycles rather than across output tokens. As a result, standard intuitions about compression fail to apply. In this work, we ask what survives when recursive reasoners are compressed. Across a full precision sweep, three tasks, and two recursive architectures, we find that aggressive compression preserves local prediction but destroys global reasoning: cell accuracy holds while puzzle-exact accuracy collapses to zero under naive INT4 pruning, distillation, and linear attention alike. Token-level objectives, including quantization-aware training, cannot repair it. The collapse is architectural -- it strikes MLP-mixing recursion but not attention on the same task -- and we reverse it with per-channel calibrated INT4 without retraining. We also introduce carry-trajectory fidelity, the cosine similarity to the full-precision reasoning path, as a label-free signal that predicts this damage and its recovery before a task evaluation. The combined result is a deployment recipe: flash-streamed embeddings remove a 99.4MB bottleneck, INT8 at one cycle matches full-depth accuracy at 6x fewer FLOPs (8MB SoC), and calibrated INT4 fits a 4MB microcontroller.

</details>


### [245] [Multipath Adaptive Gated Bottleneck Latent ODE with Raman Data Fusion for Cell Culture Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26520)
*Johnny Peng,Thanh Tung Khuat,Ellen Otte,Katarzyna Musial,Bogdan Gabrys*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合GB-Latent ODE与MP-JIT-FT的自适应框架，用于哺乳动物细胞培养过程的早期多日预测，通过融合拉曼光谱数据，在38批5L生物反应器运行中优于全局Latent ODE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物细胞培养过程中关键参数随时间漂移，异常趋势往往在无法干预时才被确认，需要早期多日预测来及时调整补料、采样和控制策略。

Method: 提出GB-Latent ODE（门控瓶颈潜常微分方程），通过可学习的变量级门控和掩码感知瓶颈压缩高维稀疏输入；结合MP-JIT-FT（多路径即时微调），从相似历史轨迹聚类多个候选工况并为每个工况微调独立模型生成多条可能路径；融合拉曼光谱数据通过软传感器生成伪观测值丰富稀疏的离线测量。

Result: 在38批涵盖14种条件的5L生物反应器运行中，MP-JIT-FT结合拉曼融合在9个目标变量中8个优于全局Latent ODE基线，取得最佳平均排名；局部发散度量表明多路径优势在局部相似前缀发散时最大，拉曼融合在早期动态能代表后期行为时最有效。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应框架有效解决了生物过程预测中测量稀疏、条件异质和轨迹发散等挑战，为细胞培养过程的早期干预提供了可行的预测手段。

Abstract: Mammalian cell-culture processes underpin the manufacture of many biopharmaceuticals, yet keeping a run on track is hard: critical process parameters drift over days, and an off-specification trend is often confirmed too late to intervene. Early-stage, multi-day forecasts could enable timely adjustment of feeding, sampling, and control, but bioprocess forecasting is challenging because measurements are sparse and irregularly sampled, operating conditions are heterogeneous across cell lines and media, and runs with near-identical early behaviour can diverge into different futures. We propose an adaptive framework combining a Gated Bottleneck Latent Ordinary Differential Equation (GB-Latent ODE) with Multi-Path Just-In-Time Fine Tuning (MP-JIT-FT). The GB-Latent ODE augments the stan dard Latent ODE with learnable variable-wise gating and a mask-aware bottleneck that compress high-dimensional sparse inputs, improving learning under limited data. Given a partially observed run, MP-JIT-FT retrieves similar historical trajectories, clusters the local neighbourhood into candidate regimes, and fine-tunes a separate model per regime to produce multiple plausible paths, each with a reconstruction-based confidence score, not a single averaged forecast. We further fuse Raman spectroscopy data: a machine-learning soft sensor turns dense Raman spectra into pseudo-observations that enrich the sparse offline measurements for more robust training. On 38 fed-batch 5L bioreactor runs spanning 14 conditions, MP-JIT-FT with Raman fusion achieves the best average rank and outperforms a global Latent ODE baseline on 8 of 9 target variables. Using local-divergence metrics, we show the multi-path gains are largest when locally similar prefixes diverge, whereas Raman fusion helps most when early dynamics are representative of later behaviour.

</details>


### [246] [Theory-Scale Auto-Formalization of Logics for Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26525)
*Yuming Feng,Frederick Pu,One An,Osbert Bastani,Li Zhang,Jiani Huang,Xujie Si,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LCS-Bench，一个基于《计算机科学逻辑》教材的理论级自动形式化基准，覆盖327个教材条目、4,076个Lean声明和85K+行Lean代码，通过半自动智能体流水线构建，并引入定义等价性检查器实现细粒度评估。对14个模型的评估表明SOTA模型仅达到20.1%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化工作主要聚焦于孤立语句，但理论级自动形式化（需连贯翻译数百个相互依赖的定义、引理和定理）因一致性、忠实性、可扩展性和正确性方面的挑战而尚未解决。

Method: 提出LCS-Bench基准，通过新型半自动智能体流水线构建，利用概念图、形式化签名规划、问题追踪、基于反例搜索的sorry填充，辅以人类专家的忠实性审查。通过数据引擎自动派生出五个评估轨道的基准，并引入定义等价性检查器作为评估协议。

Result: LCS-Bench包含327个教材条目、4,076个Lean声明和超过85,000行Lean代码。对14个模型的评估显示，SOTA模型在自动形式化任务上仅达到20.1%的准确率。

Conclusion: LCS-Bench具有高质量、一致性和忠实性；该基准具有挑战性；分析揭示了理论级自动形式化的关键发现，并为未来研究指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Auto-formalization is critical for scalable formal verification, but existing progress largely focuses on isolated statements, while theory-scale auto-formalization, which coherently translates hundreds of interdependent definitions, lemmas, and theorems, remains open due to challenges in consistency, faithfulness, scalability, and correctness. In this paper, we introduce LCS-Bench, a stand-alone, theory-scale benchmark based on Logics for Computer Science. LCS-Bench is built through a novel semi-automated agentic pipeline that leverages concept graphs, formal signature planning, issue tracking, sorry-filling with counter-example search, complemented by faithfulness review from human experts. The resulting artifact covers 327 textbook items, over 4,076 Lean declarations, and more than 85K lines of Lean code. The dataset supports broad evaluation through a data engine that automatically derives five tracks of evaluation benchmarks, measuring different aspects of auto-formalization and theorem-proving capabilities. We also introduce a novel evaluation protocol featuring definitional equivalence checkers, enabling more fine-grained and faithful assessment. Through extensive evaluation on 14 models, we demonstrate that (1) LCS-Bench is of high quality, consistent, and faithful; (2) the benchmark is challenging, with state-of-the-art models achieving only 20.1% on auto-formalization tasks; and (3) our analysis reveals key findings regarding theory-scale auto-formalization and suggests promising directions for future work.

</details>


### [247] [Sample-efficient Transfer Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Reward Shaping and Policy-Ratio Reweighting Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26527)
*Wenjie Huang,Yang Li,Jingjia Teng,Mingwei Jin,Kai Song,Yougang Bian,Yongfu Li,Qisong Yang,Helai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种安全迁移强化学习框架，通过自适应教师干预机制、教师引导的安全迁移模块和加权优化机制，解决了自动驾驶高速公路变道决策中迁移不匹配和训练安全问题，在安全性和学习效率上分别提升52.2%和5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习方法在源域与目标域之间存在分布偏移导致迁移不匹配，引发训练震荡和性能下降；同时目标域适应依赖探索性交互，在安全关键的变道场景中难以保证训练安全。

Method: 1) 基于瞬时安全成本的自适应教师干预机制，约束危险探索并渐进削弱干预强度，附带混合行为策略的回报界理论分析；2) 教师引导的安全迁移模块，通过奖励塑形将教师策略的动作评估信息嵌入学生学习，随策略安全性提升衰减教师引导；3) 教师引导的加权优化机制，利用似然比因子调整策略优化中的样本权重以稳定迁移性能。

Result: 在不同交通密度实验和真实NGSIM数据集验证下，该方法在安全性上超越基线方法52.2%以上，学习效率提升5.0%以上，验证了安全感知迁移策略的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全迁移强化学习框架能有效缓解迁移不匹配问题，在保证训练安全的同时提升学习效率，为自动驾驶高速公路变道决策提供了可行且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Transfer learning improves policy learning efficiency by reusing knowledge from source tasks, providing a feasible paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous highway lane changing decision-making. Existing methods frequently encounter transfer mismatch induced by distribution shifts between source and target domains, leading to training oscillation and performance decline. Besides, target domain adaptation depends on exploratory interactions, which struggles to guarantee training safety in safety-critical lane changing cases. To tackle these limitations, this paper proposes a safe transfer reinforcement learning framework for autonomous highway lane changing. First, we design an adaptive teacher intervention mechanism based on instantaneous safety cost to restrain risky exploration and fade intervention strength progressively, with theoretical analysis on return bounds for mixed behavior policy. This intervention also produces dual-source samples for joint training. Second, a teacher-guided safe transfer module embeds action evaluation information of teacher policy into student learning via reward shaping to boost training safety and efficiency, with teacher guidance decaying as policy safety rises. Third, a teacher-guided weighted optimization mechanism adjusts sample weights in policy optimization using a likelihood ratio factor to stabilize transfer performance. Experiments under varied traffic densities and validations on real-world NGSIM dataset reveal that our method surpasses baseline approaches by over 52.2% in safety and 5.0% in learning efficiency. Results verify the efficacy and robustness of our safety-aware transfer strategy for autonomous highway lane changing under various traffic conditions.

</details>


### [248] [CascadeFormer: Depth-Tapered Transformers Motivated by Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26538)
*Huzama Ahmad,Cao Viet Hai Nam,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种利用深度Transformer中深层网络贡献度不对称性的效率优化方法：CascadeFormer通过层宽递减匹配信息流，CascadeFlow Pruning利用累积训练梯度剪枝层，并提出梯度扇入不对称性（GFA）理论解释深层贡献不足的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准深度Transformer中，均匀堆叠的残差块导致深层网络贡献度低，存在计算效率浪费。作者旨在利用这种不对称性，提出高效架构方法和剪枝策略，并从梯度传播机制角度给出理论解释。

Method: 1) CascadeFormer：随深度递减层宽度（tapered width），匹配非均匀信息流。2) CascadeFlow Pruning：利用训练过程中累积的梯度信息直接剪枝层，无需事后分析。3) 提出GFA理论：在Pre-LayerNorm残差堆叠中，梯度由恒等路径和所有下游功能路径求和构成，导致梯度扇入随深度线性衰减（深度监督下为二次衰减），早期层梯度更丰富。

Result: CascadeFormer在相同训练预算下达到与均匀基线相当的困惑度，延迟降低8.6%，吞吐量增加9.4%。CascadeFlow Pruning在困惑度和秩稳定性（rank-stability）上优于标准启发式方法，下游准确率竞争力相当。GFA理论在1.2B参数模型上获得相关性和干预性证据支持。干预实验表明：平衡各层梯度范数无法恢复深层价值，而通过参数共享重复增加下游路径数量可恢复并提升深层贡献。

Conclusion: 深层网络贡献不足的根本瓶颈在于梯度结构（扇入衰减）而非梯度幅度；GFA为理解深度网络中的层贡献不对称性提供了理论框架。但在高秩区域以外梯度幅度能否代理扇入、以及100B+规模下的动力学行为仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Deep Transformers are composed of uniformly stacked residual blocks, yet their deepest layers often add little value. We present two efficiency methods that exploit this asymmetry. CascadeFormer tapers width with depth to match the uneven information flow across layers, achieving comparable perplexity to a uniform baseline at the same training budget while reducing latency by 8.6% and increasing throughput by 9.4%. CascadeFlow Pruning removes layers using accumulated training gradients, with no post hoc analysis. It outperforms standard heuristics on perplexity and rank-stability and stays competitive on downstream accuracy. To motivate these methods, we propose Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry (GFA) as a structural account of why deeper layers contribute less. In Pre-LayerNorm residual stacks, the gradient at a layer is the sum of an identity path and all downstream functional paths, producing a gradient fan-in that decays linearly with depth (and quadratically under deep supervision), yielding richer gradients for early layers and sparser ones for later layers. We provide correlational and interventional evidence for GFA on models trained from scratch up to 1.2B parameters. Across Transformers and ResNets, accumulated training gradients follow the theoretical fan-in and are associated with post hoc layer importance. Two interventions point to structure rather than magnitude as the bottleneck: equalizing per-layer gradient norms does not restore late-layer value, while increasing downstream path counts via parameter-shared repetition restores and elevates it. Whether gradient magnitude proxies fan-in beyond high-rank regimes, and how these dynamics behave at the 100B+ scale, remain open questions.

</details>


### [249] [From Weights to Features: SAE-Guided Activation Regularization for LLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26629)
*Evan Ning,Wei Xue,Dong Lou,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）的激活空间正则化方法，以解决EWC等权重空间正则化方法在大语言模型持续学习中因权重多语义性导致的性能不佳问题。该方法利用SAE作为单语义特征字典，通过约束优化推导新损失函数，在激活空间中平衡稳定性与可塑性，无需存储先前任务数据，且在TRACE和MedCL基准上超越EWC等传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间正则化方法（如EWC）在大语言模型中表现不佳，作者认为其原因是权重层面的多语义性（polysemanticity）导致每个权重的重要性估计过于粗糙，无法精确隔离需要保护的知识。因此需要更细粒度的特征空间正则化方法。

Method: 1) 使用预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）构建单语义特征字典，将模型激活映射到稀疏特征空间；2) 从约束优化角度推导新损失函数，在SAE特征空间施加正则化以显式平衡稳定性与可塑性；3) 在当前任务数据上计算紧凑的SAE特征掩码，仅保留该掩码用于后续训练，无需存储先前任务数据；4) 证明EWC是该方法的特例（单侧权重空间惩罚设置）。

Result: 在TRACE和MedCL持续学习基准上，该方法在不引入任务特定架构组件的情况下取得最优结果，尤其是超越EWC等传统权重空间正则化方法。同时提供经验证据：任务相关表示在SAE特征基上线性可分，而在权重基上无法区分，验证了多语义性假说。

Conclusion: 激活空间正则化比权重空间正则化更适合大语言模型的持续学习，SAE特征字典能够提供更精细的知识隔离能力，且具有更高的内存效率。权重空间的多语义性是EWC等方法在大语言模型中失效的重要原因。

Abstract: Weight-space regularization methods such as Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) are the standard approach to catastrophic forgetting in continual learning. However, those methods tend to underperform when applied to large language models. We argue that such underperformance can be partly explained by the ``polysemantic'' nature of large language models: per-weight importance estimates utilized by EWC-style regularization are too coarse and cannot isolate the knowledge that needs protection. In this paper, we propose regularizing instead in the model's activation space, using pretrained Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a monosemantic feature dictionary. From the perspective of constrained optimization, we derive a new loss function that uses the SAE feature dictionary to explicitly balance stability and plasticity, and show that EWC is a special case in the one-sided weight-space penalty setting. Unlike replay-based methods that store or revisit examples from earlier tasks, our method requires no previous-task data after mask construction: current-task data is used to compute a compact SAE feature mask, and only this mask is retained for later training. Further, since the feature space has significantly lower dimensionality than the parameter space, the proposed method is more memory efficient. On the TRACE and MedCL continual learning benchmarks, the method achieves the strongest result among approaches without introducing task-specific architectural components, also surpassing traditional weight-space regularization methods like EWC. Beyond performance comparisons, we provide empirical evidence for the polysemanticity thesis: task-relevant representations are linearly separable in the SAE feature basis but indistinguishable from chance in the weight basis, and weight-space protection is nearly non-selective at the concept level.

</details>


### [250] [Target-Aware Bandit Allocation for Scalable Surrogate Optimization in Chemical Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26657)
*Mohammad Haddadnia,Yuvan Chali,Abhilash Jayaraj,Constance Kraay,Joana Reis,Felix Strieth-Kalthoff,Haribabu Arthanari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BOBa是一种基于多臂老虎机的代理优化框架，通过自适应地将计算分配给动作空间的分区，消除全库推理，在结构药物发现等大规模离散空间中实现高效的高价值候选筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分子库（数十亿至万亿化合物）使得全库代理推理本身成为计算瓶颈，亟需一种能自适应分配计算资源、避免全库推理的优化方法。

Method: 将动作空间划分为多个分区，并将每个分区视为多臂老虎机中的一个臂；通过乐观不确定性驱动的探索策略，自适应地将推理和评估计算集中于经验上最有前景的分区。

Result: 在真实合成按需库上的实验表明，乐观不确定性老虎机结合有意义的分区策略能有效分配推理和评估；揭示了筛选性能与代理推理成本之间的可调权衡。

Conclusion: BOBa支持在现有库上的实用优化，并为超大规模库虚拟筛选建立了可行路径。

Abstract: Identifying high-utility candidates from massive discrete spaces under expensive evaluations is a recurring challenge across the sciences, with structure-based drug discovery as a prominent example. While surrogate-based optimization can increase sample efficiency by reducing the number of expensive evaluations, modern molecular libraries have reached billions to trillions of compounds, making full-library surrogate inference itself a major computational bottleneck. We introduce BOBa, a bandit-guided surrogate optimization framework that eliminates full-library inference by adaptively allocating computation across partitions of the action space. By treating partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit, BOBa concentrates inference and evaluations on empirically promising partitions while maintaining principled exploration. Experiments on real-world synthesis-on-demand libraries demonstrate that optimism-under-uncertainty bandits, combined with meaningful action space partitioning, are essential for effective allocation of inference and evaluations. Our findings reveal a tunable tradeoff between screening performance and surrogate inference cost, which supports practical optimization over current libraries, and establishes a viable route to ultra-large library virtual screening.

</details>


### [251] [Zero-Shot Size Transfer for Neural ODEs on Sparse Random Graphs: Graphon Limits and Adjoint Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26662)
*Mingsong Yan,Zhida Wang,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为图神经微分方程（GNDE）在稀疏随机图上的零样本尺寸迁移建立了定量理论：证明了GNDE解收敛到其无限节点极限（图神经微分方程，Graphon-NDE）的速率为O((α_n n)^{-1/2})，建立了伴随系统的一致收敛界，并证明了离散化-优化与优化-离散化训练策略的渐近一致性，实验验证了理论速率和零样本迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNDE利用GNN参数化神经ODE速度场，具有局部、尺寸无关的滤波器特性，暗示了零样本尺寸迁移原则（即在小图上训练后可部署到更大的相似图上），但缺乏严格的定量理论支持。本文旨在为这一原则在稀疏随机图（从图论中采样）上建立严格的数学基础。

Method: 将Graphon-NDE及其伴随方程作为GNDE前向和伴随系统的无限节点极限，并证明其适定性；对于从图论采样的n节点稀疏随机图（稀疏参数α_n），利用概率方法证明GNDE解到Graphon-NDE解的轨迹收敛率；分析显式欧拉离散化下的DTO和OTD训练策略，推导隐藏状态和参数梯度的误差界。

Result: 1) GNDE解以O((α_n n)^{-1/2})速率（忽略对数因子）高概率收敛到Graphon-NDE解；2) 伴随系统的隐藏状态和参数梯度达到一致的时间均匀收敛界；3) DTO与OTD渐近一致，隐藏状态和局部参数梯度差异分别为O(1/M)和O(1/M^2)；4) HSBM和Tent图论的实验验证了理论速率，四个图论类上的零样本迁移实验证实了所学GNDE可在更大独立采样图上准确部署。

Conclusion: 本文为GNDE的零样本尺寸迁移原则提供了严格的定量理论基础，揭示了收敛速率与图稀疏性和节点数的关系，并为DTO和OTD训练策略的渐近行为建立了理论保证，实验充分支持了理论发现。

Abstract: Graph Neural Differential Equations (GNDEs) model continuous-time graph dynamics by parameterizing Neural ODE velocity fields with Graph Neural Networks. Their local, size-independent filters suggest a zero-shot size-transfer principle: train on a small graph and deploy on larger, similar graphs without retraining. We develop a quantitative theory for this principle on sparse random graphs sampled from graphons. We consider Graphon Neural Differential Equations (Graphon-NDEs) and adjoint Graphon-NDEs as the infinite-node limits of the forward and adjoint GNDE systems, and establish well-posedness. For an $n$-node random graph with sparsity parameter $α_n$, we prove trajectory-wise convergence of GNDE solutions to Graphon-NDE solutions at rate $O((α_n n)^{-1/2})$, up to logarithmic factors, with high probability. We also establish uniform-in-time convergence bounds for adjoint systems governing hidden-state and parameter gradients. We further study discretize-then-optimize (DTO) and optimize-then-discretize (OTD) training. Under explicit Euler discretization with $M$ steps, we show that DTO and OTD are asymptotically consistent, with hidden-state and local parameter-gradient discrepancies of orders $O(1/M)$ and $O(1/M^2)$, respectively, up to sparsity and logarithmic factors. Experiments on HSBM and tent graphons support the theoretical rates, while zero-shot transfer experiments across four graphon classes demonstrate accurate deployment of learned GNDEs on larger independently sampled graphs.

</details>


### [252] [PersistentKV: Page-Aware Decode Scheduling for Long-Context LLM Serving on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26666)
*Muhammad Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PersistentKV，一种基于原生块表的解码注意力引擎和页面感知调度策略，通过按KV-head分组映射工作、重用K/V tile、紧凑工作队列调度等设计，在混合序列长度和低活跃长上下文解码场景下，结合自适应策略选择FlashInfer或PersistentKV，实现1.063-1.399x的吞吐提升，表明工作分配而非注意力算子是服务系统的关键变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大模型服务中，KV缓存移动逐渐成为比稠密矩阵乘法更严重的瓶颈。现代分页注意力系统（如FlashInfer）虽提供了优化的原生分页解码注意力内核，但单一内核实现并非总是最优的服务调度方案：低活跃长上下文解码会低效利用GPU，混合序列长度则需要在精确长度启动和粗粒度填充批次之间权衡。

Method: 设计PersistentKV引擎：(1) 按KV-head分组映射工作，重用K/V tile以跨分组查询头；(2) 支持原生页表；(3) 提出紧凑工作队列调度，仅执行非空的行-KV头-序列分割任务。并设计自适应策略，在小活跃批次用FlashInfer，B1长上下文用PersistentKV序列分割，B8长上下文用PersistentKV工作队列调度。

Result: 在RTX 3060上（FP16, page size 16, Hq=32, Hkv=8, d=128），在保持与FlashInfer相同正确性下：B8双峰、均匀和Zipf-like工作负载吞吐提升1.063-1.265x，B1分桶trace提升1.399x；B4双峰边界情况下自适应策略通过选择FlashInfer避免了PersistentKV的回归。

Conclusion: 研究识别了自适应页面感知解码调度的具体系统应用场景，并证明工作分配（而非注意力数学）是服务系统中决定性的变量。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model (LLM) serving is increasingly limited by key-value (KV) cache movement rather than dense matrix multiplication. Modern paged-attention systems reduce KV-cache fragmentation and mature kernels such as FlashInfer provide highly optimized native-paged decode attention. However, the best single-kernel implementation is not always the best serving schedule: low-active long-context decode can under-utilize commodity GPUs, while mixed sequence lengths introduce a tension between many exact-length launches and coarse padded batches. We present PersistentKV, a native block-table decode attention engine and page-aware scheduling study for grouped-query attention (GQA). PersistentKV maps work by KV-head group, is designed to reuse K,V tiles across grouped query heads, supports native page tables, and adds a compact workqueue schedule that executes only non-empty row-KV-head-sequence-split tasks. On an RTX 3060 with FP16, page size 16, Hq=32, Hkv=8, d=128, and identical correctness tolerance against FlashInfer, a calibrated adaptive policy selects FlashInfer for small active batches, PersistentKV sequence splitting for B1 long-context steps, and PersistentKV workqueue scheduling for B8 long-context steps. With thresholds and split counts fixed on calibration traces, one held-out trace seed improves synchronized wall throughput by 1.063-1.265x on B8 bimodal, uniform, and Zipf-like workloads and by 1.399x on a B1 bucketed trace. On the B4 bimodal boundary case, the policy avoids the PersistentKV regression by selecting FlashInfer. These results identify a concrete systems niche for adaptive page-aware decode scheduling and show that work assignment, not only attention math, is a decisive serving-system variable.

</details>


### [253] [Algorithmic Foundations of Deep Learning: Complexity-Theoretic Rates and a Characterization of Universal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26705)
*Anastasis Kratsios,Simone Brugiapaglia,Bum Jun Kim,Gregory Cousins,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经网络不仅应被视为灵活基函数，还应被视为计算模型。如果函数可通过实数电路在预设基本门语言上计算，则可用具有显式深度、宽度和参数量约束的NN以可比精度计算。NN复杂度由正则性和算法复杂度共同决定。满足自然并行化条件的可定义NN模型是通用逼近器当且仅当包含非仿射非线性。理论应用于连续函数逼近、Besov类极小极大最优逼近、全纯函数对数误差复杂度，以及NN可模拟牛顿-拉夫森求根、幂迭代等数值算法。在k顶点图最短路径问题上，编译热带动态规划电路得到仅需O(log(1/ε))非零参数的NN，相比通用Lipschitz逼近尺度O(ε^{-c k^2})实现了指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点通过正则性（regularity）刻画NN表达能力，无法区分正则性相似但直观复杂度不同的对象（如平方根函数与布朗路径）。作者希望从算法复杂度（algorithmic complexity）角度理解NN表达能力，弥补仅依赖基展开视角的不足。

Method: 将NN视为实数电路计算模型：若函数可由预设基本门语言上的实数电路计算，则存在NN以显式的深度、宽度和非零参数界实现同等精度逼近。提出可定义NN模型及自然并行化条件，证明其通用逼近性等价于含非仿射非线性。

Result: 证明了连续函数通用逼近、Besov类极小极大最优逼近、全纯函数对数误差复杂度；NN可模拟牛顿-拉夫森、幂迭代等算法；图最短路径问题上，NN非零参数仅O(log(1/ε))，远优于通用Lipschitz逼近的O(ε^{-c k^2})。

Conclusion: NN表达能力应由正则性和算法复杂度共同刻画，为理解NN在各类问题上的高效性提供了理论统一框架。

Abstract: Feedforward neural network (NN) expressivity is typically studied by emulating optimal basis-expansion schemes. While powerful, this perspective is incomplete: it primarily captures complexity through regularity, and therefore does not distinguish intuitively simple and complicated objects with comparable regularity, such as the square-root function and a typical Brownian path.
  The guiding message is that neural networks should be viewed not only as flexible basis functions, but also as models of computation. If a function is computable by a real-valued circuit over a prescribed elementary gate language, then it can be computed to comparable accuracy by an NN with explicit depth, width, and non-zero-parameter bounds controlled by the depth, width, gate count, and gate structure. Thus, neural-network complexity is not governed by regularity alone, but also by algorithmic complexity. We then show that any definable NN model satisfying a natural parallelization condition, allowing possibly multivariate non-linearities such as attention or layer normalization, is a universal approximator if and only if it contains a non-affine nonlinearity.
  The scope of our theory is illustrated by deducing universal approximation guarantees for continuous functions, minimax-optimal approximation guarantees for Besov classes, logarithmic-error complexity for holomorphic functions, and by showing that NNs can emulate numerical algorithms such as Newton-Raphson root finding and power iteration without architecture-specific arguments. Its precision is illustrated by shortest-path computation on $k$-vertex graphs: compiling the tropical dynamic-programming circuit yields NNs with O(log(1/ε)) non-zero parameters, exponentially improving in 1/ε over the generic $O(ε^{-c k^2})$ Lipschitz-approximation scale, for a constant c>0.

</details>


### [254] [HyperDFlash: MHC-Aligned Block Speculative Decoding with Gated Residual Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26744)
*Luxi Lin,Shuang Peng,Rui Ma,Junhao Hua,Shuwei Fan,Zhengda Qin,Qiang Wang,Hongjian Sun,Fangmin Chen,Songwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperDFlash是一种针对DeepSeek-V4多超连接(MHC)架构设计的块并行推测解码框架，通过预坍缩残差状态、门控残差缩减器和针对性KL蒸馏损失，解决了MHC架构下特征对齐问题，在数学推理、代码生成和对话任务中显著提升了平均接受长度和解码加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek-V4的MTP模块在后续位置草稿精度急剧下降，误差累积损害接受率；原有的DFlash方法无法无缝适配MHC架构，因为多路径残差流导致特征对齐问题。

Method: 1) 采用预坍缩残差状态作为唯一条件信号，保留多路径结构信息并实现对齐；2) 用轻量级门控残差缩减器替代通用线性压缩器，参数继承自超连接头，实现输入感知路径聚合且参数量减少三个数量级；3) 在LM-head上应用针对性KL蒸馏损失，对完整目标概率分布进行正则化。

Result: 在数学推理、代码合成和对话基准测试中，HyperDFlash在平均接受草稿长度和解码加速比上持续优于原生MTP基线和DFlash适配方案。

Conclusion: MHC对齐、门控缩减和针对性蒸馏对于高性能推测解码是有效的，验证了HyperDFlash框架的优越性。

Abstract: We present HyperDFlash, a block-parallel speculative decoding framework tailored to the novel multi-hyper-connection (MHC) architecture proposed by DeepSeek-V4. Despite the strong initial-token drafting performance of the native Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) module in DeepSeek-V4, its draft accuracy degrades sharply at later positions, as error accumulation from unverified intermediate tokens harms acceptance rates. Although the original DFlash method supports efficient one-pass block drafting, it cannot be seamlessly adapted to the MHC paradigm, since the multi-path residual stream of DeepSeek-V4 induces feature misalignment with conventional drafting designs. To resolve this mismatch, we propose two model-aligned optimizations for MHC residual streams. First, we adopt pre-collapse residual states as the exclusive conditioning signal, preserving multi-path structural information and aligning the drafter with the native prediction pathway of the target model. Second, we replace the heavy generic linear compressor with a lightweight gated residual reducer, whose parameters are inherited from the built-in hyper-connection head. This design yields input-aware path aggregation with three orders of magnitude fewer parameters while maintaining architectural alignment.
